\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\left(\frac{1}{\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}-\frac{1}{\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}\right)(n_{j,g}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{2}}{\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}(n_{j,g}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]

\displaystyle-\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})}{\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}(n_{j,g}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})].

(S83)

Under Assumption
9
, the first term on the RHS of (
S83
) can be bounded as follows:

\left|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{2}}{\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}(n_{j,g}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]\right|\leq\frac{K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{2}}{\min_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}(|\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}|\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g}))}.

Thus, by Lemma
S1
(ii, v), it follows that

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{2}}{\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}(n_{j,g}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]=O_{p}(G_{j}T^{-1}).

(S84)

Combining this result with (
S80
), (
S82
) and (
S83
), we obtain:

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\left(\frac{\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t};\phi_{j,g}^{\ast})}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}-\frac{\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t};\phi_{j,g}^{\ast})}{n_{j,g}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}(\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}-\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g}))+O_{p}(G_{j}T^{-1}).

(S85)

We now take care of the first term on the RHS of (
S85
).
Under Assumptions
1
,
8
and
9
, we have the following:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}(\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}-\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}))\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\left(\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[(\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}-\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}))^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\mathbb{E}\left[(\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}-\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}))^{2}\right]\leq KG_{j}T^{-1}.

Thus, by Markov’s inequality, it follows that:

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}(\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}-\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}(\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}))=O_{p}((nT^{-1})^{1/2}).

(S86)

Applying a Taylor expansion, we obtain:

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}(\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}))

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi,g}(\tilde{\phi}_{j,g})(\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}),

(S87)

where
\tilde{\phi}_{j,g}
lies between
\hat{\phi}_{j,g}
and
\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}
, and

\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi}(\phi_{j,g})\equiv(n_{j,g}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi}(z_{i,t};\phi_{j,g})\text{ \ and \ }\psi_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi}(z_{i,t};\phi_{j,g})\equiv\frac{\partial\psi_{j,\gamma\gamma}(z_{i,t};\phi_{j,g})}{\partial\phi_{j,g}}.

Using Assumptions
8
and
9
, along with the triangle and
Cauchy-Schwarz inequalities, we have

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi}(\tilde{\phi}_{j,g})-\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi}(\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}))(\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi}(\tilde{\phi}_{j,g})-\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi}(\phi_{j,g}^{\ast})\right\|||\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}||

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}M(z_{i,t})}{(n_{j,g}T)}||\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}||^{2}

\displaystyle\leq K\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}(n_{j,g}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}M(z_{i,t})^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}||\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}||^{4}\right)^{1/2}=O_{p}(G_{j}T^{-1}),

(S88)

where the last equality uses (
S13
) and Markov’s
inequality.

Let
\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi,g}\equiv\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi}(\phi_{j,g}^{\ast})]
. By Assumptions
8
and
9
,
along with the triangle inequality and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality:

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi}(\phi_{j,g}^{\ast})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi,g})(\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast})\right|

\displaystyle\leq K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\left|(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi}(\phi_{j,g}^{\ast})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi,g})(\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast})\right|

\displaystyle\leq K\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi}(\phi_{j,g}^{\ast})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi,g}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}||\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}||^{2}\right)^{1/2}.

(S89)

Under Assumptions
1
and
8
, the covariance inequality for
mixing processes implies:

\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi}(\phi_{j,g}^{\ast})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi,g}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq KG_{j}T^{-1},

which, combined with Markov’s inequality, (
S20
) and (
S89
), establishes

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi}(\phi_{j,g}^{\ast})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi,g})(\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast})=O_{p}(G_{j}T^{-1}).

(S90)

Combining the results in (
S87
), (
S88
) and (
S90
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}))

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi,g}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast})+O_{p}(G_{j}T^{-1}).

(S91)

Using the triangle inequality, we can write:

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma\phi,g}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma\theta,g}(\hat{\theta}_{j,g}-\theta_{j,g}^{\ast})\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma\gamma,g}(\hat{\gamma}_{j,g}-\gamma_{j,g}^{\ast}+\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g})\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma\gamma,g}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}\right|,

(S92)

where
\psi_{j,\gamma\gamma a}(z_{i,t};\phi_{j,g})\equiv\partial\psi_{j,\gamma\gamma}(z_{i,t};\phi_{j,g})/\partial a
for
a\in\{\theta,\gamma\}
. By Assumptions
8
and
9
, and Lemma
S1
(iii),

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma\theta,g}(\hat{\theta}_{j,g}-\theta_{j,g}^{\ast})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}(n_{j,g}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{\min_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}||\hat{\theta}_{j,g}-\theta_{j,g}^{\ast}||

\displaystyle\leq Kn||\hat{\theta}_{j,g}-\theta_{j,g}^{\ast}||=O_{p}((nT^{-1})^{1/2}).

(S93)

By Assumptions
8
and
9
, and Lemma
S1
(vi),

\left|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma\gamma,g}(\hat{\gamma}_{j,g}-\gamma_{j,g}^{\ast}+\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g})\right|=O_{p}((nT^{-1})^{1/2}).

(S94)

Moreover, under Assumptions
1
,
8
and
9
, and Lemma
S1
(viii), it follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma\gamma,g}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\left(\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma\gamma,g}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}^{2}]\leq K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}^{2}]\leq G_{j}T^{-1},

which together with Markov’s inequality implies that

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma\gamma,g}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}=O_{p}((nT^{-1})^{1/2}).

(S95)

From (
S92
)-(
S95
), it follows
that

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma\gamma,g}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast})=O_{p}((nT^{-1})^{1/2}).

(S96)

Finally, combining (
S91
) and (
S96
), we obtain

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[C_{2,j,g}(z_{i,t})]}{(n_{j,g}T)\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}))=O_{p}((nT^{-1})^{1/2}+G_{j}T^{-1}),

(S97)

which, along with (
S85
) and (
S86
),
establishes the lemma’s claim.
Q.E.D.

Lemma S5
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
,
we have

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(\frac{(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{T}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})\right)=O_{p}((G_{j}T^{-1})^{1/2}).

(S98)

Proof of Lemma
S5
. The term on the
LHS of (
S98
) can be written as

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(\frac{(\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{2}-(\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})\right).

(S99)

The first term on the RHS of (
S99
) can be expressed as

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(\frac{(\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(\frac{(\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}}{n_{j,g}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})\right)

\displaystyle-\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{(\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}.

(S100)

By Assumption
9
, Lemma
S1
(ii, v), we have

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{(\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K}{\min_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}|\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}|}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{2}=O_{p}(G_{j}T^{-1}).

(S101)

By the similar arguments for deriving (
S97
), we can
also show that

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(\frac{(\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}}{n_{j,g}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})\right)=O_{p}((G_{j}T^{-1})^{1/2}),

which along with (
S100
) and (
S101
)
implies that

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(\frac{(\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})\right)=O_{p}((G_{j}T^{-1})^{1/2}).

(S102)

The second term on the RHS of (
S99
) can be expressed as

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{2}-(\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t}))^{2}}{n_{j,g}|\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})|}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})\right)\right|

\displaystyle+2\left|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(\frac{\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})}{n_{j,g}|\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})||\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}|^{1/2}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})\right)\right|

(S103)

with probability approaching 1. By the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t}))^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t}))^{4}\right)^{1/2}}{\min_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}|\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}|}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{2}\right)^{1/2}.

(S104)

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
(iii),
8
and
9
, we can
apply Rosenthal’s inequality to obtain

\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t}))^{4}\right]\leq KG_{j}T^{-1},

(S105)

which together with Markov’s inequality, and Lemma
S1
(ii,
v) shows that

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t}))^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})\right)=O_{p}(G_{j}T^{-1}).

(S106)

Similarly, we can show that

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(\frac{\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})(-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{1/2}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}|\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]|\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{2}\right)^{1/2}}{\min_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}|\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})(-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{1/2}|}

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t}))^{2}\right)^{1/2}\overset{}{=}O_{p}(G_{j}T^{-1}),

which along with (
S103
) and (
S106
)
implies that

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{2}-(\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})\right)=O_{p}(G_{j}T^{-1}).

(S107)

The claim of the lemma now follows from (
S99
), (
S102
) and (
S107
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S6
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
,
we have

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{T}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})]-\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})]}{n_{j,g}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}=O_{p}((G_{j}T^{-1})^{1/2})

(S108)

and

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{T}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}-(\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{j,\gamma}\left(z_{i,t}\right)])^{2}}{n_{j,g}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}=O_{p}((G_{j}T^{-1})^{1/2}).

(S109)

Proof of Lemma
S6
. Recall that
\psi_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\equiv\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]
. Using this expression, we have

\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})^{2}=\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})^{2}+2\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]+\sum_{t\leq T}(\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}

and

\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})^{2}]=\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})^{2}]+\sum_{t\leq T}(\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})^{2}])}{n_{j,g}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})^{2}])}{n_{j,g}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}+2\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]}{n_{j,g}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}.

(S110)

By Assumptions
1
,
2
,
8
(iii) and
9
, it follows
that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})^{2}])}{n_{j,g}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})^{2}])\right)}{T^{2}n_{j,g}^{2}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(n_{j,g}T)^{-1/2}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})^{2}])\right|^{2}\right]}{Tn_{j,g}}\leq KG_{j}T^{-1},

(S111)

where the second inequality follows by (
S565
) in Lemma
S47
.
Similarly,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]}{n_{j,g}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]\right)}{T^{2}n_{j,g}^{2}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(n_{j,g}T)^{-1/2}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]\right|^{2}\right]}{Tn_{j,g}}\leq KG_{j}T^{-1}

(S112)

which, combined with (
S110
), (
S111
)
and Markov’s inequality, shows the claim in (
S108
).

To show the claim in (
S109
), we first express the term on
its LHS as

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{2}-T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}}{n_{j,g}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t}))^{2}}{n_{j,g}}+2\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]}{n_{j,g}(-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{1/2}}.

(S113)

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
,
8
and
9
, we can apply
Markov’s inequality to obtain:

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\right)^{2}=O_{p}(G_{j}T^{-1}),

(S114)

and

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]}{n_{j,g}(-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{1/2}}=O_{p}((G_{j}T^{-1})^{1/2}).

(S115)

The claim in (
S109
) now follows from (
S113
), (
S114
) and (
S115
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S7
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
,
we have

\frac{\left(MQLR_{n,T}\right)^{2}-(\overline{QLR}_{n,T})^{2}}{(nT)^{1/2}\omega_{n,T}^{2}}=O_{p}(1).

Proof of Lemma
S7
. First, observe that
by the definitions of
MQLR_{n,T}
and
\overline{QLR}_{n,T}
, and the
triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\frac{\left|MQLR_{n,T}-\overline{QLR}_{n,T}\right|}{\omega_{n,T}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left|MQLR_{n,T}-MQLR_{n,T}^{\ast}\right|}{\omega_{n,T}}+\frac{\left|MQLR_{n,T}^{\ast}-\overline{QLR}_{n,T}\right|}{\omega_{n,T}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left|R_{j,n,T}(\hat{\phi}_{j})-T^{1/2}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{V}_{j,i}]\right|}{(nT)^{1/2}\omega_{n,T}}+O_{p}(1)=O_{p}(1),

(S116)

where the first equality follows from Theorem
1
, and the second
equality follows from Theorem
2
. By the definition of
\omega_{n,T}
, Assumption
2
(ii), the triangle inequality and Hölder’s
inequality, we have

\frac{\left|\overline{QLR}_{n,T}\right|}{(nT)^{1/2}\omega_{n,T}}\leq(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{\mathbb{E}[|\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})|]}{\omega_{n,T}}\leq K.

(S117)

Combining the results in (
S116
) and (
S117
), we conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{\left|\left(MQLR_{n,T}\right)^{2}-(\overline{QLR}_{n,T})^{2}\right|}{(nT)^{1/2}\omega_{n,T}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(MQLR_{n,T}-\overline{QLR}_{n,T})^{2}}{(nT)^{1/2}\omega_{n,T}^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\overline{QLR}_{n,T}|MQLR_{n,T}-\overline{QLR}_{n,T}|}{(nT)^{1/2}\omega_{n,T}^{2}}\overset{}{=}O_{p}(1),

(S118)

which shows the claim of the lemma.
Q.E.D.

Lemma S8
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
,
we have

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\phi}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\phi}_{j,i}-\phi_{j,i}^{\ast})=O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}(nT)^{-1/2}),

where
\psi_{j,\phi}(z_{i,t})\equiv\psi_{j,\phi}(z_{i,t},\phi_{j,i}^{\ast})
and
\psi_{j,\phi}(z_{i,t},\phi_{j,i})\equiv\partial\psi_{j}(z_{i,t},\phi_{j,i})/\partial\phi_{j,i}
.

Proof of Lemma
S8
. By the definition of
\psi_{j,\phi}(z_{i,t})
, we have

\psi_{j,\phi}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\phi}_{j,i}-\phi_{j,i}^{\ast})=\psi_{j,\theta}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\theta}_{j}-\theta_{j}^{\ast})+\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\gamma}_{j,i}-\gamma_{j,i}^{\ast}),

(S119)

which leads to the expression:

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\phi}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\phi}_{j,i}-\phi_{j,i}^{\ast})

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\theta}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\theta}_{j}-\theta_{j}^{\ast})

\displaystyle+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\gamma}_{j,i}-\gamma_{j,i}^{\ast}),

(S120)

where
\psi_{j,a}(z_{i,t})\equiv\psi_{j,a}(z_{i,t},\phi_{j,i}^{\ast})
and
\psi_{j,a}(z_{i,t},\phi_{j,i})\equiv\partial\psi_{j}(z_{i,t},\phi_{j,i})/\partial a
for
a\in\{\theta,\gamma\}
.

By the triangle inequality and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, the first term
after the equality in (
S120
) can be bounded as:

\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\theta}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\theta}_{j}-\theta_{j}^{\ast})\right|\leq\frac{||\hat{\theta}_{j}-\theta_{j}^{\ast}||}{nT}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\left|\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\right|\left\|\psi_{j,\theta}(z_{i,t})\right\|.

(S121)

Under Assumptions
2
(ii) and
8
,

\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\right|\left\|\psi_{j,\theta}(z_{i,t})\right\|\right]}{nT}\leq\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}[\left\|\psi_{j,\theta}(z_{i,t})\right\|^{2}]\right)^{1/2}}{nT}\leq K\omega_{n,T},

which, along with Markov’s inequality, Lemma
S1
(iii) and (
S121
), establishes

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\theta}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\theta}_{j}-\theta_{j}^{\ast})=O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}(nT)^{-1/2}).

(S122)

Next, we rewrite the second term after the equality in (
S120
) as:

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\gamma}_{j,i}-\gamma_{j,i}^{\ast})

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right]}{-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}

\displaystyle+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right]}{-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}

\displaystyle+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\gamma}_{j,g}-\gamma_{j,g}^{\ast}+\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}).

(S123)

Using Assumptions
2
(ii),
8
and
9
, we bound the
second moment of the first term:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left((nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right]}{-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\omega_{n,T}^{2}(nT)^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}^{2}]\left(\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right]}{-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}\omega_{n,T}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq K\omega_{n,T}^{2}(nT)^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}(n_{j,g}T)^{2}\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}^{2}]\leq K\omega_{n,T}^{2}(nT)^{-1},

where the second inequality follows from Lemma
S1
(viii). Hence by Markov’s inequality and Assumption
1
(i), the first term on
the right of (
S123
) satisfies:

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right]}{-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}=O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}(nT)^{-1/2}).

(S124)

For the second term in the RHS of (
S123
), we can apply
the triangle inequality and Holder’s inequality to obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right]}{-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq K(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[|\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}|^{2}\right]}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right]\right|^{2}\right]}.

(S125)

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
(ii, iii), it follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right]}{\omega_{n,T}}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right]}{\omega_{n,T}}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle KT\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})}{\omega_{n,T}}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right|^{2+\delta/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle KT\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})}{\omega_{n,T}}\right|^{4+\delta}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right|^{4+\delta}\right]\right)^{1/2}\leq K(n_{j,g}T),

where
\delta>0
is defined in Assumption
2
(ii). The first
inequality follows from Assumption
1
together with a covariance
inequality for strongly mixing processes, and the second inequality follows
from Hölder’s inequality. This together with Assumption
1
(i),
Lemma
S1
(viii) and (
S125
), and
Markov’s inequality implies that the first term on the right of (
S123
) satisfies

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right]}{-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}=O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}(nT)^{-1/2}).

(S126)

By the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, the third term after the equality in (
S123
) can be bounded as

\displaystyle\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\gamma}_{j,g}-\gamma_{j,g}^{\ast}+\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g})\right|

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}(\hat{\gamma}_{j,g}-\gamma_{j,g}^{\ast}+\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g})^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}T^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{2}\right)^{1/2}.

(S127)

By Assumptions
2
(iii) and
8
, and Markov’s inequality,

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}T^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{2}=O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}^{2}n).

(S128)

Combining this with Assumption
1
(i), (
S127
) and
Lemma
S1
(vii), we establish a bound of the third term on
the right of (
S123
):

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\gamma}_{j,g}-\gamma_{j,g}^{\ast}+\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g})=O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}(nT)^{-1/2}).

(S129)

Collecting results from (
S123
), (
S124
), (
S126
) and (
S129
), we obtain:

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\gamma}_{j,i}-\gamma_{j,i}^{\ast})=O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}(nT)^{-1/2}).

(S130)

The lemma follows from (
S120
), (
S122
) and (
S130
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S9
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
, we have

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\gamma}_{j,i}-\gamma_{j,i}^{\ast}))^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-2}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}s_{j,\gamma,i}^{2}\right)\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right)^{2}\right]+O_{p}(G_{j}^{1/2}(nT)^{-1}).

Proof of Lemma
S9
. We start with the
following characterization:

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\gamma}_{j,i}-\gamma_{j,i}^{\ast}))^{2}

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\gamma}_{j,i}-\gamma_{j,i}^{\ast})^{2}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})])+n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})](\hat{\gamma}_{j,i}-\gamma_{j,i}^{\ast})^{2}.

(S131)

By the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, the first term after the equality in (
S131
) can be bounded as:

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\gamma}_{j,i}-\gamma_{j,i}^{\ast})^{2}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})])\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}(\hat{\gamma}_{j,g}-\gamma_{j,g}^{\ast})^{2}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})])\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{2}(\hat{\gamma}_{j,g}-\gamma_{j,g}^{\ast})^{4}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\left(n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\left(\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})]\right)\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}.

(S132)

Using Assumptions
1
,
2
(iii) and the covariance inequality
for strongly mixing processes, we can derive:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\left(n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})])\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-2}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})])\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle KT\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-2}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\mathbb{E}\left[|\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})|^{4+\delta}\right]\leq KG_{j}T.

Applying Markov’s inequality, we obtain:

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\left(n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})])\right)^{2}=O_{p}(G_{j}T).

Combining this result with (
S132
) and Lemma
S1
(iv), we establish the bound for the first term on the right
of (
S131
)

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\gamma}_{j,i}-\gamma_{j,i}^{\ast})^{2}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})])=O_{p}(G_{j}(nT^{3/2})^{-1}).

(S133)

Collecting the results from (
S131
) and (
S133
), we obtain:

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\gamma}_{j,i}-\gamma_{j,i}^{\ast}))^{2}=n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})](\hat{\gamma}_{j,i}-\gamma_{j,i}^{\ast})^{2}+O_{p}(G_{j}(nT^{3/2})^{-1}),

which we rewrite as:

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\gamma}_{j,i}-\gamma_{j,i}^{\ast}))^{2}

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})]}{\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}^{2}-2n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})]}{\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}(\hat{\gamma}_{j,g}-\gamma_{j,g}^{\ast}+\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g})

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})](\hat{\gamma}_{j,g}-\gamma_{j,g}^{\ast}+\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g})^{2}+O_{p}(G_{j}(nT^{3/2})^{-1}).

(S134)

Recall that
s_{j,\gamma,i}^{2}\equiv\mathbb{E}_{T}[\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})]
and
\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\equiv\psi_{j,\gamma}\left(z_{i,t}\right)(-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{-1/2}
. The
first term after the second equality in (
S134
) can be
written as

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})]\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}^{2}}{\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}s_{j,\gamma,i}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}^{2}}{-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}\right]

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}s_{j,\gamma,i}^{2}\left(\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}^{2}}{-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}-\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}^{2}}{-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}\right]\right).

(S135)

Under Assumption
1
, we know that

\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}^{2}}{-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left((n_{j,g}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right)^{2}\right]=(n_{j,g}^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right)^{2}\right].

Thus, we have:

n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}s_{j,\gamma,i}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}^{2}}{-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}\right]=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-2}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}s_{j,\gamma,i}^{2}\right)\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right)^{2}\right],

(S136)

where

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right)^{2}\right]\leq K

(S137)

by arguments analogous to those used to establish (
S1
). Using
Assumptions
1
,
8
and
9
, and Lemma
S1
(viii), we also derive:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}s_{j,\gamma,i}^{2}\left(\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}^{2}}{-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}-\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}^{2}}{-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}\right]\right)\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq n^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}s_{j,\gamma,i}^{2}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}^{4}}{\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\right]\leq Kn^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}^{4}]\leq KG_{j}(nT)^{-2},

which along with Markov’s inequality implies that

n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}s_{j,\gamma,i}^{2}\left(\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}^{2}}{-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}-\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}^{2}}{-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}\right]\right)=O_{p}(G_{j}^{1/2}(nT)^{-1}).

(S138)

Combining the results from (
S135
), (
S136
) and (
S138
), we establish a bound
for the first term on the right of (
S134
):

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})]\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}^{2}}{\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-2}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}s_{j,\gamma,i}^{2}\right)\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right)^{2}\right]

(S139)

\displaystyle+O_{p}(G_{j}^{1/2}(nT)^{-1})\overset{}{=}O_{p}(G_{j}(nT)^{-1}),

where the second equality follows from Assumption
8
and (
S137
).

By Assumption
8
and Lemma
S1
(vii), we obtain a
bound for the third term on the right of (
S134
):

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})](\hat{\gamma}_{j,g}-\gamma_{j,g}^{\ast}+\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}(\hat{\gamma}_{j,g}-\gamma_{j,g}^{\ast}+\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g})^{2}=O_{p}((nT)^{-1}).

(S140)

From (
S139
) and (
S140
), and the
Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we establish a bound for the second term on the
right of (
S134
):

n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})]}{\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g}(\hat{\gamma}_{j,g}-\gamma_{j,g}^{\ast}+\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,g})=O_{p}(G_{j}^{1/2}(nT)^{-1}),

which along with (
S134
), (
S139
)
and (
S140
), completes the proof of the lemma..
Q.E.D.

Lemma S10
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
we have

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\gamma}_{1,i}-\gamma_{1,i}^{\ast})(\hat{\gamma}_{2,i}-\gamma_{2,i}^{\ast})\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t,t^{\prime}\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t^{\prime}})]\right)+O_{p}(G_{2}^{1/2}(nT)^{-1}).

(S141)

Proof of Lemma
S10
. First, we observe
that the term on the LHS of (
S141
) can be written as:

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\gamma}_{1,i}-\gamma_{1,i}^{\ast})(\hat{\gamma}_{2,i}-\gamma_{2,i}^{\ast})\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\gamma}_{1,i}-\gamma_{1,i}^{\ast})(\hat{\gamma}_{2,i}-\gamma_{2,i}^{\ast})\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]

\displaystyle+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\gamma}_{1,i}-\gamma_{1,i}^{\ast})(\hat{\gamma}_{2,i}-\gamma_{2,i}^{\ast})\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]).

(S142)

By the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we can bound the second term after the
equality in (
S142
) as:

\displaystyle\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\gamma}_{1,i}-\gamma_{1,i}^{\ast})(\hat{\gamma}_{2,i}-\gamma_{2,i}^{\ast})\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])\right|

\displaystyle\leq(nT)^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\gamma}_{1,i}-\gamma_{1,i}^{\ast})^{4}\right)^{1/4}\times\left(\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\gamma}_{2,i}-\gamma_{2,i}^{\ast})^{4}\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{i\leq n}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}.

(S143)

By Assumptions
1
,
2
(iii), the covariance inequality for
strongly mixing processes and Hölder’s inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle KT\max_{t\leq T}\left\|\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right\|_{2+\delta/2}^{2}\leq KT\max_{t\leq T}\left\|\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right\|_{4+\delta}^{2}\left\|\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right\|_{4+\delta}^{2}\leq KT.

Hence by Markov’s inequality,

\sum_{i\leq n}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])\right)^{2}=O_{p}(nT),

which together with (
S143
) and Lemma
S1
(iv) leads to:

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\gamma}_{1,i}-\gamma_{1,i}^{\ast})(\hat{\gamma}_{2,i}-\gamma_{2,i}^{\ast})\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])=O_{p}(T^{-3/2}).

(S144)

The first order condition for
\gamma_{1,i}^{\ast}
implies that
\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]=0
for any
i
. Consequently,

\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]=\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]-\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]=\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})]

for any
i
. Using this expression, we can rewrite the first term after the
equality in (
S142
) as:

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}(\hat{\gamma}_{2,g}-\gamma_{2,g}^{\ast})\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\gamma}_{1,i}-\gamma_{1,i}^{\ast})\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})]

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}}{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}}{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}}\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})]

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}(\hat{\gamma}_{2,g}-\gamma_{2,g}^{\ast}+\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g})\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\gamma}_{1,i}-\gamma_{1,i}^{\ast}+\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i})\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})]

\displaystyle-n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}}{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\gamma}_{1,i}-\gamma_{1,i}^{\ast}+\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i})\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})]

\displaystyle-n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}(\hat{\gamma}_{2,g}-\gamma_{2,g}^{\ast}+\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g})\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}}{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}}\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})],

(S145)

where

\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}\equiv(n_{j,g}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma\gamma}(z_{i,t};\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}),

for
j=1,2
, and any
g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}
. By the Cauchy-Schwarz
inequality,

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{2}\left(\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}}{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}}-\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}}{\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}]}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\frac{n_{2,g}^{2}\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}^{2}}{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}(\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}])^{2}}(\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}-\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}])^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{2}\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}^{4}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{2}(\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}-\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}])^{4}\right)^{1/2}}{\min_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}(\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}])^{2}},

(S146)

By Lemma
S1
(viii) and Markov’s inequality, we have

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{2}\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}^{4}=O_{p}(G_{2}T^{-2}).

(S147)

Similarly, we can show that

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{2}(\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}-\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}])^{4}=O_{p}(G_{2}T^{-2}).

(S148)

Combining the results from Assumption
9
and Lemma
S1
(ii), (
S146
), (
S147
) and (
S148
), we establish:

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{2}\left(\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}}{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}}-\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}}{\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}]}\right)^{2}=O_{p}(G_{2}T^{-2}).

(S149)

Similarly, we can show that

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\left(n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\left(\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}}{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}}-\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}}{\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}]}\right)\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})]\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\left(\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}}{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}}-\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}}{\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}]}\right)^{2}(\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})])^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}^{2}(\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}-\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}])^{2}}{\min_{i\leq n}\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}^{2}(\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}])^{2}}=O_{p}(G_{2}T^{-2}).

(S150)

By Assumptions
1
,
2
(iii),
9
, and Markov’s
inequality, we have

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{2}\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}^{2}}{(\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}])^{2}}=O_{p}(nT^{-1})

(S151)

and

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})]\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}}{\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}]}\right)^{2}=O_{p}(G_{2}T^{-1}).

(S152)

Collecting the results in (
S149
), (
S150
), (
S151
) and (
S152
), and applying the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we
obtain an approximation to the first term on the right of (
S145
):

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}}{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}}{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}}\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})]

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}}{\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}]}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}}{\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}]}\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})]

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}}{\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}]}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\left(\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}}{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}}-\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}}{\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}]}\right)\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})]

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\left(\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}}{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}}-\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}}{\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}]}\right)\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})]\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}}{\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}]}

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\left(\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}}{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}}-\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}}{\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}]}\right)\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\left(\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}}{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}}-\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}}{\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}]}\right)\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})]

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}}{\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}]}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})]\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}}{\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}]}+O_{p}(G_{2}^{1/2}(nT)^{-1}).

(S153)

Since
\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}]=\Psi_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}
and
\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}]=\Psi_{2,\gamma\gamma,i}
, by the definition of
\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}
, we have

\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}=\frac{\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})]}{(\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}]\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}])^{1/2}}.

Let
\tilde{\Psi}_{j,\gamma,i}^{\ast}\equiv T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})
. Then the leading term on the far RHS of
(
S153
) can be expressed as

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}}{\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}]}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})]\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}}{\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}]}

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}-\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}]}{(\mathbb{E}[-\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}])^{1/2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}-\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}]}{(\mathbb{E}[-\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}])^{1/2}}\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,i}^{\ast}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g}}\tilde{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{\ast}\sigma_{12,\gamma,i^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}\tilde{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}^{\ast}\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,i}^{\ast}}{n_{2,g}}+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g},i^{\prime}\neq i}\frac{\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}\tilde{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{\ast}\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{\ast}}{n_{2,g}}.

(S154)

By Assumption
1
, Hölder’s inequality and Lemma
S1
(viii), it follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left((nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}}{n_{2,g}}(\tilde{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}^{\ast}\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,i}^{\ast}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}^{\ast}\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,i}^{\ast}])\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(nT)^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}^{2}}{n_{2,g}^{2}}\mathbb{E}[(\tilde{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}^{\ast}\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,i}^{\ast})^{2}]\leq KG_{2}(nT)^{-2},

where we write
\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,i}^{\ast}\equiv\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}^{\ast}
for any
i\in I_{2,g}
and any
g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}
. This along with Markov’s inequality implies that

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}}{n_{2,g}}(\tilde{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}^{\ast}\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,i}^{\ast}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}^{\ast}\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,i}^{\ast}])=O_{p}(G_{2}^{1/2}(nT)^{-1}).

(S155)

Since
\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}^{\ast}\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,i}^{\ast}]=T^{-1}\sum_{t,t^{\prime}\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t^{\prime}})]
, from (
S155
) it follows that the first term on
the far RHS of (
S154
) can be alternatively written
as:

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}\tilde{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}^{\ast}\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,i}^{\ast}}{n_{2,g}}

(S156)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}\left(\sum_{t,t^{\prime}\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t^{\prime}})]\right)+O_{p}(G_{2}^{1/2}(nT)^{-1}).

For the second term on the RHS of the second equality in (
S154
), we can write it as

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g},i^{\prime}\neq i}\frac{\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}\tilde{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}^{\ast}\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{\ast}}{n_{2,g}}

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g},i^{\prime}<i}\left(\frac{\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}\tilde{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}^{\ast}\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{\ast}}{n_{2,g}}+\frac{\sigma_{12,\gamma,i^{\prime}}\tilde{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{\ast}\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,i}^{\ast}}{n_{2,g}}\right).

(S157)

By Assumption
1
, Lemma
S1
(viii) and Hölder’s
inequality, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left((nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g},i^{\prime}<i}\frac{\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}\tilde{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}^{\ast}\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{\ast}}{n_{2,g}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g},i^{\prime}<i}\frac{\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}^{2}\mathbb{E}[(\tilde{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}^{\ast}\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{\ast})^{2}]}{n_{2,g}^{2}}\leq G_{2}(nT)^{-2},

which together with Markov’s inequality implies that

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g},i^{\prime}<i}\frac{\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}\tilde{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}^{\ast}\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{\ast}}{n_{2,g}}=O_{p}(G_{2}^{1/2}(nT)^{-1}).

(S158)

Similarly, we can show that

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g},i^{\prime}<i}\frac{\sigma_{12,\gamma,i^{\prime}}\tilde{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{\ast}\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,i}^{\ast}}{n_{2,g}}=O_{p}(G_{2}^{1/2}(nT)^{-1}).

(S159)

Collecting the results from (
S154
), and (
S156
)-(
S159
) yields an approximation
to the first term on the right of (
S145
):

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}}{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}}{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}}\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})]

(S160)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}\left(\sum_{t,t^{\prime}\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t^{\prime}})]\right)+O_{p}(G_{2}^{1/2}(nT)^{-1}).

We now consider the second term on the right of (
S145
).
By the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, Assumption
8
and Lemma
S1
(vii), we have

\displaystyle\left|n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}(\hat{\gamma}_{2,g}-\gamma_{2,g}^{\ast}+\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g})\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\gamma}_{1,i}-\gamma_{1,i}^{\ast}+\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i})\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})]\right|

\displaystyle\leq Kn^{-1}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}(\hat{\gamma}_{2,g}-\gamma_{2,g}^{\ast}+\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g})^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\gamma}_{1,i}-\gamma_{1,i}^{\ast}+\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i})^{2}\right)^{1/2}\overset{}{=}O_{p}((nT)^{-1}).

(S161)

As for the third term on the right of (
S145
), we have
by Assumption
8
, Lemma
S1
(ii, vi, viii), and
Markov’s inequality:

\displaystyle\left|n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}}{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\gamma}_{1,i}-\gamma_{1,i}^{\ast}+\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i})\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}\right|

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\frac{n_{2,g}\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g}^{2}}{\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\gamma}_{1,i}-\gamma_{1,i}^{\ast}+\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i})^{2}\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(G_{2}^{1/2}(nT)^{-1}).

(S162)

Finally, the last term on the right of (
S145
) can be
bounded by

\displaystyle\left|n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}(\hat{\gamma}_{2,g}-\gamma_{2,g}^{\ast}+\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g})\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}}{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}}\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}\right|

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}(\hat{\gamma}_{2,g}-\gamma_{2,g}^{\ast}+\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma,g}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,g})^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}^{2}}{\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}^{2}}\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(n^{-1}T^{-1/2}).

(S163)

The claim of the lemma now follows from the results in (
S145
), (
S160
), (
S161
), (
S162
) and (
S163
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S11
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
,
we have

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{1,\phi}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\phi}_{1,i}-\phi_{1,i}^{\ast})-\psi_{2,\phi}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\phi}_{2,i}-\phi_{2,i}^{\ast}))^{2}

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sigma_{1,\gamma,i}^{4}+n_{2,g}^{-2}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}s_{j,\gamma,i}^{2}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right)^{2}\right]\\
-2n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t,t^{\prime}\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t^{\prime}})]\right)\end{array}\right)+O_{p}(T^{-3/2}).

Proof of Lemma
S11
. From the expression
of
\psi_{j,\phi}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\phi}_{j,i}-\phi_{j,i}^{\ast})
in (
S119
), we have

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{1,\phi}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\phi}_{1,i}-\phi_{1,i}^{\ast})-\psi_{2,\phi}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\phi}_{2,i}-\phi_{2,i}^{\ast}))^{2}

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\Delta\psi_{\theta}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}))^{2}+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\Delta\psi_{\gamma}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\gamma}_{i}-\gamma_{i}^{\ast}))^{2}

\displaystyle+2(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})^{\top}\Delta\psi_{\theta}(z_{i,t})^{\top}\Delta\psi_{\gamma}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\gamma}_{i}-\gamma_{i}^{\ast}),

(S164)

where

\displaystyle\Delta\psi_{\theta}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})

\displaystyle\equiv\psi_{1,\theta}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})-\psi_{2,\theta}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast}),

\displaystyle\Delta\psi_{\gamma}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\gamma}_{i}-\gamma_{i}^{\ast})

\displaystyle\equiv\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\gamma}_{1,i}-\gamma_{1,i}^{\ast})-\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\gamma}_{2,i}-\gamma_{2,i}^{\ast}).

By the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\theta}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\theta}_{j}-\theta_{j}^{\ast}))^{2}\leq||\hat{\theta}_{j}-\theta_{j}^{\ast}||^{2}(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\left\|\psi_{j,\theta}(z_{i,t})\right\|^{2}.

(S165)

Since
(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\left\|\psi_{j,\theta}(z_{i,t})\right\|^{2}=O_{p}(1)
by Assumption
8

and Markov’s inequality, it follows from Lemma
S1
(iii) and
(
S165
) that for both
j=1,2
:

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\theta}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\theta}_{j}-\theta_{j}^{\ast}))^{2}=O_{p}((nT)^{-1}),

which establishes:

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\Delta\psi_{\theta}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}))^{2}=O_{p}((nT)^{-1}).

(S166)

For
j,j^{\prime}=1,2
, we can apply the triangle inequality and
Cauchy-Schwarz inequality to get

\displaystyle\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\theta}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\theta}_{j}-\theta_{j}^{\ast})\psi_{j^{\prime},\gamma}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\gamma}_{j^{\prime},i}-\gamma_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast})\right|

\displaystyle\leq||\hat{\theta}_{j}-\theta_{j}^{\ast}||(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}|\hat{\gamma}_{j^{\prime},i}-\gamma_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast}|\sum_{t\leq T}\left\|\psi_{j,\theta}(z_{i,t})\psi_{j^{\prime},\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq||\hat{\theta}_{j}-\theta_{j}^{\ast}||\left(n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}|\hat{\gamma}_{j^{\prime},i}-\gamma_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast}|^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left((nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\left\|\psi_{j,\theta}(z_{i,t})\psi_{j^{\prime},\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}.

(S167)

Since by Assumption
8
and Markov’s inequality
(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}||\psi_{j,\theta}(z_{i,t})\psi_{j^{\prime},\gamma}(z_{i,t})||^{2}=O_{p}(1)
, it follows from (
S167
),
Lemma
S1
(iii, iv) that

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\theta}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\theta}_{j}-\theta_{j}^{\ast})\psi_{j^{\prime},\gamma}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\gamma}_{j^{\prime},i}-\gamma_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast})=O_{p}((nT^{2})^{-1/2}).

(S168)

Combining the results in (
S164
), (
S166
) and (
S168
), we obtain

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{1,\phi}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\phi}_{1,i}-\phi_{1,i}^{\ast})-\psi_{2,\phi}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\phi}_{2,i}-\phi_{2,i}^{\ast}))^{2}

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\gamma}_{1,i}-\gamma_{1,i}^{\ast})^{2}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{1,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})+\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\gamma}_{2,i}-\gamma_{2,i}^{\ast})^{2}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{2,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})

\displaystyle-2(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\gamma}_{1,i}-\gamma_{1,i}^{\ast})(\hat{\gamma}_{2,i}-\gamma_{2,i}^{\ast})\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})+O_{p}((nT^{2})^{-1/2})

(S169)

which together with Lemma
S9
and Lemma
S10
, and Assumption
1
(i) establishes the claim of the
lemma.
Q.E.D.

Lemma S12
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
, we have

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t},\hat{\phi}_{i})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})^{2}]

\displaystyle+2(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\Delta\psi_{\phi}(z_{i,t};\phi_{i}^{\ast})(\hat{\phi}_{i}-\phi_{i}^{\ast})

\displaystyle+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\hat{\phi}_{i}-\phi_{i}^{\ast})^{\top}\Delta\psi_{\phi}(z_{i,t};\phi_{i}^{\ast})^{\top}\Delta\psi_{\phi}(z_{i,t};\phi_{i}^{\ast})(\hat{\phi}_{i}-\phi_{i}^{\ast})

\displaystyle+O_{p}(T^{-3/2}+\omega_{n,T}T^{-1}+\omega_{n,T}^{2}(nT)^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})^{2}]+O_{p}(T^{-1/2}+\omega_{n,T}T^{-1}+\omega_{n,T}^{2}(nT)^{-1/2}).

Proof of Lemma
S12
. Recall that
\Delta\psi(z_{i,t};\phi_{i})\equiv\psi_{1}(z_{i,t};\phi_{1,i})-\psi_{2}(z_{i,t};\phi_{2,i})
where
\phi_{i}\equiv(\phi_{1,i}^{\top},\phi_{2,i}^{\top})^{\top}
. Define

\Delta\psi_{\phi}(z_{i,t};\phi_{i})\equiv\frac{\partial\Delta\psi_{\phi}(z_{i,t};\phi_{i})}{\partial\phi_{i}}\text{ \ and \ }\Delta\psi_{\phi\phi}(z_{i,t};\phi_{i})\equiv\frac{\partial^{2}\Delta\psi_{\phi}(z_{i,t};\phi_{i})}{\partial\phi_{i}\partial\phi_{i}^{{}^{\top}}}.

Applying the Taylor expansion, we obtain

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{1}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{1,i})-\psi_{2}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{2,i}))^{2}

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})^{2}+2(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\Delta\psi_{\phi}(z_{i,t};\phi_{i}^{\ast})(\hat{\phi}_{i}-\phi_{i}^{\ast})

\displaystyle+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\hat{\phi}_{i}-\phi_{i}^{\ast})^{\top}\Delta\psi_{\phi}(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{i})^{\top}\Delta\psi_{\phi}(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{i})(\hat{\phi}_{i}-\phi_{i}^{\ast})

\displaystyle+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t},\tilde{\phi}_{i})(\hat{\phi}_{i}-\phi_{i}^{\ast})^{\top}\Delta\psi_{\phi\phi}(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{i})(\hat{\phi}_{i}-\phi_{i}^{\ast}),

(S171)

where
\tilde{\phi}_{i}
is a mean value in between
\phi_{i}^{\ast}
and
\hat{\phi}_{i}
. By Assumptions
1
and
2
(ii), it follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})^{2}])}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-2}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})^{2}]}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\max_{t\leq T}\left\|(\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})/\omega_{n,T})^{2}\right\|_{2+\delta/2}\leq K(nT)^{-1},

which together with Markov’s inequality implies that

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})^{2}=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})^{2}]+O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}^{2}(nT)^{-1/2}).

(S172)

In view of (
S171
) and (
S172
), the
first equality in (
) follows if

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\hat{\phi}_{i}-\phi_{i}^{\ast})^{\top}\Delta\psi_{\phi}(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{i})^{\top}\Delta\psi_{\phi}(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{i})(\hat{\phi}_{i}-\phi_{i}^{\ast})

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\hat{\phi}_{i}-\phi_{i}^{\ast})^{\top}\Delta\psi_{\phi}(z_{i,t};\phi_{i}^{\ast})^{\top}\Delta\psi_{\phi}(z_{i,t};\phi_{i}^{\ast})(\hat{\phi}_{i}-\phi_{i}^{\ast})+O_{p}(T^{-3/2}),

(S173)

and

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{i})(\hat{\phi}_{i}-\phi_{i}^{\ast})^{\top}\Delta\psi_{\phi\phi}(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{i})(\hat{\phi}_{i}-\phi_{i}^{\ast})=O_{p}(T^{-3/2}+\omega_{n,T}T^{-1}).

(S174)

By the definition of
\Delta\psi_{\phi}(z_{i,t},\phi_{i})
, we can
write:

\Delta\psi_{\phi}(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{i})(\hat{\phi}_{i}-\phi_{i}^{\ast})=\psi_{1,\phi}(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{1,i})(\hat{\phi}_{1,i}-\phi_{1,i}^{\ast})-\psi_{2,\phi}(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{2,i})(\hat{\phi}_{2,i}-\phi_{2,i}^{\ast}).

Therefore, (
S173
) follows if for
j,j^{\prime}=1,2
the
following hold:

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\hat{\phi}_{j,i}-\phi_{j,i}^{\ast})^{\top}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\psi_{j,\phi}(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{j,i})^{\top}\psi_{j^{\prime},\phi}(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i})\\
-\psi_{j,\phi}(z_{i,t})\psi_{j^{\prime},\phi}(z_{i,t})\end{array}\right)(\hat{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i}-\phi_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast})=O_{p}(T^{-3/2}).

(S175)

We next verify (
S175
). By Lemma
S1
(iii, iv),

n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}||\hat{\phi}_{j,i}-\phi_{j,i}^{\ast}||^{4}\leq K\left(||\hat{\theta}_{j}-\theta_{j}^{\ast}||^{4}+n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}||\hat{\gamma}_{j,g}-\gamma_{j,g}^{\ast}||^{4}\right)=O_{p}(T^{-2}).

(S176)

By the triangle inequality and Assumption
8
(i), we have

\displaystyle\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\hat{\phi}_{j,i}-\phi_{j,i}^{\ast})^{\top}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\psi_{j,\phi}(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{j,i})^{\top}\psi_{j^{\prime},\phi}(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i})\\
-\psi_{j,\phi}(z_{i,t})\psi_{j^{\prime},\phi}(z_{i,t})\end{array}\right)(\hat{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i}-\phi_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast})\right|

\displaystyle\leq(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{M}_{3}(z_{i,t})||\hat{\phi}_{j,i}-\phi_{j,i}^{\ast}||^{2}||\hat{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i}-\phi_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast}||^{2}

\displaystyle+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{M}_{3}(z_{i,t})||\hat{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i}-\phi_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast}||^{2}||\hat{\phi}_{j,i}-\phi_{j,i}^{\ast}||

\displaystyle+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{M}_{3}(z_{i,t})||\hat{\phi}_{j,i}-\phi_{j,i}^{\ast}||^{2}||\hat{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i}-\phi_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast}||,

(S179)

where
\tilde{M}_{3}(z_{i,t})\equiv M(z_{i,t})(M(z_{i,t})+||\psi_{j,\phi}(z_{i,t})||+||\psi_{j^{\prime},\phi}(z_{i,t})||)
. By Assumption

) and (

(S181)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left((nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}||\hat{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i}-\phi_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast}||^{4}\right)^{1/2}\times\left((nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{M}_{3}(z_{i,t})^{4}\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle\times\left((nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}||\hat{\phi}_{j,i}-\phi_{j,i}^{\ast}||^{4}\right)^{1/4}\overset{}{=}O_{p}(T^{-3/2}).

Similarly,

(S182)

(S183)

\displaystyle\leq

We next turn to verify (
S174
). For
j,j^{\prime}=1,2
,
by the triangle inequality and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j}(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{j,i})-\psi_{j}(z_{i,t};\phi_{j,i}^{\ast}))(\hat{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i}-\phi_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast})^{\top}\psi_{j^{\prime},\phi\phi}(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i})(\hat{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i}-\phi_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast})\right|

\displaystyle\leq(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}M(z_{i,t})||\psi_{j^{\prime},\phi\phi}(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i})||\times||\hat{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i}-\phi_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast}||^{2}||\hat{\phi}_{j,i}-\phi_{j,i}^{\ast}||

\displaystyle\leq K(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}M^{2}(z_{i,t})||\hat{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i}-\phi_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast}||^{3}||\hat{\phi}_{j,i}-\phi_{j,i}^{\ast}||

\displaystyle+K(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}M(z_{i,t})||\psi_{j^{\prime},\phi\phi}(z_{i,t};\phi_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast})||\times||\hat{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i}-\phi_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast}||^{2}||\hat{\phi}_{j,i}-\phi_{j,i}^{\ast}||

\displaystyle\leq K\left(1+\max_{i\leq n}||\hat{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i}-\phi_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast}||\right)(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{M}_{4}(z_{i,t})||\hat{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i}-\phi_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast}||^{2}||\hat{\phi}_{j,i}-\phi_{j,i}^{\ast}||,

(S184)

where

. By the similar

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j}(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{j,i})-\psi_{j}(z_{i,t};\phi_{j,i}^{\ast}))(\hat{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i}-\phi_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast})^{\top}\psi_{j^{\prime},\phi\phi}(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i})(\hat{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i}-\phi_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast})=O_{p}(T^{-3/2}).

(S185)

By the triangle inequality and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})(\hat{\phi}_{j,i}-\phi_{j,i}^{\ast})^{\top}\psi_{j,\phi\phi}(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{j,i})(\hat{\phi}_{j,i}-\phi_{j,i}^{\ast})\right|

\displaystyle\leq K(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\left\|\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\psi_{j,\phi\phi}(z_{i,t};\phi_{j,i}^{\ast})\right\|||\hat{\phi}_{j,i}-\phi_{j,i}^{\ast}||^{2}

(S186)

By Assumption

(S187)

and

(S190)

and

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\hat{\phi}_{j,i}-\phi_{j,i}^{\ast})^{\top}\psi_{j,\phi}(z_{i,t})\psi_{j^{\prime},\phi}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i}-\phi_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast})=O_{p}(T^{-1}).

S190
), we first observe that by the

\displaystyle\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})\Delta\psi_{\phi}(z_{i,t};\phi_{i}^{\ast})(\hat{\phi}_{i}-\phi_{i}^{\ast})\right|^{2}

(S193)

).

Finally, we verify (
S191
). From Assumption
2
(iii) and Markov’s inequality, it follows that

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\left\|\psi_{j,\phi}(z_{i,t})\psi_{j^{\prime},\phi}(z_{i,t})\right\|^{2}=O_{p}(1).

(S194)

Therefore, by the triangle inequality and Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\hat{\phi}_{j,i}-\phi_{j,i}^{\ast})^{\top}\psi_{j,\phi}(z_{i,t})\psi_{j^{\prime},\phi}(z_{i,t})(\hat{\phi}_{j^{\prime},i}-\phi_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast})\right|

(S195)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\times\left((nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\left\|\psi_{j,\phi}(z_{i,t})\psi_{j^{\prime},\phi}(z_{i,t})\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}\overset{}{=}O_{p}(T^{-1}),

).

Lemma S13
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
, we have
\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}(\hat{s}_{j,\gamma,i}^{2}-s_{j,\gamma,i}^{2})^{2}=O_{p}(1)
.

Proof of Lemma
S13
. By the
definitions of
\hat{s}_{j,\gamma,i}^{2}
and

, and

(ii), we obtain the following expression:

\displaystyle\hat{s}_{j,\gamma,i}^{2}-s_{j,\gamma,i}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}{-\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}-\frac{\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})]}{-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}

\displaystyle=\frac{T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t};\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}))}{-\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}

\displaystyle+\frac{T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t};\phi_{j,g}^{\ast})}{\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})

\displaystyle+\frac{T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t};\phi_{j,g}^{\ast})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})])}{-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}},

wpa1. From the expression above, it is evident that the claim of the lemma
follows if the following results hold:

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})))^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1),

(S196)

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t}))^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1),

(S197)

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t};\phi_{j,g}^{\ast})]))^{2}}{n_{j,g}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1).

(S198)

We now proceed to verify (
S196
), (
S197
) and (
S198
).

By the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, the term before the equality in (
S196
) can be bounded as follows:

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})))^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}

\displaystyle\leq KT^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{2}\right)^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}

\displaystyle+KT^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right)^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}.

(S199)

By Assumption
8
and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{2}\right)^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}M(z_{i,t})^{2}||\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}||^{2}\right)^{2}}{\min_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}

\displaystyle\leq K\frac{\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}||\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}||^{4}}{\min_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(M(z_{i,t})^{2}-\mathbb{E}[M(z_{i,t})^{2}])\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+K\frac{\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}||\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}||^{4}n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\mathbb{E}[M(z_{i,t})^{2}])\right)^{2}}{\min_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}.

(S200)

Applying Assumptions
1
,
8
, and the covariance inequality for
strong mixing processes, we obtain:

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}(M(z_{i,t})^{2}-\mathbb{E}[M(z_{i,t})^{2}])\right)^{2}\right]\leq K.

(S201)

Combining this with Lemma
S1
(i, ii, iii) and Markov’s
inequality, we establish

\frac{\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}||\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}||^{4}}{\min_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(M(z_{i,t})^{2}-\mathbb{E}[M(z_{i,t})^{2}])\right)^{2}=o_{p}(G_{j}T^{-1}).

(S202)

Since
T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\mathbb{E}[M(z_{i,t})^{2}])\leq K
, from Lemma
S1
(ii) and (
S13
) it follows that

\frac{\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}||\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}||^{4}n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\mathbb{E}[M(z_{i,t})^{2}])\right)^{2}}{\min_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}=O_{p}(G_{j}T^{-2}),

(S203)

which along with (
S200
) and (
S202
)
verifies (
S196
).

To verify (
S197
), we first observe that by Lemma
S1
(ii), the term on its LHS satisfies:
