\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t}))^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{2}}{\min_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\max_{i\leq n}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})\right)^{2}.

(S204)

By the similar arguments for showing (
S61
), we can
establish

\max_{i\leq n}T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})=O_{p}(1).

Combining this with (
S204
), Assumption
9
and
Lemma
S1
(ii, v), we obtain:

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t}))^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{2}=O_{p}(G_{j}T^{-1}).

(S205)

To verify (
S198
), we observe that by Assumptions
1
,
8
and
9
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})]))^{2}\right]}{n_{j,g}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}

(S206)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{\max_{t\leq T}\left\|\psi_{j,\gamma}^{2}(z_{i,t})\right\|_{2+\delta/2}^{2}}{n_{j,g}T\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\leq KG_{j}T^{-1}.

The condition in (
S198
) now follows from Assumptions
1
(i), (
S206
) and Markov’s inequality.
Q.E.D.

Lemma S14
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
, we have

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(\frac{(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{T}[\hat{\psi}_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}}{\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}-\frac{(\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}}{\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}\right)^{2}=O_{p}(1).

(S207)

Proof of Lemma
S14
. Since we can write

\displaystyle\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{j,g}))^{2}}{\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}-\frac{(\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}}{\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}

\displaystyle=\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{j,g}))^{2}-(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{2}}{\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}

\displaystyle-\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{2}}{\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})

\displaystyle+\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{2}-(\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}}{\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}},

the claim in (
S207
) follows if the following conditions
hold:

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{((T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{j,g}))^{2}-(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{2})^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1),

(S208)

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{4}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1),

(S209)

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{((T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{2}-(\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\phi_{j,g}^{\ast})])^{2})^{2}}{n_{j,g}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1).

(S210)

We now proceed to verify (
S208
), (
S209
) and (
S210
).

By the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, the term before the equality in (
S208
) can be bounded as follows:

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{((T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{j,g}))^{2}-(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{2})^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}

\displaystyle\leq K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})))^{4}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}

\displaystyle+K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})))^{2}(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{2}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K\max_{i\leq n}(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}M(z_{i,t}))^{4}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}||\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}||^{4}}{\min_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}

\displaystyle+\frac{K\max_{i\leq n}(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{2}(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}M(z_{i,t}))^{2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}||\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}||^{2}}{\min_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}.

(S211)

By the similar arguments for showing (
S61
), we can
show that

\max_{i\leq n}T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}M(z_{i,t})=O_{p}(1),

(S212)

which together with Markov’s inequality, Assumption
8
and Lemma
S1
(i, ii, iii) implies that

\frac{\max_{i\leq n}(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}M(z_{i,t}))^{4}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}||\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}||^{4}}{\min_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}=O_{p}(G_{j}T^{-2}).

(S213)

From Assumption
8
, Lemma
S1
(ii), (
S61
) and (
S212
), we also have

\frac{\max_{i\leq n}(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{2}(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}M(z_{i,t}))^{2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}||\hat{\phi}_{j,g}-\phi_{j,g}^{\ast}||^{2}}{\min_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}=O_{p}(G_{j}T^{-1}),

which together with Assumption
1
(i), (
S211
) and (
S213
) verifies (
S208
).

To verify (
S209
), we first observe that the term on its
LHS satisfies:

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{4}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\max_{i\leq n}(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{4}}{\min_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{2}.

This combined with Assumption
9
, Lemma
S1
(ii, v)
and (
S61
) implies that

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{4}}{n_{j,g}\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}^{2}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}(\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})-\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g})^{2}=O_{p}(G_{j}T^{-1}).

(S214)

The claim in (
S209
) follows from Assumption
1
(i)
and (
S214
).

To verify (
S210
), we first observe that by Assumption
9
, the term on its LHS satisfies:

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\frac{((T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))^{2}-(\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2})^{2}}{n_{j,g}\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])\right)^{4}

\displaystyle+K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])\right)^{2}(\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}.

(S215)

Applying Rosenthal’s inequality and Assumption
8
, we get

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])\right)^{4}\right]\leq KT^{-2},

which together with Markov’s inequality implies that

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])\right)^{4}=O_{p}(T^{-1}).

(S216)

Similarly, we can show that

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])\right)^{2}(\mathbb{E}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}=O_{p}(1),

which together with (
S215
) and (
S216
) verifies (
S210
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S15
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
, we have

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{12,\gamma,i}-\sigma_{12,\gamma,i})^{2}=O_{p}(1).

(S217)

Proof of Lemma
S15
. By the
definitions of
\hat{\sigma}_{12,\gamma,i}^{2}
and
\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}^{2}
, we can write

\displaystyle\hat{\sigma}_{12,\gamma,i}-\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}

\displaystyle=\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{2,i})]}{|\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})|^{1/2}}-\frac{\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]}{(\Psi_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}\Psi_{1,\gamma\gamma,i})^{1/2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{2,i})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])}{|\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})|^{1/2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\mathbb{E}_{T}[\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]\left(|\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})|^{1/2}-(\Psi_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}\Psi_{2,\gamma\gamma,i})^{1/2}\right)}{|\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})|^{1/2}}.

(S218)

From the expression above, the claim of the lemma follows if

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{2,i})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]))^{2}}{n_{2,g}\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})}=O_{p}(1)

(S219)

and

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{(\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})](|\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})|^{1/2}-(\Psi_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}\Psi_{2,\gamma\gamma,i})^{1/2}))^{2}}{n_{2,g}\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})}=O_{p}(1).

(S220)

To verify (
S219
), we first observe that by Assumption
8
and the triangle inequality:

\displaystyle\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{2,i})-\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{1,i})-\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))(\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{2,i})-\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))\right|

\displaystyle+\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{1,i})-\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right|

\displaystyle+\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{2,i})-\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t}))\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right|

\displaystyle\leq K(1+\max_{i\leq n}||\hat{\phi}_{1,i}-\phi_{1,i}^{\ast}||)(||\hat{\phi}_{1,i}-\phi_{1,g}^{\ast}||+||\hat{\phi}_{2,i}-\phi_{2,i}^{\ast}||)T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}M_{5}(z_{i,t})^{2},

(S221)

where
M_{5}(z_{i,t})\equiv M(z_{i,t})+\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})+\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})
. Thus, by the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{2,i})-\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})))^{2}}{n_{2,g}\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K(1+\max_{i\leq n}||\hat{\phi}_{1,i}-\phi_{1,i}^{\ast}||^{2})}{\min_{i\leq n}|\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})|}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(||\hat{\phi}_{1,i}-\phi_{1,g}^{\ast}||+||\hat{\phi}_{2,i}-\phi_{2,i}^{\ast}||)^{2}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}M_{1}(z_{i,t})^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K(1+\max_{i\leq n}||\hat{\phi}_{1,i}-\phi_{1,i}^{\ast}||)\max_{i\leq n}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}M_{1}(z_{i,t})^{2}\right)^{2}}{\min_{i\leq n}|\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})|}

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(||\hat{\phi}_{1,i}-\phi_{1,g}^{\ast}||^{2}+||\hat{\phi}_{2,i}-\phi_{2,i}^{\ast}||^{2})\right).

(S222)

By the similar arguments for showing (
S61
), we can
show that

\max_{i\leq n}T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}M_{1}(z_{i,t})^{2}=O_{p}(1),

which, along with Assumptions
1
(i) and
8
, Lemma
S1
(ii, iii, iv) and (
S222
) implies that

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{2,i})-\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})))^{2}}{n_{2,g}\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})}=O_{p}(1).

(S223)

Next, by Assumptions
1
and
8
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle KT^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\max_{t\leq T}\left\|\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right\|_{2+\delta/2}^{2}\leq K,

which along with Markov’s inequality, Assumptions
1
(i) and Lemma
S1
(ii) shows that

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\mathbb{E}[\psi_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\psi_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]))^{2}}{n_{2,g}\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})}=O_{p}(1).

(S224)

The desired result in (
S219
) follows from (
S223
) and (
S224
).

To verify (
S220
), we first notice that the term on its
LHS satisfies:

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\frac{(\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})](|\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})|^{1/2}-(\Psi_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}\Psi_{2,\gamma\gamma,i})^{1/2}))^{2}}{n_{2,g}|\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})|}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(|\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})|^{1/2}-(\Psi_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}\Psi_{2,\gamma\gamma,i})^{1/2}))^{2}}{\min_{i\leq n}|\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})|}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})-\Psi_{1,\gamma\gamma,i}\Psi_{2,\gamma\gamma,i}))^{2}}{\min_{i\leq n}|\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})|^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq K\max_{i\leq n}(\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})-\Psi_{1,\gamma\gamma,i})^{2}\frac{\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}(\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})-\Psi_{2,\gamma\gamma,i})^{2}}{\min_{i\leq n}|\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})|^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})-\Psi_{1,\gamma\gamma,i})^{2}}{\min_{i\leq n}|\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})|^{2}}+\frac{K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}(\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})-\Psi_{2,\gamma\gamma,i})^{2}}{\min_{i\leq n}|\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,i})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,i})|^{2}},

which together with Lemma
S1
(ii, v) shows (
S220
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S16
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
and
3
, we have:

\omega_{n,T}^{2}=\sigma_{n,T}^{2}+(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\left(\sigma_{1,\gamma,i}^{4}+n_{2,g}^{-2}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g}}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2}-2n_{2,g}^{-1}\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}^{2}\right)+O(\omega_{n,T}T^{-1}).

Proof of Lemma
S16
. To prove the
claim in the lemma, we first observe that by Assumption
1
(iii),

\mathrm{Cov}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i},\tilde{U}_{i})=0\text{ \ \ and \ \ }\mathrm{Cov}(\tilde{V}_{i},\tilde{U}_{i})=0.

Therefore,

\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i}+\tilde{V}_{i}+\tilde{U}_{i})=\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i}+\tilde{V}_{i})+\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{U}_{i}).

Together with the definition of
\omega_{n,T}^{2}
, this yields

\displaystyle\omega_{n,T}^{2}

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i}+\tilde{V}_{i})+\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{U}_{i}))

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i})+\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{V}_{i})+2\mathrm{Cov}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i},\tilde{V}_{i})+\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{U}_{i}))

\displaystyle=\sigma_{n,T}^{2}+n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{V}_{i})+\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{U}_{i}))+2n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Cov}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i},\tilde{V}_{i}).

(S225)

We next study the second and the third terms in the far RHS of (
S225
).

For model 1,
\tilde{U}_{1,i}=0
and

\displaystyle\tilde{V}_{1,i}

\displaystyle=(2T^{1/2})^{-1}\tilde{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}^{2}=(2T^{1/2})^{-1}\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=(2T^{3/2})^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)^{2}+T^{-3/2}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right),

(S226)

while for model 2, we have

\displaystyle\tilde{V}_{2,i}

\displaystyle=(2n_{2,g}T^{1/2})^{-1}\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}=(2n_{2,g}T^{1/2})^{-1}\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=(2T^{3/2})^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}n_{2,g}^{-1}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)^{2}+T^{-3/2}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}n_{2,g}^{-1}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right),

(S227)

and

\tilde{U}_{2,i}=(n_{2,g}T^{3/2})^{-1}\sum_{\{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g}:i^{\prime}<i\}}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right)\left(\sum_{t^{\prime}\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i^{\prime},t^{\prime}}\right)\right).

(S228)

Subtracting (
S227
) from (
S226
)
yields

\displaystyle\tilde{V}_{1,i}-\tilde{V}_{2,i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2T^{3/2})^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)^{2}-n_{2,g}^{-1}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)^{2})

(S229)

\displaystyle+T^{-3/2}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}(\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right)-n_{2,g}^{-1}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right)).

For any
h\geq 1
with
t-h\geq 1
and
j,j^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}
,

\mathrm{Cov}\left(\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t-h}\right)^{2},\tilde{\psi}_{j^{\prime},\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}\tilde{\psi}_{j^{\prime},\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right)\right)=\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t-h}\right)^{2}\tilde{\psi}_{j^{\prime},\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{j^{\prime},\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right]=0,

(S230)

where the second equality is by Assumption
3
. For any
h\geq 0

with
t+h\leq T
and
j,j^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{Cov}\left(\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t+h}\right)^{2},\tilde{\psi}_{j^{\prime},\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}\tilde{\psi}_{j^{\prime},\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right)\right)\right|

(S231)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K\alpha_{i}^{1/4}\left\|\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t+h}\right)^{2}\right\|_{4}\left\|\tilde{\psi}_{j^{\prime},\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}\tilde{\psi}_{j^{\prime},\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K\alpha_{i}^{1/4}(h)\left\|\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t+h}\right)^{2}\right\|_{4}\left\|\tilde{\psi}_{j^{\prime},\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right\|_{4}\left\|\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}\tilde{\psi}_{j^{\prime},\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right)\right\|_{4}\leq K\alpha_{i}^{1/4}(h)t^{1/2},

where we use the covariance inequality for strong mixing processes, Hölder’s inequality, Assumption
2
(iii), and a Rosenthal-type
inequality
||\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}\tilde{\psi}_{j^{\prime},\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right)||_{4}\leq Kt^{1/2}
(cf. (
S568
) in the proof of Lemma
S47
). Combining (
S230
) and (
S231
) yields

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{Cov}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}(\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)^{2}-n_{2,g}^{-1}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)^{2}),\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right)-n_{2,g}^{-1}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right)\right)\right|

(S232)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K\sum_{j\leq 2}\sum_{j^{\prime}\leq 2}\sum_{0\leq h\leq T-1}\sum_{t\leq T-h}\mathrm{Cov}\left(\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t+h}\right)^{2},\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}\tilde{\psi}_{j^{\prime},\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{j^{\prime},\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K\sum_{j\leq 2}\sum_{j^{\prime}\leq 2}\sum_{0\leq h\leq T-1}\sum_{t\leq T-h}\alpha_{i}^{1/4}(h)t^{1/2}\leq K\sum_{j\leq 2}\sum_{j^{\prime}\leq 2}\sum_{0\leq h\leq T}\alpha_{i}^{1/4}(h)(T-h)^{3/2}\leq KT^{3/2}.

The last inequality follows since

\sum_{0\leq h\leq T}\alpha_{i}^{1/4}(h)(T-h)^{3/2}=T^{3/2}\sum_{0\leq h\leq T}\alpha_{i}^{1/4}(h)(1-h/T)^{3/2}\leq T^{3/2}\sum_{0\leq h\leq T}\alpha_{i}^{1/4}(h)\leq KT^{3/2},

(S233)

where the final inequality is implied by Assumption
1
(iv). Using (
S229
) and (
S232
),

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{V}_{i})

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{V}_{1,i}-\tilde{V}_{2,i})

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}\left(T^{-3/2}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}(\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right)-n_{2,g}^{-1}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right))\right)

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}\left((2T^{3/2})^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)^{2}-n_{2,g}^{-1}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)^{2})\right)+O_{p}(T^{-3/2}).

(S234)

From Assumptions
1
and
2
(iii) and the covariance inequality
of strong mixing process, it follows that

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}\left((2T^{3/2})^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)^{2}-n_{2,g}^{-1}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)^{2})\right)

(S235)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K(nT^{3})^{-1}\sum_{j\leq 2}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)^{2}\right)\leq KT^{-2}.

Let
\sigma_{j,\gamma,i,t}\equiv(\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)^{2}])^{1/2}
and
\sigma_{12,\gamma,i,t}\equiv\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)]
. A direct
calculation using Assumption
3
yields

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left(T^{-3/2}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}(\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right)-n_{2,g}^{-1}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right))\right)

(S236)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-3}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right)-n_{2,g}^{-1}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-3}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}(\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right))+n_{2,g}^{-2}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right)))

\displaystyle-2T^{-3}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}\mathrm{Cov}(\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right),n_{2,g}^{-1}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-3}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}(\sigma_{1,\gamma,i,t}^{2}\sigma_{1,\gamma,i,t^{\prime}}^{2}+n_{2,g}^{-2}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i,t}^{2}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i,t^{\prime}}^{2}-2n_{2,g}^{-1}\sigma_{12,\gamma,i,t}\sigma_{12,\gamma,i,t^{\prime}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2T^{3})^{-1}\left[\left(\sum_{t\leq T}\sigma_{1,\gamma,i,t}^{2}\right)^{2}+n_{2,g}^{-2}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i,t}^{2}\right)^{2}-2n_{2,g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}\sigma_{12,\gamma,i,t}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle-T^{-3}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}\sigma_{1,\gamma,i,t}^{4}+n_{2,g}^{-2}\sum_{t\leq T}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i,t}^{4}-2n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sigma_{12,\gamma,i,t}^{2}\right)

Under Assumption
2
(iii),

T^{-3}\left|\sum_{t\leq T}\sigma_{1,\gamma,i,t}^{4}+n_{2,g}^{-2}\sum_{t\leq T}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i,t}^{4}-2n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sigma_{12,\gamma,i,t}^{2}\right|\leq KT^{-2},

which along with (
S236
) and the definitions of
\sigma_{j,\gamma,i}^{2}
and
\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}
implies that

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}\left(T^{-3/2}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}(\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right)-n_{2,g}^{-1}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t^{\prime}}\right))\right)

(S237)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\sigma_{1,\gamma,i}^{4}+n_{2,g}^{-2}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{4}-2n_{2,g}^{-1}\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}^{2})+O(T^{-2}).

Using (
S234
), (
S235
) and (
S237
), we obtain

n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{V}_{i})=(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\sigma_{1,\gamma,i}^{4}+n_{2,g}^{-2}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{4}-2n_{2,g}^{-1}\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}^{2})+O(T^{-3/2}).

(S238)

Similarly, by Assumption
3
we have

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{U}_{i})

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{U}_{2,i})=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-2}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\sum_{\{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g}:i^{\prime}<i\}}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2}

\displaystyle=(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-2}\left[\left(\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\right)^{2}-\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{4}\right],

(S239)

which, along with (
S238
) shows that

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{V}_{i})+\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{U}_{i}))

\displaystyle=(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\left(\sigma_{1,\gamma,i}^{4}+n_{2,g}^{-2}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g}}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2}-2n_{2,g}^{-1}\sigma_{12,\gamma,i}^{2}\right)+O(T^{-2}).

(S240)

To bound the third term in the far RHS of (
S225
), we
observe that by Assumptions
1
,
2
(ii, iii) and the covariance
inequality of strong mixing process,

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{Cov}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi\left(z_{i,t}\right),\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})^{2}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h\leq T}\sum_{t\leq T-h}\left(\left|\mathrm{Cov}\left(\Delta\psi\left(z_{i,t+h}\right),\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})^{2}\right)\right|+\left|\mathrm{Cov}\left(\Delta\psi\left(z_{i,t}\right),\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t+h})^{2}\right)\right|\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h\leq T}\sum_{t\leq T-h}\alpha_{i}^{\delta/(2+\delta)}(h)\left(\left\|\Delta\psi\left(z_{i,t+h}\right)\right\|_{2+\delta}\left\|\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})^{2}\right\|_{2+\delta}+\left\|\Delta\psi\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right\|_{2+\delta}\left\|\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t+h})^{2}\right\|_{2+\delta}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K\omega_{n,T}\sum_{h\leq T}(T-h)\alpha_{i}^{\delta/(2+\delta)}(h)\leq K\omega_{n,T}T,

where the final inequality follows by arguments analogous to those used to
establish (
S233
). This implies

\displaystyle\left|n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Cov}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i},\tilde{V}_{i})\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\left|\mathrm{Cov}\left(\tilde{\Psi}_{i},(2T^{3/2})^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})^{2}-n_{2,g}^{-1}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})^{2})\right)\right|

(S241)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\left|\mathrm{Cov}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi\left(z_{i,t}\right),\sum_{t\leq T}(\tilde{\psi}_{1,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})^{2}-n_{2,g}^{-1}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}(z_{i,t})^{2})\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K\omega_{n,T}T^{-1}.

The claim of the lemma follows from Assumption
2
(v), (
S225
), (
S240
) and (
S241
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S17
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
, we have
\omega_{n,T}^{2}\leq K
.

Proof of Lemma
S17
. From the
definition of
\omega_{n,T}^{2}
, we have

\omega_{n,T}^{2}=n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i}+\tilde{V}_{i}+\tilde{U}_{i})\leq Kn^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i})+\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{V}_{i})+\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{U}_{i})).

(S242)

Under Assumptions
1
and
8
(iii), we can use covariance
inequality for strong mixing processes to obtain

n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i})=n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right)\leq Kn^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\max_{t\leq T}\left\|\Delta\psi\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right\|_{2+\delta}^{2}\leq K,

(S243)

where the first inequality follows from arguments analogous to those used in
establishing (
S1
). From the definition of
\tilde{V}_{i}
, we
have

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{V}_{i})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}\left((2T^{1/2})^{-1}\tilde{\Psi}_{1,\gamma,i}^{2}-(2n_{2,g}T^{1/2})^{-1}\tilde{\Psi}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\right)

(S244)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(2T)^{-1}\max_{j=1,2}n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{j,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right)^{4}\right]\leq KT^{-1},

where the last inequality is by Assumptions
1
and
8
(iii), and
Rosenthal’s inequality. Similarly,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{U}_{i})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{2,g}^{-2}T^{-1}\sum_{\{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g}:i^{\prime}<i\}}\mathrm{Var}\left(\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right)\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t^{\prime}\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i^{\prime},t^{\prime}}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n_{2,g}^{-2}T^{-1}\sum_{\{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g}:i^{\prime}<i\}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right)^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t^{\prime}\leq T}\tilde{\psi}_{2,\gamma}^{\ast}\left(z_{i^{\prime},t^{\prime}}\right)\right)^{2}\right]\leq Kn_{2,g}^{-1}T^{-1}

which implies that

n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{U}_{i})\leq n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}Kn_{2,g}^{-1}T^{-1}\leq KT^{-1}.

(S245)

The claim of the lemma follows from (
S242
)-(
S245
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S18
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
, we have:

\frac{(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\left(\left(n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\right)^{2}-\left(n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\right)^{2}\right)}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2}).

Proof of Lemma
S18
. Next note that

\displaystyle\frac{\left|(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\left(\left(n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\right)^{2}-\left(n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\right)^{2}\right)\right|}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left|(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\left(n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}-\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2})\right)\left(n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}+\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2})\right)\right|}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\left(n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}-\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2})\right)^{2}}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\left(n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}-\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2})\right)\left(n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\right)\right|}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}

(S246)

From Lemma
S13
and (
S207
) in Lemma
S14
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{j,\gamma,i}^{2}-\sigma_{j,\gamma,i}^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}\left(\frac{(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{T}[\hat{\psi}_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}}{\hat{\Psi}_{j,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{j,g})}-\frac{(\mathbb{E}_{T}[\psi_{j,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])^{2}}{\Psi_{j,\gamma\gamma,g}}\right)^{2}

(S247)

\displaystyle+2\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{j,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{j,g}}(\hat{s}_{j,\gamma,i}^{2}-s_{j,\gamma,i}^{2})^{2}\overset{}{=}O_{p}(1).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\left(n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}-\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2})\right)^{2}}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}-\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2})^{2}}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}=O_{p}(n^{-1}).

(S248)

By the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\frac{\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\left(n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}-\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2})\right)\left(n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\right)\right|}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(nT)^{-1}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\left(n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}-\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2})\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\left(n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(nT)^{-1}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}-\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2})^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{4}\right)^{1/2}}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{KG_{2}^{1/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}-\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2})^{2}\right)^{1/2}}{(nT)\omega_{n,T}^{2}}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(n^{-1/2}(G_{2}n)^{-1/2}(T\omega_{n,T}^{2})^{-1})=O_{p}(n^{-1/2}),

(S249)

where the third inequality follows by Assumption
8
(iii), and the
equality follows from (
S247
) and Assumptions
1
(i) and
2
(v). Combining the results in (
S246
),
(
S248
) and (
S249
), we get the
desired conclusion.
Q.E.D.

Lemma S19
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
, we have:

\frac{(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-2}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\left(\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g}}\hat{s}_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2}-\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g}}s_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2}\right)}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2}).

Proof of Lemma
S19
. We apply the
triangle inequality along with the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, and obtain

\displaystyle\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-2}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g}}\hat{s}_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2}-\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g}}s_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-2}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}-\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2})(\hat{s}_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2}-s_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2})\right|

\displaystyle+\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-2}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}-\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2})s_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2}\right|+\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-2}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g}}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}(\hat{s}_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2}-s_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2})\right|

\displaystyle\leq(nT)^{-1}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}-\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2})^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{s}_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2}-s_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2})^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+(nT)^{-1}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}-\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2})^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g}}s_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{4}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+(nT)^{-1}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{4}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{s}_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2}-s_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2})^{2}\right)^{1/2},

which along with Assumptions
8
and
9
, (
S247
) and Lemma
S13
implies that

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-2}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g}}\hat{s}_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2}-\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sigma_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g}}s_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2}\right)=O_{p}((n^{1/2}T)^{-1}).

(S250)

The claim of the lemma follows from (
S250
) and
Assumption
2
(v).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S20
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
2
,
we have: (i)
\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}\leq K
and
\hat{\sigma}_{n,T}^{2}\leq K
wpa1; and (ii)
\hat{\sigma}_{S,n,T}^{2}\geq 0
.

Proof of Lemma
S20
.
(i) Since
|\hat{\sigma}_{12,\gamma,i}|\leq\hat{\sigma}_{1,\gamma,i}^{2}\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}
for all
i
, from (
31
), it follows that

\displaystyle\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{1,\gamma,i}^{4}+2n_{2,g}^{-1}\hat{\sigma}_{1,\gamma,i}^{2}\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}+n_{2,g}^{-2}\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{4})

(S251)

\displaystyle+(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-2}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g},i^{\prime}\neq i}\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\left(\hat{\sigma}_{1,\gamma,i}^{4}+n_{2,g}^{-2}\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g}}\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\hat{s}_{1,\gamma,i}^{4}+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\left(n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\hat{s}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\right)^{2}.

By Assumptions
8
and
9
,
s_{j,\gamma,i}^{2}\leq K
. Together
with Lemma
S13
, this implies

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\hat{s}_{1,\gamma,i}^{4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\left((nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{s}_{1,\gamma,i}^{2}-s_{1,\gamma,i}^{2})^{2}+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}s_{1,\gamma,i}^{4}\right)

(S252)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}s_{1,\gamma,i}^{4}+O_{p}((nT)^{-1})\leq K,

wpa1. Similarly,

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\left(n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\hat{s}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}s_{2,\gamma,i}^{4}

(S253)

\displaystyle+2(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{s}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}-s_{2,\gamma,i}^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{2,g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}s_{2,\gamma,i}^{4}+O_{p}((nT)^{-1})\leq K,

with probability approaching 1. Combining (
S251
), (
S252
) and (
S253
) yields
\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}\leq K
with probability approaching 1.

Next, by Lemma
S12
and Lemma
S17
,

\hat{\sigma}_{n,T}^{2}\leq(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\Delta\psi(z_{i,t},\hat{\phi}_{i})^{2}=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})^{2}]+O_{p}(T^{-1/2}).

(S254)

Since
\mathbb{E}[\Delta\psi(z_{i,t})^{2}]\leq K
by Assumption
8
(iii), from (
S254
), we obtain
\hat{\sigma}_{n,T}^{2}\leq K
wpa1.

Finally, since
\hat{s}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\geq\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\geq 0
for all
i\leq n
, the definition of
\hat{\sigma}_{S,n,T}^{2}
implies

\displaystyle\hat{\sigma}_{S,n,T}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{1,\gamma,i}^{4}+n_{2,g}^{-2}\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\hat{s}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}-2n_{2,g}^{-1}\hat{\sigma}_{12,\gamma,i}^{2})

(S255)

\displaystyle+(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}\left(n_{2,g}^{-2}\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{2,g},i^{\prime}\neq i}\hat{s}_{2,\gamma,i^{\prime}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}\sum_{i\in I_{2,g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{1,\gamma,i}^{4}+n_{2,g}^{-2}\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{4}-2n_{2,g}^{-1}\hat{\sigma}_{12,\gamma,i}^{2}).

Next, note that

\hat{\sigma}_{1,\gamma,i}^{4}+n_{2,g}^{-2}\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{4}-2n_{2,g}^{-1}\hat{\sigma}_{12,\gamma,i}^{2}=(\hat{\sigma}_{1,\gamma,i}^{2}-n_{2,g}^{-1}\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2})^{2}+2n_{2,g}^{-1}(\hat{\sigma}_{1,\gamma,i}^{2}\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}-\hat{\sigma}_{12,\gamma,i}^{2}).

(S256)

Moreover, since
\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{T}[\hat{\psi}_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})]=0

by the first-order condition of
(\hat{\gamma}_{j,i})_{i\leq n}
, we may
write

\hat{\sigma}_{12,\gamma,i}^{2}=\frac{(\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{T}[(\hat{\psi}_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{T}[\hat{\psi}_{1,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])(\hat{\psi}_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})-\widehat{\mathbb{E}}_{T}[\hat{\psi}_{2,\gamma}(z_{i,t})])])^{2}}{|\hat{\Psi}_{1,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{1,g})\hat{\Psi}_{2,\gamma\gamma}(\hat{\phi}_{2,g})|}.

Together with the definitions of
\hat{\sigma}_{j,\gamma,i}^{2}
and the
Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, this yields

\hat{\sigma}_{12,\gamma,i}^{2}\leq\hat{\sigma}_{1,\gamma,i}^{2}\hat{\sigma}_{2,\gamma,i}^{2}.

(S257)

Combining (
S255
), (
S256
) and (
S257
) therefore establishes that
\hat{\sigma}_{S,n,T}^{2}\geq 0
.
Q.E.D.

Appendix E
Proofs for Lemmas in Appendix
B

Throughout the proof, we let
x_{j,i,t}
,
\bar{x}_{j,i}
,
\bar{x}_{j,t}

and
\bar{x}_{j}
denote the
j
th entries of
x_{i,t}
,
\bar{x}_{i}
,
\bar{x}_{t}
and
\bar{x}
for
j=1,\ldots,d_{x}
respectively.

Lemma S21
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
6
,
we have
\sum_{i\leq n}\xi_{nT,i}\rightarrow_{d}N(0,1)
, where
\xi_{nT,i}
is defined in (
85
) in the proof of
Theorem
6
.

Proof of Lemma
S21
. The claim of the lemma
follows by arguments analogous to those used in the proof of Theorem
A4
, provided that Lemma
S69
, Lemma
S70

and Lemma
S71
still hold. We next sketch the verification of
these lemmas.

First, we observe that
\psi_{j}(z_{i,t};\phi_{j,i,t}^{\ast})=\varepsilon_{j,i,t}^{2}
and
\psi_{j\gamma}(z_{i,t};\phi_{j,i,t}^{\ast})=\varepsilon_{j,i,t}
for
j=1,2
. This shows that Assumptions
11
(i) and
12
(i) are maintained in Assumptions
5
(ii, iii), respectively. Moreover,
Assumption
12
(ii) holds due to Lemma
S34
(ii) and Lemma
S40
(iii). Additionally, Assumptions
10
(ii) and
12
(iii) hold
for model 1. The only hindering factors for the direct application of
Theorem
A4
are Assumptions
10
(ii) and
12
(iii) for
model 2. This is because the two-way fixed effect model involves
n
and
T

groups across
i
and
T
separately.

However as there is only one group across
i
in the definition of
\tilde{U}_{2,i}
, Assumptions
10
(ii) and
12
(iii) hold for model 2 with
\mathcal{G}_{2}=\{1\}
and
\mathcal{M}_{2}=\{1\ldots,T\}
. Consequently,
we can apply Lemma
S70
and Lemma
S71
to obtain

(n\omega_{n,T}^{2})^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\left(\mathbb{E}[\tilde{U}_{i}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{nT,i-1}]-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{U}_{i}^{2}]\right)=O_{p}\left(T^{-1/2}+\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1/2}\right),

(S258)

and

\max_{j=1,2}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\max_{i\in I_{g}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|n_{g}^{1/2}\tilde{U}_{j,i}\right|^{4}\right]\leq K,

(S259)

respectively. Applying similar reasoning as presented in the proof of Lemma
S69
,

(n\omega_{n,T}^{2})^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[(\tilde{\Psi}_{i}+\tilde{V}_{i}^{\ast})\tilde{U}_{j,i}|\mathcal{F}_{nT,i-1}\right]=O_{p}\left(T^{-1/2}+\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\right).

(S260)

This expression serves as the counterpart of (
S725
). The
detailed proof for (
S260
) is provided at the end of this
proof. In view of (
S258
), (
S259
) and (
S260
), we can verify (
105
) and (
106
) using
the same arguments in the proof of Theorem
A4
.

We proceed to verify (
S260
). Applying Lemma
S69
with
\mathcal{G}_{1}=\{1,\ldots,G_{1}\}
,
\mathcal{M}_{1}=\{1\ldots,T\}
,
\mathcal{G}_{2}=\{1,\ldots,n\}
and
\mathcal{M}_{2}=\{1\}
obtains

(n\omega_{n,T}^{2})^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[(\tilde{\Psi}_{i}+\tilde{V}_{i}^{\ast})\tilde{U}_{1,i}|\mathcal{F}_{nT,i-1}\right]=O_{p}\left(T^{-1/2}+\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\right).

(S261)

By the independence of
\{z_{i,t}\}_{t\leq T}
across
i
we have

\frac{\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[(\tilde{\Psi}_{i}+\tilde{V}_{i}^{\ast})\tilde{U}_{2,i}|\mathcal{F}_{nT,i-1}\right]}{n\omega_{n,T}^{2}}=\sum_{i=1}^{n-1}\frac{C_{2,i}^{\top}e_{2,i}}{n^{2}T^{1/2}\omega_{n,T}}

(S262)

where
C_{2,i}\equiv\sum_{i^{\prime}=i+1}^{n}c_{j,i^{\prime}}
,
c_{j,i^{\prime}}\equiv\mathbb{E}[(\tilde{\Psi}_{i^{\prime}}+\tilde{V}_{i^{\prime}}^{\ast})e_{2,i^{\prime}}/\omega_{n,T}]
and
e_{2,i}=(\varepsilon_{2,i,t})_{t\leq T}
. Moreover,

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{i\leq n}\frac{C_{2,i}^{\top}e_{2,i}}{n^{2}T^{1/2}\omega_{n,T}}\right|^{2}\right]\leq\frac{K\max_{i\leq n}\lambda_{\max}(\mathrm{Cov}(e_{2,i}))\sum_{i\leq n}C_{2,i}^{\top}C_{2,i}}{n^{4}T\omega_{n,T}^{2}}\leq\frac{K\sum_{i\leq n}C_{2,i}^{\top}C_{2,i}}{n^{3}T},

(S263)

where the second inequality is by Assumption
5
(ii) and (
S453
). By the definition of
\tilde{V}_{i}^{\ast}
,

\mathbb{E}[|\tilde{V}_{i}^{\ast}|^{2}]=\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left((\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t})^{2}-\mathbb{E}[(\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t})^{2}]\right)^{2}\right]}{(2T^{3/2})^{2}}\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t})^{4}\right]}{4T}\leq KT^{-1},

(S264)

where the second inequality is by Assumption
1
(ii, iv) and
Rosenthal’s inequality of strong mixing processes. In view of Assumptions
5
(ii, iii) and (
S264
), we can use the same arguments
for proving (
S734
) to show that

\max_{i\leq n}|c_{j,i}|^{2}\leq K\left(1+M_{2}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{2}}n_{g}^{-2}\right)\leq K.

(S265)

This, along with the definition of
C_{2,i}
, implies that

\max_{i\leq n}\frac{C_{2,i}^{\top}C_{2,i}}{n^{2}}\leq\max_{i\leq n}\left(\frac{\sum_{i^{\prime}=i+1}^{n}|c_{j,i^{\prime}}|}{n}\right)^{2}\leq K.

(S266)

Therefore by Assumption
5
(ii), (
S263
) and (
S266
),

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{i\leq n}\frac{C_{2,i}^{\top}e_{2,i}}{T^{1/2}n^{2}\omega_{n,T}}\right|^{2}\right]\leq KT^{-1}

which, together with the Markov inequality and (
S262
),
implies that

\frac{\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[(\tilde{\Psi}_{i}+\tilde{V}_{i}^{\ast})\tilde{U}_{2,i}|\mathcal{F}_{nT,i-1}\right]}{n\omega_{n,T}^{2}}=O_{p}(T^{-1/2})

(S267)

The claim in (
S260
) now follows from (
S261
)
and the above result.
Q.E.D.

Proof of Lemma
6
.
(a) We first
observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\varphi_{n,T}^{2\text{-side}}(p)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\left|MQLR_{n,T}\right|}{\hat{\omega}_{n,T}}>z_{1-p/2}\right)

(S268)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\overline{QLR}_{n,T}\right|>\hat{\omega}_{n,T}z_{1-p/2}+\left|QLR_{n,T}-|\widehat{\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]}-QLR_{n,T}^{\ast}\right|\right).

From Lemma
10
, it follows that

\hat{\omega}_{n,T}z_{1-p/2}+|\widehat{\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]}-\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]|=O_{p}(G_{1}).

(S269)

By Theorem
6
and Lemma
10
,

QLR_{n,T}-\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]-\overline{QLR}_{n,T}=O_{p}(\omega_{n,T})=O_{p}(1).

(S270)

Therefore, by the triangle inequality

\hat{\omega}_{n,T}z_{1-p/2}+\left|QLR_{n,T}-|\widehat{\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]}-\overline{QLR}_{n,T}\right|=O_{p}(1).

This along with
\left|\overline{QLR}_{n,T}\right|\succ G_{1}
and
(
S268
) implies that

\mathbb{E}[\varphi_{n,T}^{2\text{-side}}(p)]\geq\mathbb{P}\left(\left|QLR_{n,T}^{\ast}\right|>O_{p}(G_{1})\right)\rightarrow 1,

as
n,T\rightarrow\infty
. This establishes the claim in this part of the
lemma.

(b) The proof for the one-sided test follows by analogous arguments and is
therefore omitted.
Q.E.D.

Proof of Lemma
7
. From the
definitions of
\tilde{V}_{j,i}
and
\tilde{U}_{j,i}
, we have

\tilde{V}_{i}=\tilde{V}_{1,i}-\tilde{V}_{2,i}=(2T^{1/2})^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(n_{g}^{-1}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{\ast 2}-(n^{-1}+T^{-1})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast 2})-T^{-3/2}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i,t^{\prime}}^{\ast},

(S271)

and

\tilde{U}_{i}=\tilde{U}_{1,i}-\tilde{U}_{2,i}=\sum_{i^{\prime}=N_{g}+1}^{i-1}T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}(n_{g}^{-1}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{1,i^{\prime},t}^{\ast}-n^{-1}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i^{\prime},t}^{\ast})-\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{N_{g}}T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}n^{-1}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i^{\prime}t}^{\ast}.

(S272)

Recall that
\tilde{\Psi}_{i}=(2T^{1/2})^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})
. From Assumption
6
(i), it
follows that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Cov}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i},\tilde{V}_{i})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(4T)^{-1}\mathrm{Cov}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}),\sum_{t\leq T}(n_{g}^{-1}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{\ast 2}-(n^{-1}+T^{-1})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast 2})\right)

(S273)

\displaystyle-(2T^{2})^{-1}\mathrm{Cov}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}),\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i,t^{\prime}}^{\ast}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(4n_{g}T)^{-1}\mathrm{Cov}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}),\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{\ast 2}\right)

\displaystyle-(4T)^{-1}(n^{-1}+T^{-1})\mathrm{Cov}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}),\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast 2}\right).

Using Assumptions
1
and
5
(ii), Lemma
S34
(ii),
Lemma
S40
(iii), and the covariance inequality of strong mixing
process,

\left|\mathrm{Cov}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}),\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{\ast 2}\right)\right|+\left|\mathrm{Cov}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}),\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast 2}\right)\right|<K\omega_{n,T}T\text{,}

which together with Assumption
1
(i) and (
S273
)
implies that

\displaystyle\left|n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Cov}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i},\tilde{V}_{i})\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K\omega_{n,T}n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(n_{g}^{-1}+n^{-1}+T^{-1})

(S274)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle K\omega_{n,T}n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}(1+n_{g}(n^{-1}+T^{-1}))\leq KG_{1}\omega_{n,T}n^{-1}.

Here the last inequality holds by
n_{g}(n^{-1}+T^{-1})\leq K
. Under
Assumption
6
(i) and some direct calculation, we can show that

\mathrm{Cov}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i},\tilde{U}_{i})=0\text{ \ \ and \ \ }\mathrm{Cov}(\tilde{V}_{i},\tilde{U}_{i})=0.

(S275)

Since
\sigma_{n,T}^{2}=n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i})
, combining (
S274
) and (
S275
) yields

\displaystyle\omega_{n,T}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i}+\tilde{V}_{i}+\tilde{U}_{i})

(S276)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i})+\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{V}_{i})+\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{U}_{i})+2\mathrm{Cov}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i},\tilde{V}_{i}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sigma_{n,T}^{2}+n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{V}_{i})+n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{U}_{i})+O(G_{1}\omega_{n,T}n^{-1}).

To finish the proof, it remains to derive the approximations for
n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{V}_{i})
and
n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{U}_{i})
.

Using Assumption
6
(i), we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{U}_{i})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i^{\prime}=N_{g}+1}^{i-1}T^{-1}\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}(n_{g}^{-1}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{1,i^{\prime},t}^{\ast}-n^{-1}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i^{\prime},t}^{\ast})\right)+\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{N_{g}}T^{-1}\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}n^{-1}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i^{\prime}t}^{\ast}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i^{\prime}=N_{g}+1}^{i-1}T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathrm{Var}\left(n_{g}^{-1}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{1,i^{\prime},t}^{\ast}-n^{-1}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i^{\prime},t}^{\ast}\right)+\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{N_{g}}T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathrm{Var}\left(n^{-1}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i^{\prime}t}^{\ast}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i^{\prime}=N_{g}+1}^{i-1}T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(n_{g}^{-2}\sigma_{1,i}^{2}\sigma_{1,i^{\prime}}^{2}+n^{-2}\sigma_{2,i}^{2}\sigma_{2,i^{\prime}}^{2}-2n_{g}^{-1}n^{-1}\sigma_{1,2,i}\sigma_{1,2,i^{\prime}})+\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{N_{g}}T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}n^{-2}\sigma_{2,i}^{2}\sigma_{2,i^{\prime}}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{g}^{-2}\sum_{i^{\prime}=N_{g}+1}^{i-1}\sigma_{1,i}^{2}\sigma_{1,i^{\prime}}^{2}+n^{-2}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{i-1}\sigma_{2,i}^{2}\sigma_{2,i^{\prime}}^{2}-2(nn_{g})^{-1}\sum_{i^{\prime}=N_{g}+1}^{i-1}\sigma_{1,2,i}\sigma_{1,2,i^{\prime}}.

This implies that

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{U}_{i})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{i^{\prime}=N_{g}+1}^{i-1}\sigma_{1,i}^{2}\sigma_{1,i^{\prime}}^{2}+n^{-3}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{i-1}\sigma_{2,i}^{2}\sigma_{2,i^{\prime}}^{2}

(S277)

\displaystyle-2n^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{i^{\prime}=N_{g}+1}^{i-1}\sigma_{1,2,i}\sigma_{1,2,i^{\prime}}.

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2n)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\left(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sigma_{1,i}^{2}\right)^{2}+(2n)^{-1}\left(n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sigma_{2,i}^{2}\right)^{2}-n^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sigma_{1,2,i}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle-(2n)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sigma_{1,i}^{4}-(2n^{3})^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sigma_{2,i}^{4}+n^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sigma_{1,2,i}^{2}.

Since
\sigma_{1,i}^{4}+\sigma_{2,i}^{4}+\sigma_{1,2,i}^{2}<K
by Lemma
S34
(ii), Lemma
S40
(iii), it follows that

(2n)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sigma_{1,i}^{4}+(2n^{3})^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sigma_{2,i}^{4}+n^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sigma_{1,2,i}^{2}=O\left(n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\right).

(S278)

Similarly,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{V}_{i})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2T^{1/2})^{-2}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathrm{Var}(n_{g}^{-1}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{\ast 2}-(n^{-1}+T^{-1})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast 2})+T^{-3}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}\mathrm{Var}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i,t^{\prime}}^{\ast})

(S279)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-3}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}\sigma_{2,i}^{4}+(2T^{1/2})^{-2}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathrm{Var}(n_{g}^{-1}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{\ast 2}-(n^{-1}+T^{-1})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast 2}),

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2T)^{-1}\sigma_{2,i}^{4}+(2T^{1/2})^{-2}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathrm{Var}(n_{g}^{-1}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{\ast 2}-(n^{-1}+T^{-1})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast 2})-(2T^{2})^{-1}\sigma_{2,i}^{4}.

Since

\mathrm{Var}(n_{g}^{-1}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{\ast 2}-(n^{-1}+T^{-1})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast 2})\leq Kn_{g}^{-2}\text{ \ \ and \ \ }\sigma_{2,i}^{4}\leq K,

from (
S279
) and Assumption
1
(i), it follows that

n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{V}_{i})=(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sigma_{2,i}^{4}+O\left(n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\right).

(S280)

Collecting the results in (
S276
), (
S277
), (
S278
) and (
S280
), we have

\displaystyle\omega_{n,T}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sigma_{n,T}^{2}+(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sigma_{2,i}^{4}+(2n)^{-1}\left(n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sigma_{2,i}^{2}\right)^{2}+(2n)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\left(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sigma_{1,i}^{2}\right)^{2}

(S281)

\displaystyle-n^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sigma_{1,2,i}\right)^{2}+O\left(G_{1}\omega_{n,T}n^{-1}+n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\right).

The claim of the lemma follows from (
S281
), the
definition of
\sigma_{U,n,T}^{2}
and Assumption
6
(ii).
Q.E.D.

Proof of Theorem
8
.
From the definition of

\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}
, we can write

\displaystyle\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{4}+(2n)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-2}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+(2n^{3})^{-1}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}\right)^{2}-n^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{1,2,i}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{4}+n^{-3}\sum_{g=2}^{G_{1}}\sum_{g^{\prime}=1}^{g-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g^{\prime}}}\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\left(n_{g}^{-2}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}\right)^{2}+n^{-2}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}\right)^{2}-2(n_{g}n)^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{1,2,i}\right)^{2}\right).

Thus it suffices to show that, for each
g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}
,

n_{g}^{-2}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}\right)^{2}+n^{-2}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}\right)^{2}-2n^{-1}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{1,2,i}\right)^{2}\geq 0.

(S283)

By the triangle inequality and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{1,2,i}\right)^{2}\leq\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}\right)^{2}\leq\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}\right).

Therefore for any
g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}

\displaystyle n_{g}^{-2}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}\right)^{2}+n^{-2}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}\right)^{2}-2n^{-1}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{1,2,i}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle n_{g}^{-2}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}\right)^{2}+n^{-2}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}\right)^{2}-2n^{-1}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}-n^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}\right)^{2}\geq 0,

which establishes (
S283
) and hence the lemma.
Q.E.D.

Proof of Lemma
9
. The first
claim of the lemma follows directly from Lemma
S28
. We now
establish the second claim. By the definition of
\hat{\omega}_{n,T}^{2}
,
we have the deterministic bounds

\hat{\sigma}_{n,T}^{2}-\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}\leq\hat{\omega}_{n,T}^{2}\leq\max\left\{\hat{\sigma}_{n,T}^{2}-\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2},\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}+\sigma_{n,T}^{2}\right\}.

Therefore,

\frac{\left|\hat{\omega}_{n,T}^{2}-\omega_{n,T}^{2}\right|}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}\leq\frac{\left|\hat{\sigma}_{n,T}^{2}-\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}-\omega_{n,T}^{2}\right|}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}+\frac{\left|\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}+\sigma_{n,T}^{2}-\omega_{n,T}^{2}\right|}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}.

(S284)

For the first term, by the triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\frac{\left|\hat{\sigma}_{n,T}^{2}-\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}-\omega_{n,T}^{2}\right|}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\left|\hat{\sigma}_{n,T}^{2}-\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}-(\sigma_{n,T}^{2}+\sigma_{U,n,T}^{2})\right|}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}+\frac{\left|\omega_{n,T}^{2}-(\sigma_{n,T}^{2}+\sigma_{U,n,T}^{2})\right|}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}

(S285)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\left|\hat{\sigma}_{n,T}^{2}-\sigma_{n,T}^{2}-2\sigma_{U,n,T}^{2}-(\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}-\sigma_{U,n,T}^{2})\right|}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\left|\hat{\sigma}_{n,T}^{2}-\sigma_{n,T}^{2}-2\sigma_{U,n,T}^{2}\right|}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}+\frac{\left|\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}-\sigma_{U,n,T}^{2}\right|}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}+o_{p}(1),

where the equality is by Lemma
7
. Under Assumption
6
,
we have
\sigma_{U,n,T}^{2}=s_{U,n,T}^{2}
, where
s_{U,n,T}^{2}
is
defined in Lemma
S32
. It therefore follows from Lemma
S32
that

\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}-\sigma_{U,n,T}^{2}=O_{p}\left(n^{-1}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\right)^{1/2}\right)=O_{p}\left(\omega_{n,T}^{2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\right)^{1/2}\right)=o_{p}(\omega_{n,T}^{2}).

(S286)

Here, the second equality follows from Assumption
5
(i), while the
third equality is implied by Assumption
6
(ii). Combining (
S285
), (
S286
) and Lemma
S30
yields:

\left|\hat{\sigma}_{n,T}^{2}-\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}-\omega_{n,T}^{2}\right|=o_{p}(\omega_{n,T}^{2}).

(S287)

For the second term in (
S284
), Lemma
7
and (
S286
) imply

\frac{\left|\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}+\sigma_{n,T}^{2}-\omega_{n,T}^{2}\right|}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}\leq\frac{\left|\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}-\sigma_{U,n,T}^{2}\right|}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}+\frac{\left|\omega_{n,T}^{2}-\sigma_{n,T}^{2}-\sigma_{U,n,T}^{2}\right|}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}=o_{p}(1).

Substituting these bounds into (
S284
) gives

\left|\hat{\omega}_{n,T}^{2}-\omega_{n,T}^{2}\right|=o_{p}(\omega_{n,T}^{2}),

which is equivalent to the second claim in (
87
).
Q.E.D.

Proof of Lemma
10
. By Lemma
S22
,

\displaystyle\hat{\sigma}_{n,T}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\hat{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\hat{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}+O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}n^{-1}+G_{1}^{1/2}n^{-3/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}+O_{p}(n^{-1})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{4}+\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{4})+o_{p}(1),

where the third line uses the facts that
\omega_{n,T}\leq K
(established
below), and
G_{1}^{1/2}n^{-3/2}=(G_{1}n^{-1})^{1/2}n^{-1}\leq Kn^{-1}
. By
Lemma
S34
(ii) and Lemma
S40
(iii), (
S322
), and Markov’s inequality,

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{4}+\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{4})=O_{p}(1)

and thus,

\hat{\sigma}_{n,T}^{2}=O_{p}(1).

(S288)

Next by Assumptions
1
(i) and
5
(iii), and Lemma
S32
,

\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}-s_{U,n,T}^{2}=o_{p}(1).

(S289)

Moreover, by Assumption
1
(i), Lemma
S34
(ii) and Lemma
S40
(iii),

\displaystyle s_{U,n,T}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sigma_{2,i}^{2}s_{2,i}^{2}+(2n)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-2}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}s_{1,i}^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+(2n^{3})^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\leq n}s_{2,i}^{2}\right)^{2}+n^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}s_{1,2,i}\right)^{2}\overset{}{\leq}K,

and hence, by (
S289
),

\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}=O_{p}(1).

(S290)

Combining (
S288
) and (
S290
) gives

\hat{\omega}_{n,T}^{2}\leq\hat{\sigma}_{n,T}^{2}+\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}=O_{p}(1)

which proves the first claim of the lemma.

Next note that

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2nT)^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}Tn_{g}^{-1}\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}-(1+Tn^{-1})\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2nT)^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}Tn_{g}^{-1}s_{1,i}^{2}-(1+Tn^{-1})\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}s_{2,i}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+(2nT)^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}Tn_{g}^{-1}(\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}-s_{1,i}^{2})-(1+Tn^{-1})\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}-s_{2,i}^{2})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2nT)^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}Tn_{g}^{-1}s_{1,i}^{2}-(1+Tn^{-1})\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}s_{2,i}^{2}\right)+O_{p}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\right),

where the last step follows from Lemma
S38
and Lemma
S44
. By Assumption
1
(i) and Lemma
S34
(ii),

(2nT)^{-1/2}(1+Tn^{-1})\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}s_{2,i}^{2}\leq K,

(S292)

and by Assumption
1
(i) and Lemma
S40
(iii),

(2nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}Tn_{g}^{-1}s_{1,i}^{2}\leq KG_{1}.

(S293)

Combining (
), (
S292
)
and (
S293
) yields

\left|\widehat{\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]}\right|\leq KG_{1}+O_{p}\left(n^{-1}+\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\right)=O_{p}(G_{1}),

(S294)

where the final equality uses Assumptions
1
(i) and
5
(iii).
This proves the second claim of the lemma. In addition, since
\sigma_{2,i}^{2}\leq s_{2,i}^{2}
and
\sigma_{1,i}^{2}\leq s_{1,i}^{2}
for any
i
, the bound
\left|\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]\right|\leq KG_{1}

follows from the definition of
\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]
, (
S292
), (
S293
) and the
triangle inequality.

Finally, to show
\omega_{n,T}^{2}\leq K
, note that

\omega_{n,T}^{2}=n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i}+\tilde{V}_{i}+\tilde{U}_{i})\leq Kn^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i})+\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{V}_{i})+\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{U}_{i})).

(S295)

By Assumption
1
, Lemma
S34
(ii) and Lemma
S40
(iii),

\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i})=\mathrm{Var}\left((2T^{1/2})^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})\right)\leq K\max_{t\leq T}\left\|\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}\right\|_{2+\delta}^{2}\leq K.

(S296)

Similarly,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{V}_{i})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{V}_{1,i})+\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{V}_{2,i}))

(S297)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K\left(n_{g}^{-2}\mathrm{Var}\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{\ast 2}\right)+\mathrm{Var}\left(T^{-3/2}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\right)^{2}\right)+n^{-2}\mathrm{Var}\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast 2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K\left(n_{g}^{-2}+\mathbb{E}\left[T^{-3}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\right)^{4}\right]\right)\leq K(n_{g}^{-2}+T^{-1}),

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{U}_{i})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{U}_{1,i})+\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{U}_{2,i}))

(S298)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left(n_{g}^{-2}\sum_{i^{\prime}=N_{g}+1}^{i-1}\mathrm{Var}\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{1,i^{\prime}t}^{\ast}\right)+n^{-2}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{i-1}\mathrm{Var}\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i^{\prime}t}^{\ast}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K(n_{g}^{-1}+n^{-1}).

Substituting (
S296
), (
S297
) and (
S298
) into (
S295
) yields
\omega_{n,T}^{2}\leq K(1+n_{g}^{-1}+n^{-1}+T^{-1})\leq K
, which proves the final claim.
Q.E.D.

E.1
Auxiliary Lemmas

Lemma S22
.

Suppose Assumptions
1
,
4
and
5
hold. Then

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\hat{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\hat{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}+O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}n^{-1}+G_{1}^{1/2}n^{-3/2}).

Proof of Lemma
S22
. By definition
\hat{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}=\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}-\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})
where
\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\equiv\varepsilon_{2,i,t}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}
, which implies

\hat{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}=\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-2\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})+(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})^{\top}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast}).

(S299)

Similarly, from the definition of
\hat{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}
,

\hat{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2}=\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2}-2\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})+(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})^{\top}\dot{x}_{i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}).

(S300)

Subtracting the population quantity and using (
S299
) and (
S300
), we obtain:

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\hat{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\hat{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}-(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}))^{2}

\displaystyle+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}((\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})^{\top}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})-(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})^{\top}\dot{x}_{i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}))^{2}

\displaystyle-4(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}))

\displaystyle+2(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})((\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})^{\top}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})-(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})^{\top}\dot{x}_{i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}))

\displaystyle-4(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})\\
-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{c}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})^{\top}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})\\
-(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})^{\top}\dot{x}_{i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})\end{array}\right).

Under Assumptions
1
and
4
(ii), Lemma
S34
(ii) and
Lemma
S40
(iii), and Markov’s inequality,

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{4}+\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{4}+||\dot{x}_{i,t}||^{4}+||\ddot{x}_{i,t}||^{4})=O_{p}(1).

(S306)

Combining this with Lemmas
S36
and
S42
, and applying
the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality yields

\displaystyle\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}))^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(1),

(S307)

\displaystyle\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}((\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})^{\top}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})-(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})^{\top}\dot{x}_{i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}))^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}((nT)^{-1}),

(S308)

\displaystyle\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})\left(\begin{array}[]{c}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})^{\top}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})\\
-(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})^{\top}\dot{x}_{i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})\\
-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{c}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})^{\top}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})\\
-(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})^{\top}\dot{x}_{i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2}).

Therefore, using Assumption
1
(i) and (
E.1
), (
S307
)-(
E.1
), we obtain

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\hat{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\hat{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}-(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}

(S317)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-4(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}))+O_{p}(n^{-2}).

It therefore suffices to show that

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}))=O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}n^{-1}+G_{1}^{1/2}n^{-3/2}),

(S318)

which together with (
S317
) completes the proof.

From the definitions of
\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}
and
\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}
, we have

\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}=(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2})^{2}=\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-2\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})+(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}

(S319)

and

\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2}=(\varepsilon_{1,i,t}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t})^{2}=(\varepsilon_{1,i,t}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast})^{2}=\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}-2\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}.

(S320)

Substituting these expansions yields

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}))

(S321)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}))

\displaystyle-2(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast})(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}))

\displaystyle+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}((\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2})(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})).

By Assumption
1
, Lemma
S34
(ii) and Lemma
S40
(iii),

\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 4}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast 4}]\leq Kn^{-2}\text{, \ \ }\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast 4}]\leq Kn^{-4}\text{ \ \ and \ \ }\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 4}]\leq Kn_{g}^{-2}.

(S322)

Combining this with Assumption
1
(i), (
S307
), the
Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, and Markov’s inequality leads to

\displaystyle\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}((\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2})(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}))\right|^{2}

(S323)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}((\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2})^{2}

\displaystyle\times(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}))^{2}\overset{}{=}O_{p}\left(n^{-3}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\right)

and

\displaystyle\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast})(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}))\right|^{2}

(S324)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}+\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2})
