\displaystyle\times(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}))^{2}\overset{}{=}O_{p}\left(G_{1}n^{-3}\right).

Moreover, by Assumption
5
(ii), (
S307
), and
Markov’s inequality,

\displaystyle\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}))\right|^{2}

(S325)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\omega_{n,T}^{2}\times(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}))^{2}\overset{}{=}O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}^{2}n^{-2}).

Combining (
S321
), (
S323
), (
S324
) and (
S325
), we conclude that

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}\left(\omega_{n,T}n^{-1}+n^{-3/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\right)^{1/2}+G_{1}^{1/2}n^{-3/2}\right),

which, together with
\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\leq G_{1}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}
, establishes the claim in (
S318
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S23
.

Suppose Assumptions
1
,
4
and
5
hold. Then

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}-(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast})^{2}+O_{p}\left(\omega_{n,T}G_{1}n^{-1}+n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1/2}\right).

Proof of Lemma
S23
. Using the
decompositions in (
S319
) and (
S320
),
we obtain

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}-(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}

(S326)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast})^{2}

\displaystyle+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}((\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2})^{2}

\displaystyle-4(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast})((\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2})

\displaystyle-4(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast})

\displaystyle+2(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})((\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}).

By (
S322
), together with the Cauchy–Schwarz and
Markov’s inequalities,

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}((\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2})^{2}=O_{p}\left(n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\right).

(S327)

Next, note that

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast})((\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2})

(S328)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{3}-(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}

\displaystyle-(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 3}.

From Assumption
1
(i), Lemma
S34
(ii), Lemma
S40
(iii), and (
S322
),

\mathbb{E}[|\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{3}|]\leq\left\|\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\right\|_{4}\left\|(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{3}\right\|_{4/3}\leq Kn^{-3/2}.

Hence, by Markov’s inequality,

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{3}=O_{p}(n^{-3/2}).

(S329)

Similarly, the remaining three terms in (
S328
) satisfy:

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}\left(G_{1}n^{-3/2}\right),

(S330)

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}\left(n^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{1/2}\right),

(S331)

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}\left(n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1/2}\right).

(S332)

Combining (
S328
)-(
S332
) yields

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast})((\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2})=O_{p}\left(n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1/2}\right).

(S333)

Let
\bar{u}_{1,i}\equiv T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}
and
\bar{u}_{1,i}^{\ast}\equiv\bar{u}_{1,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{u}_{1,i}]
. By Assumption
5
(ii), Lemma
S34
(ii) and Hölder’s inequality

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}[\bar{u}_{1,i}]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\left|(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}]\right|

(S334)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\omega_{n,T}T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}||(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})/\omega_{n,T}||_{2}||\varepsilon_{2,i,t}||_{2}\leq K\omega_{n,T}.

Together with Assumption
1
and (
S322
), this
implies

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\bar{u}_{1,i}]\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}\right|^{2}\right]=n^{-2}\sum_{i\leq n}(\mathbb{E}[\bar{u}_{1,i}])^{2}\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}]\leq K\omega_{n,T}^{2}n^{-2}.

Therefore, by Markov’s inequality,

n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\bar{u}_{1,i}]\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}=O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}n^{-1}).

(S335)

Next, by Assumptions
1
and
5
(ii), together with the covariance
inequality for strong mixing processes,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\bar{u}_{1,i}^{\ast 2}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\right)

(S336)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2T^{-2}\sum_{0\leq h\leq T}\sum_{t\leq T-h}\left|\mathrm{Cov}((\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})\varepsilon_{2,i,t},(\varepsilon_{2,i,t+h}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t+h}^{2})\varepsilon_{2,i,t+h}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2T^{-2}\max_{t\leq T}\left\|(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\right\|_{2+\delta/3}^{2}\sum_{0\leq h\leq T}\sum_{t\leq T-h}\alpha_{i}^{\delta/(6+\delta)}(h)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle KT^{-1}\max_{t\leq T}\left\|(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\right\|_{2+\delta/3}^{2}.

Here the last inequality follows since, by Assumption
1
(iv),

T^{-1}\sum_{0\leq h\leq T}\sum_{t\leq T-h}\alpha_{i}^{\delta/(6+\delta)}(h)\leq K\sum_{0\leq h\leq T}(1-h/T)a^{\delta h/(6+\delta)}\leq K.

By Hölder’s inequality

\displaystyle\left\|(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\right\|_{2+\delta/3}^{2+\delta/3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}|^{2+\delta/3}]

(S337)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\mathbb{E}[|\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}|^{3+\delta/2}])^{2/3}(E[|\varepsilon_{2,i,t}|^{6+\delta}])^{1/3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K\omega_{n,T}^{2+\delta/3}(\mathbb{E}[|(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})/\omega_{n,T}|^{3+\delta/2}])^{2/3}\leq K\omega_{n,T}^{2+\delta/3},

where the second inequality follows from Lemma
S34
(ii) and the
last from Assumption
5
(ii). Combining (
S335
) and
(
S336
) yields

\mathbb{E}[\bar{u}_{1,i}^{\ast 2}]\leq K\omega_{n,T}^{2}T^{-1}.

(S338)

Finally, together with (
S322
), the triangle inequality,
and Hölder’s inequality

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\bar{u}_{1,i}^{\ast}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}\right|\right]\leq n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[|\bar{u}_{1,i}^{\ast}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}|]\leq n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}||\bar{u}_{1,i}^{\ast}||_{2}||\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}||_{2}\leq K\omega_{n,T}(nT)^{-1/2}.

Therefore, by Assumption
1
(i) and Markov’s inequality,

n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\bar{u}_{1,i}^{\ast}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}=O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}n^{-1}).

(S339)

Combining (
S335
) and (
S339
), we
obtain

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}=n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\bar{u}_{1,i}]\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\bar{u}_{1,i}^{\ast}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}=O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}n^{-1}).

(S340)

Similarly,

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})=O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}n^{-1})

which, along with (
S340
), yields:

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})=O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}n^{-1}).

(S341)

Next, define
\bar{u}_{2,g,t}\equiv n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})\varepsilon_{1,i,t}
and
\bar{u}_{2,g,t}^{\ast}\equiv\bar{u}_{2,g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{u}_{2,g,t}]
. By
arguments analogous to those used to establish (
S334
),

\left|\mathbb{E}[\bar{u}_{2,g,t}]\right|\leq K\omega_{n,T}\text{
\ \ \ and \ \ \ }\mathbb{E}[\bar{u}_{2,g,t}^{\ast 2}]\leq K\omega_{n,T}^{2}n_{g}^{-1}.

Hence by (
S322
), Holder’s inequality and Markov’s
inequality,

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}

(S342)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{t\leq T}n_{g}\mathbb{E}[\bar{u}_{2,g,t}]\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{t\leq T}n_{g}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}\bar{u}_{2,g,t}^{\ast}\overset{}{=}O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}G_{1}n^{-1}).

By (
S341
) and (
S342
), the fourth
term in (
S326
) satisfies

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast})=O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}G_{1}n^{-1}).

(S343)

Moreover, by Assumption
5
(i), (
S322
), and
Holder’s inequality

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[|(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}|]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\omega_{n,T}(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\left\|(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})/\omega_{n,T}\right\|_{2}\left\|(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}\right\|_{2}\leq K\omega_{n,T}n^{-1}.

Together with Markov’s inequality, this implies

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}=O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}n^{-1}).

(S344)

Similarly,

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}=O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}G_{1}n^{-1}).

(S345)

Combining (
S344
) and (
S345
) yields

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})((\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2})=O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}G_{1}n^{-1}).

(S346)

The claim of the lemma follows immediately from (
S326
), (
S327
), (
S333
), (
S343
) and (
S346
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S24
.

Suppose Assumptions
1
,
4
and
5
hold. Then

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}s_{2,i}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}]+n^{-3}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{i^{\prime}\leq n}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}s_{2,i,t}^{2}\sigma_{2,i^{\prime},t}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\left(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}s_{1,i,t}^{2}\right)\left(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sigma_{1,i,t}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-2(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}s_{1,2,g,t}\sigma_{1,2,g,t}+O_{p}\left(n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1/2}\right),

where

s_{j,i,t}^{2}\equiv\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{j,i,t}^{2}]\text{, }s_{j,i}^{2}=T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}s_{j,i,t}^{2}\text{, }s_{1,2,g,t}\equiv n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}]\text{ and }\sigma_{1,2,g,t}\equiv n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}].

Proof of Lemma
S24
. By Lemma
S34
(ii) and Lemma
S40
(iii), (
S322
) and Hölder’s inequality,

\mathbb{E}[|(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast})-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}|]\leq\left\|\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\right\|_{2}\left\|\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}+\left\|\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\right\|_{2}||\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}||_{2}\leq Kn_{g}^{-1/2}.

Therefore, by Markov’s inequality,

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast})-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}=O_{p}\left(n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{1/2}\right).

(S347)

By Assumption
1
(i), (
S322
) and Markov’s
inequality,

\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast}=O_{p}(n^{-1}),

(S348)

which together with Assumptions
5
(i) and (
S347
)
implies that

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast})-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast}=O_{p}\left(n^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{1/2}\right).

(S349)

Therefore,

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast})^{2}

(S350)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast})-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast})^{2}+O_{p}\left(n^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{1/2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast})^{2}+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}

\displaystyle-2(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast})\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}+O_{p}\left(n^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{1/2}\right).

The claim of the lemma follows from (
S350
), Lemmas
S25
-
S27
below.
Q.E.D.

Lemma S25
.

Suppose Assumptions
1
,
4
and
5
hold. Then

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}s_{2,i}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}]

\displaystyle+n^{-3}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{i^{\prime}\leq n}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}s_{2,i,t}^{2}\sigma_{2,i^{\prime},t}^{2}\right)+O_{p}\left(n^{-3/2}\right).

Proof of Lemma
S25
. We begin by
decomposing

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast 2}

(S351)

\displaystyle+2(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}.

We analyze the three terms on the right-hand side in turn.

Using Assumption
1
, Lemma
S34
(ii), (
S322
), the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and Markov’s
inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}])\right|\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}\right]

(S352)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}])\right)^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 4}\right]\right)^{1/2}\leq Kn^{-3/2}.

This, together with Markov’s inequality, implies that

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}]\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}])\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}

(S353)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}s_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}+O_{p}(n^{-3/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}s_{2,i}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}+O_{p}(n^{-3/2}),

where
s_{2,i}^{2}=T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}s_{2,i,t}^{2}
. Using Assumption
1
, Lemma
S34
(ii), and (
S322
), we further
have

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}s_{2,i}^{2}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}])\right)^{2}\right]=n^{-2}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[s_{2,i}^{4}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}])^{2}]\leq Kn^{-2}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 4}]\leq Kn^{-3},

which implies

n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}s_{2,i}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}=n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}s_{2,i}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}]+O_{p}(n^{-3/2}).

(S354)

Combining (
S353
) and (
S354
) yields

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}=n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}s_{2,i}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}]+O_{p}(n^{-3/2}).

(S355)

Using Assumption
1
, (
S322
) and Lemma
S34
(ii), we have

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}])\right|\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast 2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left(n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}])\right)^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast 4}\right]\right)^{1/2}\leq Kn^{-3/2}.

Together with Markov’s inequality, this implies that

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast 2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\left(n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}])\right)\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast 2}

(S356)

\displaystyle+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}]\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast 2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}s_{2,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast 2}+O_{p}(n^{-3/2}),

where
s_{2,t}^{2}\equiv n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}s_{2,i,t}^{2}
. By the
definition of
\ \bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}
, we can write

\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast 2}=n^{-2}\sum_{i\leq n}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast 2}+2n^{-2}\sum_{i=2}^{n}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{i-1}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i^{\prime},t}^{\ast}.

(S357)

Using this expression, we may write

T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}s_{2,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast 2}=(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{i\leq n}s_{2,t}^{2}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast 2}+2(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{i=2}^{n}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{i-1}s_{2,t}^{2}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i^{\prime},t}^{\ast}.

(S358)

Under Assumption
1
and Lemma
S34
(ii), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left((n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}s_{2,t}^{2}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast 2}-\sigma_{2,i,t}^{2})\right)^{2}\right]

(S359)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(n^{2}T)^{-2}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t\leq T}s_{2,t}^{2}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast 2}-\sigma_{2,i,t}^{2})\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K(n^{4}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\max_{t\leq T}\left\|s_{2,t}^{2}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast 2}-\sigma_{2,i,t}^{2})\right\|_{2+\delta}^{2}\leq Kn^{-4}

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left((n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i=2}^{n}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{i-1}\sum_{t\leq T}s_{2,t}^{2}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i^{\prime},t}^{\ast}\right)^{2}\right]

(S360)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(n^{2}T)^{-2}\sum_{i=2}^{n}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{i-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t\leq T}s_{2,t}^{2}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i^{\prime},t}^{\ast}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K(n^{4}T)^{-1}\sum_{i=2}^{n}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{i-1}\max_{t\leq T}||\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i^{\prime},t}^{\ast}||_{2+\delta}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K(n^{4}T)^{-1}\sum_{i=2}^{n}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{i-1}\max_{t\leq T}||\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}||_{4+2\delta}^{2}||\varepsilon_{2,i^{\prime},t}^{\ast}||_{4+2\delta}^{2}\leq Kn^{-3}.

Therefore, by (
S358
)-(
S360
), and
Markov’s inequality,

T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}s_{2,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast 2}=(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{i\leq n}s_{2,t}^{2}\sigma_{2,i,t}^{2}+O_{p}(n^{-3/2}).

(S361)

Combining (
S356
), (
S357
) and (
S361
) yields

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast 2}=n^{-3}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{i^{\prime}\leq n}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}s_{2,i,t}^{2}\sigma_{2,i^{\prime},t}^{2}\right)+O_{p}(n^{-3/2}).

(S362)

The third term in (
S351
) can be written as

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}]

(S363)

\displaystyle+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}\sum_{i\leq n}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}]).

By the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\left((nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}\sum_{i\leq n}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}])\right)^{2}

(S364)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast 2}\times T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\left(n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}])\right)^{2}.

From Assumption
1
, Lemma
S34
(ii) and (
S322
), it follows that

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}])\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{4}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{i\leq n}(\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{8}]\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 4}])^{1/2}\leq Kn^{-2}.

Hence by Markov’s inequality,

T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\left(n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}])\right)^{2}=O_{p}(n^{-2}).

(S365)

Since
T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast 2}=O_{p}(n^{-1})

by (
S322
), from (
S364
) and (
S365
) it follows that

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}\sum_{i\leq n}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}])=O_{p}(n^{-3/2}).

(S366)

Let
v_{2,t}\equiv\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}]
. Then we may write:

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}]=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}v_{2,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}=(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}v_{2,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}.

Note that
\left|\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}]\right|\leq K
by Assumption
1
(i), (
S322
) and Lemma
S34
(ii). Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left((nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}]\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(n^{2}T)^{-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}v_{2,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(n^{2}T)^{-2}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t\leq T}v_{2,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K(n^{4}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\max_{t\leq T}\left\|v_{2,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\right\|_{2+\delta}^{2}\leq Kn^{-4},

which together with Markov’s inequality implies that

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}]=O_{p}(n^{-2}).

(S367)

From (
S363
), (
S366
) and (
S367
), we obtain

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{i\leq n}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}=O_{p}(n^{-3/2}).

(S368)

The claim of the lemma now follows from (
S351
), (
S355
), (
S362
) and (
S367
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S26
.

Suppose Assumptions
1
,
4
and
5
. Then

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\left(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}s_{1,i,t}^{2}\right)\left(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sigma_{1,i,t}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+O_{p}\left(n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1/2}\right).

Proof of Lemma
S26
. We begin the proof by
writing

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}]\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}

(S369)

\displaystyle+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}])\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}.

By Hölder’s inequality, the second term in (
S369
)
satisfies

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}])\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}\right|\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}])\right|\right]

(S370)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}])\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}||\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}||_{2}\left\|\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}])\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle KT\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1/2},

where the last inequality holds by Assumption
1
, Lemma
S34
(ii) and (
S322
). Therefore,

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}])\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}=O_{p}\left(n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1/2}\right).

(S371)

The first term in (
S369
) satisfies

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}](\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}])\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}])\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}]\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t\leq T}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}])\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}]\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle TK\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\max_{t\leq T}\left\|(\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}])\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}]\right\|_{2+\delta}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle KT\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{2}\left\|\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}\right\|_{2+\delta}^{2}\leq KG_{1}T,

which, together with Assumption
1
(i) and Markov’s inequality, implies

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}](\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}])=O_{p}(G_{1}^{1/2}n^{-3/2}).

(S372)

It is clear that
\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}]=n_{g}^{-2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sigma_{1,i,t}^{2}
. Hence

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}]\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}]\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}]

(S373)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\left(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}s_{1,i,t}^{2}\right)\left(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sigma_{1,i,t}^{2}\right).

The claim of the lemma follows from (
S369
), (
S371
), (
S372
) and (
S373
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S27
.

Suppose Assumptions
1
,
4
and
5
hold. Then

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast})\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}=(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}s_{1,2,g,t}\sigma_{1,2,g,t}+O_{p}(G_{1}n^{-3/2}).

Proof of Lemma
S27
. Let
\bar{v}_{1,g,t}\equiv n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}
and
\bar{v}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}\equiv\bar{v}_{1,g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{v}_{1,g,t}]
. Then

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{t\leq T}n_{g}\bar{v}_{1,g,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}

(S374)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{t\leq T}n_{g}\mathbb{E}[\bar{v}_{1,g,t}]\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}+\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{t\leq T}n_{g}\bar{v}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}.

From Assumption
1
, Lemma
S34
(ii) and Lemma
S40
(iii),

\mathbb{E}[\bar{v}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}]\leq n_{g}^{-2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}]\leq Kn_{g}^{-2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\left\|\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}\right\|_{2}\leq Kn_{g}^{-1}T^{-1}.

(S375)

This, together with (
S322
) and Hölder’s inequality,
implies that

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{t\leq T}n_{g}\mathbb{E}[|\bar{v}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}|]\leq\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{t\leq T}n_{g}\left\|\bar{v}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\left\|\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\leq KG_{1}T^{1/2}.

Therefore, by Assumption
1
(i) and Markov’s inequality,

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{t\leq T}n_{g}\bar{v}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}=O_{p}(G_{1}n^{-3/2}).

(S376)

From Assumption
1
, Lemma
S34
(ii) and Lemma
S40
(iii),

\left|\mathbb{E}[\bar{v}_{1,g,t}]\right|\leq n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\mathbb{E}[|\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}|]\leq n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\left\|\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\right\|_{2}\left\|\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\leq KT^{-1/2},

which, together with Assumption
1
and (
S322
),
implies that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{t\leq T}n_{g}\mathbb{E}[\bar{v}_{1,g,t}]\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\bar{v}_{1,g,t}]\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\bar{v}_{1,g,t}]\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{\ast}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle KT\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\max_{t\leq T}\left\|\mathbb{E}[\bar{v}_{1,g,t}]\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{\ast}\right\|_{2+\delta}^{2}\leq Kn.

Therefore, by Assumption
1
(i) and Markov’s inequality,

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{t\leq T}n_{g}\mathbb{E}[\bar{v}_{1,g,t}]\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}=O_{p}(n^{-3/2}),

(S377)

From (
S374
), (
S376
) and (
S377
), it follows that

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}=O_{p}(G_{1}n^{-3/2}).

(S378)

Let
\bar{v}_{2,g,t}\equiv n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}
and
\bar{v}_{2,g,t}^{\ast}\equiv\bar{v}_{2,g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{v}_{2,g,t}]
. Then

\displaystyle\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}\bar{v}_{2,g,t}

(S379)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}\mathbb{E}[\bar{v}_{2,g,t}]+\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}\bar{v}_{2,g,t}^{\ast}.

Using Assumption
1
, Lemma
S34
(ii) and Lemma
S40
(iii),

\mathbb{E}[\bar{v}_{2,g,t}^{\ast 2}]\leq n_{g}^{-2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\mathbb{E}[|\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}|^{2}]\leq Kn_{{}g}^{-1}.

(S380)

This, together with (
S322
) and Hölder’s inequality,
implies that

\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}\mathbb{E}[|\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}\bar{v}_{2,g,t}^{\ast}|]\leq\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}\left\|\bar{v}_{2,g,t}^{\ast}\right\|_{2}\left\|\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}\right\|_{4}\left\|\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}\right\|_{4}\leq KG_{1}Tn^{-1/2}.

Therefore, by Assumption
1
(i) and Markov’s inequality, the second
term in (
S379
) satisfies

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}\bar{v}_{2,g,t}^{\ast}=O_{p}(G_{1}n^{-3/2}).

(S381)

Note that
\mathbb{E}[\bar{v}_{2,g,t}]=s_{1,2,g,t}
. Let
\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{\ast}\equiv s_{1,2,g,t}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{\ast}
. Then the
first term in (
S379
) can be written as

\displaystyle\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}\mathbb{E}[\bar{v}_{2,g,t}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\left(\sum_{i\leq n}\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{\ast}\right)\left(\sum_{i\leq n}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\right)

(S382)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\sum_{i^{\prime}=2}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{i-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1,i^{\prime},t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}+\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i^{\prime},t}^{\ast}).

Under Assumption
1
, Lemma
S34
(ii) and Lemma
S40
(iii), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}])\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t\leq T}(\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}])\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle KT\sum_{i\leq n}\max_{t\leq T}\left\|\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\right\|_{2+\delta}^{2}\leq KnT,

which together with Assumption
1
(i) and Markov’s inequality implies
that

\displaystyle(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}]+O_{p}(n^{-2})

(S383)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}s_{1,2,g,t}\sigma_{1,2,g,t}+O_{p}(n^{-2}).

Under Assumption
1
, Lemma
S34
(ii) and Lemma
S40
(iii), we have

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{i^{\prime}=2}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{i-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1,i^{\prime},t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\right)^{2}\right]=\sum_{i^{\prime}=2}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{i-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1,i^{\prime},t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\right)^{2}\right]\leq Kn^{2}T.

(S384)

Similarly,

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{i^{\prime}=2}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{i-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i^{\prime},t}^{\ast}\right)^{2}\right]\leq Kn^{2}T

which together with Assumption
1
(i), (
S384
) and
Markov’s inequality implies that

(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i^{\prime}=2}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{i-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1,i^{\prime},t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}+\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i^{\prime},t}^{\ast})=O_{p}(n^{-3/2}).

(S385)

From (
S382
), (
S383
) and (
S385
), it follows that

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}\mathbb{E}[\bar{v}_{2,g,t}]=(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}s_{1,2,g,t}\sigma_{1,2,g,t}+O_{p}(n^{-3/2}).

This, along with (
S381
), establishes the claim of the
lemma.
Q.E.D.

Lemma S28
.

Suppose Assumptions
1
,
4
,
5
and
6
hold. Then

\frac{\widehat{\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]}-\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]}{\omega_{n,T}}=o_{p}(1).

(S386)

Proof of Lemma
9
. Under
Assumption
6
(i),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{t\leq T}(n_{g}^{-1}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{\ast 2}-n^{-1}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast 2})-T^{-1}\left(\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\left[\sum_{t\leq T}(n_{g}^{-1}\sigma_{1,i}^{2}-n^{-1}\sigma_{2,i}^{2})-\sigma_{2,i}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(Tn_{g}^{-1}\sigma_{1,i}^{2}-(1+Tn^{-1})\sigma_{2,i}^{2}).

Therefore,

\widehat{\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]}-\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]=(2nT)^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}Tn_{g}^{-1}(\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}-\sigma_{1,i}^{2})-(1+Tn^{-1})\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}-\sigma_{2,i}^{2})\right).

(S387)

Under Assumption
6
(i) and (
S437
), we have
\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}]=0
. This implies that
\sigma_{2,i}^{2}=s_{2,i}^{2}
. Similarly, under Assumption
6
(i) and (
S504
),
\sigma_{1,i}^{2}=s_{1,i}^{2}
. Therefore, by Assumption
1
(i), Lemmas
S38
and
S44
,

\widehat{\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]}-\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]=O_{p}\left(n^{-1}+\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\right).

(S388)

The claim of the lemma follows from Assumptions
5
(i) and
6
(ii), and (
S388
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S29
.

Suppose Assumptions
1
,
4
,
5
and
6
hold. Then under the null hypothesis,

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{\hat{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\hat{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2}}{2}-(nT)^{-1/2}\widehat{\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]}=O_{p}\left(n^{-1}\right).

Proof of Lemma
S29
. From Theorem
6
and Lemma
S28
, we have

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{\hat{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\hat{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2}}{2}-(nT)^{-1/2}\widehat{\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]}

(S389)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]}+\overline{QLR}_{n,T}}{(nT)^{1/2}}+\frac{QLR_{n,T}-\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]-\overline{QLR}_{n,T}}{(nT)^{1/2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]-\widehat{\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]}+\overline{QLR}_{n,T}}{(nT)^{1/2}}+O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}(nT)^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1/2}\overline{QLR}_{n,T}+O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}(nT)^{-1/2})

where

\overline{QLR}_{n,T}=(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{s_{2,i,t}^{2}-s_{1,i,t}^{2}}{2}.

Since
\overline{QLR}_{n,T}=0
under the null hypothesis, from (
S389
) it follows that

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{\hat{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\hat{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2}}{2}-(nT)^{-1/2}\widehat{\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]}=O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}(nT)^{-1/2}).

(S390)

Moreover, from Lemma
10
(iii), we have
\omega_{n,T}=O(1)
. This along with Assumption
1
(i) and (
S390
) establishes the claim of the lemma.
Q.E.D.

Lemma S30
.

Suppose Assumptions
1
,
4
,
5
and
6
hold. Then

\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{n,T}^{2}-(\sigma_{n,T}^{2}+2\sigma_{U,n,T}^{2})}{\omega_{n,T}^{2}}=o_{p}(1),

under the null hypothesis.

Proof of Lemma
S30
. Applying Assumptions
5
(i) and
6
(ii), Lemmas
S22
-
S24
, we obtain:

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\hat{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\hat{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}

(S391)

\displaystyle+4n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}s_{2,i}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}]+4n^{-3}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{i^{\prime}\leq n}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}s_{2,i,t}^{2}\sigma_{2,i^{\prime},t}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+4(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\left(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}s_{1,i,t}^{2}\right)\left(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sigma_{1,i,t}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-8(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}s_{1,2,g,t}\sigma_{1,2,g,t}+o_{p}\left(\omega_{n,T}^{2}\right).

Under Assumption
6
(i) and the null hypothesis, we have
s_{j,i,t}^{2}=\sigma_{j,i,t}^{2}
for
j=1,2
,
s_{1,2,g,t}=\sigma_{1,2,g,t}
and
\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 2}]=T^{-1}\sigma_{2,i}^{2}
. Hence,

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\hat{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\hat{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}

(S392)

\displaystyle+4(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sigma_{2,i}^{4}+4n^{-3}\left(\sum_{i\leq n}\sigma_{2,i}^{2}\right)^{2}+4n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\left(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sigma_{1,i}^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle-8n^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sigma_{1,2,i}\right)^{2}+o_{p}\left(\omega_{n,T}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}+8\sigma_{U,n,T}^{2}+o_{p}\left(\omega_{n,T}^{2}\right).

By Assumptions
1
,
5
(ii) and
6
(i),

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}]+O_{p}(\omega_{n,T}^{2}n^{-1}),

which, together with (
S392
), yields

(4nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\hat{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\hat{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}=(4n)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}]+2\sigma_{U,n,T}^{2}+o_{p}\left(\omega_{n,T}^{2}\right).

(S393)

Under the null hypothesis
\sigma_{n,T}^{2}=(4n)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}]
, so (
S393
) implies

(4nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\hat{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\hat{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}=\sigma_{n,T}^{2}+2\sigma_{U,n,T}^{2}+o_{p}\left(\omega_{n,T}^{2}\right).

(S394)

Moreover, Lemma
S29
implies

(nT)^{-1/2}MQLR_{n,T}=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{\hat{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\hat{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2}}{2}-(nT)^{-1/2}\widehat{\mathbb{E}[S_{n,T}]}=O_{p}\left(n^{-1}\right),

which, together with (
S394
), yields:

\displaystyle\hat{\sigma}_{n,T}^{2}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{(\hat{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\hat{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}}{4}-(nT)^{-1}\left(MQLR_{n,T}\right)^{2}

(S395)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sigma_{n,T}^{2}+2\sigma_{U,n,T}^{2}+O_{p}\left(n^{-2}\right)+o_{p}\left(\omega_{n,T}^{2}\right)=\sigma_{n,T}^{2}+2\sigma_{U,n,T}^{2}+o_{p}\left(\omega_{n,T}^{2}\right)

where the second equality follows from Assumption
5
(i). The claim of
the lemma follows immediately from (
S395
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S31
.

Suppose Assumptions
1
,
4
and
5
hold. Then

n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{1,2,i}-s_{1,2,i})\right)^{2}=O_{p}\left(G_{1}n^{-1}\right).

Proof of Lemma
S31
. By the definition of
\hat{\sigma}_{1,2,i}
,

\displaystyle\hat{\sigma}_{1,2,i}-s_{1,2,i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\hat{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\hat{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}-T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}]

(S396)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}])

\displaystyle-T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})-T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})

\displaystyle+(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})^{\top}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}\right)(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})

It follows that

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{1,2,i}-s_{1,2,i})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}])\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+K(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+K(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+K(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left((\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})^{\top}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})\right)^{2}.

By Assumptions
1
(i) and
4
(ii), Lemma
S36
and Lemma
S42
, the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and Markov’s inequality,

\displaystyle(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left((\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})^{\top}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})\right)^{2}

(S398)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K||\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}||^{2}||\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast}||^{2}(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}||\ddot{x}_{i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}||^{2}=O_{p}(n^{-4}).

Similarly,

(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})\right)^{2}\leq\frac{||\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}||^{2}}{nT}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}||\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}||^{2}=O_{p}(n^{-2})

(S399)

and

(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})\right)^{2}\leq\frac{||\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}||^{2}}{nT}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\left\|\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}(n^{-2}).

(S400)

Next, using the definitions of
\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}
and
\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{t\leq T}\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2})(\varepsilon_{1,i,t}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t})

(S401)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}-T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2})

\displaystyle-\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}+T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}),

and hence

\displaystyle(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}])\right)^{2}

(S402)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}])\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+K(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+K(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+K(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2})\right)^{2}.

By the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2})\right)^{2}

(S403)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}=O_{p}(n^{-1}),

where the first equality follows from (
S437
), and the last from
Lemma
S40
(iii), (
S322
), Hölder’s inequality
and Markov’s inequality. Similarly,

\displaystyle(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}\right)^{2}

(S404)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}=O_{p}(G_{1}n^{-1})

and

\displaystyle(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2})\right)^{2}

(S405)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}\leq O_{p}(G_{1}n^{-2}).

Finally, by Assumption
1
, Lemma
S34
(ii) and Lemma
S40
(iii),

(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}])\right)^{2}=O_{p}(G_{1}n^{-2}).

(S406)

Combining (
S402
)-(
S406
) yields

(nT^{2})^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}])\right)^{2}=O_{p}\left(G_{1}n^{-1}\right).

(S407)

The claim of the lemma follows from (
)-(
S400
), and (
S407
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S32
.

Suppose Assumptions
1
,
4
and
5
hold. Then
\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}-s_{U,n,T}^{2}=O_{p}(n^{-1}(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1})^{1/2})
, where

\displaystyle s_{U,n,T}^{2}

\displaystyle\equiv(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}s_{2,i}^{4}+(2n)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-2}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}s_{1,i}^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+(2n^{3})^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\leq n}s_{2,i}^{2}\right)^{2}-n^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}s_{1,2,i}\right)^{2}.

Proof of Lemma
S32
. From the definitions
of
\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}
and
\sigma_{U,n,T}^{2}
, we can write

\displaystyle\hat{\sigma}_{U,n,T}^{2}-s_{U,n,T}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{4}-s_{2,i}^{4})+(2n)^{-1}\left(\left(n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}\right)^{2}-\left(n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}s_{2,i}^{2}\right)^{2}\right)

(S408)

\displaystyle+(2n)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\left(\left(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}\right)^{2}-\left(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}s_{1,i}^{2}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+n^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{1,2,i}\right)^{2}-\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}s_{1,2,i}\right)^{2}\right).

From Lemma
S34
(ii),
s_{2,i}^{4}\leq K
. Hence, by Lemma
S39
and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{4}-s_{2,i}^{4})\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\left((nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}-s_{2,i}^{2})^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left((nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}s_{2,i}^{4}\right)^{1/2}

(S409)

\displaystyle+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}-s_{2,i}^{2})^{2}\overset{}{=}O_{p}((nT^{2})^{-1/2}).

Similarly,

\displaystyle\left|n^{-1}\left(\left(n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}\right)^{2}-\left(n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}s_{2,i}^{2}\right)^{2}\right)\right|

(S410)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-3}\left|\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}-s_{2,i}^{2})\times\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}+s_{2,i}^{2})\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n^{-1}\left(n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}-s_{2,i}^{2})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+2n^{-3}\left|\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}-s_{2,i}^{2})\times\sum_{i\leq n}s_{2,i}^{2}\right|\overset{}{=}O_{p}(n^{-2}).

Next, by the triangle inequality, the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, and Lemma
S39
,

\displaystyle\left|n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\left(\left(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}\right)^{2}-\left(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}s_{1,i}^{2}\right)^{2}\right)\right|

(S411)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2n^{-1}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\left(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}-s_{1,i}^{2})\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\left(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}s_{1,i}^{2}\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\left(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}-s_{1,i}^{2})\right)^{2}\overset{}{=}O_{p}\left(n^{-1}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\right)^{1/2}\right)

Finally, by Lemma
S31
,

\displaystyle\left|n^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\hat{\sigma}_{1,2,i}\right)^{2}-\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}s_{1,2,i}\right)^{2}\right)\right|

(S412)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2n^{-1}\left(n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{1,2,i}-s_{1,2,i})\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(n^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}s_{1,2,i}\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+n^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{1,2,i}-s_{1,2,i})\right)^{2}\overset{}{=}O_{p}(G_{1}^{1/2}n^{-3/2}).

Since
\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\geq G_{1}n^{-1}
, the claim of
the lemma follows from (
S408
)-(
S412
).
Q.E.D.

E.2
Properties of Estimators in the Two Way Linear Model

Throughout this section, we let
x_{j,i,t}
,
\bar{x}_{j,i}
,
\bar{x}_{j,t}

and
\bar{x}_{j}
denote the
j
th entries of
x_{i,t}
,
\bar{x}_{i}
,
\bar{x}_{t}
and
\bar{x}
for
j=1,\ldots,d_{x}
respectively.

Lemma S33
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
4
(ii), we have:

(i)
(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\left(x_{i,t}x_{i,t}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}[x_{i,t}x_{i,t}^{\top}]\right)=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2})
;

(ii)
n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\bar{x}_{i}\bar{x}_{i}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{i}\right]\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i}^{\top}])=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2})
;

(iii)
T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\bar{x}_{t}\bar{x}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{t}\right]\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{t}^{\top}])=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2})
;

(iv)
\bar{x}\bar{x}^{\top}=\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}\right]\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{t}^{\top}]+O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2})
.

Proof of Lemma
S33
. (i) Given Assumptions
1
(ii, iii, iv),
4
(ii) and Corollary A.2. in
26
,
we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\left(x_{j,i,t}x_{j^{\prime},i,t}-\mathbb{E}[x_{j,i,t}x_{j^{\prime},i,t}]\right)\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\left(x_{j,i,t}x_{j^{\prime},i,t}-\mathbb{E}[x_{j,i,t}x_{j^{\prime},i,t}]\right)\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\leq n}T^{-1}\sum_{t_{1},t_{2}\leq T}\mathbb{E}\left[(x_{j,i,t_{1}}x_{j^{\prime},i,t_{1}}-\mathbb{E}[x_{j,i,t_{1}}x_{j^{\prime},i,t_{1}}])(x_{j,i,t_{2}}x_{j^{\prime},i,t_{2}}-\mathbb{E}[x_{j,i,t_{2}}x_{j^{\prime},i,t_{2}}])\right]

\displaystyle\leq K\max_{i\leq n,t\leq T}||x_{j,i,t}x_{j^{\prime},i,t}||_{2+\delta/2}^{2}\sum_{h=0}^{\infty}\left|\alpha_{i}(h)\right|^{\delta/(4+\delta)}\leq K\max_{i\leq n,t\leq T}||x_{j,i,t}||_{4+\delta}^{2}||x_{j^{\prime},i,t}||_{4+\delta}^{2}\leq K,

(S413)

for any
j,j^{\prime}=1,\ldots,d_{x}
, which along with Markov’s
inequality, establishes the given claim.

(ii) We begin by observing that

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\bar{x}_{i}\bar{x}_{i}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{i}\right]\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i}^{\top}])

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\bar{x}_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{i}\right])(\bar{x}_{i}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i}^{\top}])

\displaystyle\text{ \ }+n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{i}\right](\bar{x}_{i}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i}^{\top}])+(\bar{x}_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{i}\right])\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i}^{\top}]\right).

(S414)

Relying on Assumptions
1
(ii, iv),
4
(ii) and Rosenthal’s
inequality of strong mixing processes (e.g., (
S568
) in the proof of
Lemma
S47
), we can proceed:

\displaystyle\max_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[(\bar{x}_{j,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}])^{4}]

\displaystyle\leq K\max_{i\leq n}\max\left\{\left(T^{-2}\sum_{t\leq T}\left\|x_{j,i,t}-\mathbb{E}[x_{j,i,t}]\right\|_{2+\delta}^{2}\right)^{2},\text{ }T^{-4}\sum_{t\leq T}\left\|x_{j,i,t}-\mathbb{E}[x_{j,i,t}]\right\|_{4+\delta}^{4}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq K\left(T^{-2}\max_{i\leq n,t\leq T}\left\|x_{j,i,t}\right\|_{2+\delta}^{4}+T^{-3}\max_{i\leq n,t\leq T}\left\|x_{j,i,t}\right\|_{4+\delta}^{4}\right)\leq KT^{-2}.

(S415)

Hence by the triangle inequality and Hölder’s inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\bar{x}_{j,i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{j,i}\right])(\bar{x}_{j^{\prime}i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j^{\prime}i}])\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(\bar{x}_{j,i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{j,i}\right])(\bar{x}_{j^{\prime}i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j^{\prime}i}])\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\leq n}(\mathbb{E}\left[(\bar{x}_{j,i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{j,i}\right])^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}[(\bar{x}_{j^{\prime}i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j^{\prime}i}])^{2}])^{1/2}\leq KT^{-1},

which together with Markov’s inequality implies that the first term in the
RHS of (
S414
) satisfies

n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\bar{x}_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{i}\right])(\bar{x}_{i}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i}^{\top}])=O_{p}(T^{-1}).

(S416)

We next analyze the term in the second line of (
S414
). Since by
Assumption
4
(ii) and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\max_{i\leq n,t\leq T}\left(||\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i}]||^{2}+||\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{t}]||^{2}+||\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}]||^{2}\right)\leq 3\max_{i\leq n,t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[||x_{i,t}||^{2}]\leq K,

(S417)

we can employ Assumption
1
(iii) and (
S415
) to show that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}](\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},i}])\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=n^{-2}\sum_{i\leq n}(\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}])^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[(\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},i}])^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\max_{i\leq n}\left((\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}])^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[(\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},i}])^{2}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq Kn^{-1}\max_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[(\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},i}])^{2}\right]\leq K(nT)^{-1}.

Hence by Markov’s inequality,

n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{i}\right](\bar{x}_{i}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i}^{\top}])+(\bar{x}_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{i}\right])\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i}^{\top}]\right)=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2}).

(S418)

Combining the results in (
S414
), (
S416
) and (
S418
), and applying Assumption
1
(i) establishes the lemma
claim in this part.

(iii) Let
x_{i,t}^{\ast}\equiv x_{i,t}-\mathbb{E}\left[x_{i,t}\right]

and
x_{j,i,t}^{\ast}
denote the
j
th entry of
x_{i,t}^{\ast}
. By the
definition of
\bar{x}_{t}
, we can express

(\bar{x}_{j,t}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{j,t}\right])(\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},t}])=n^{-2}\sum_{i\leq n}x_{j,i,t}^{\ast}x_{j^{\prime},i,t}^{\ast}+n^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}(x_{j,i_{1}t}^{\ast}x_{j^{\prime},i_{2}t}^{\ast}+x_{j^{\prime},i_{1}t}^{\ast}x_{j,i_{2}t}^{\ast}).

(S419)

Therefore

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\bar{x}_{j,t}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{j,t}\right])(\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},t}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},t}\right])

\displaystyle=(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}X_{j,i}^{\ast\top}X_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast}+(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}(X_{j,i_{1}}^{\ast\top}X_{j^{\prime},i_{2}}^{\ast}+X_{j^{\prime},i_{1}}^{\ast\top}X_{j,i_{2}}^{\ast}),

(S420)

where
X_{j,i}^{\ast}\equiv(x_{j,i,t}^{\ast})_{t\leq T}
for
j=1,\ldots,d_{x}
. By Assumption
4
(ii), the triangle inequality and Hölder’s inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}X_{j,i}^{\ast\top}X_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[|X_{j,i}^{\ast\top}X_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast}|\right]

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\max_{i\leq n,t\leq T}(\mathbb{E}\left[x_{j,i,t}^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}[x_{j^{\prime},i,t}^{2}])^{1/2}\leq Kn^{-1}.

Hence by Markov’s inequality,

(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}X_{j,i}^{\ast\top}X_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast}=O_{p}(n^{-1}).

(S421)

We next turn to the second term in the RHS of (
S420
). By
Assumption
1
(iii),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}X_{j,i_{1}}^{\ast\top}X_{j^{\prime},i_{2}}^{\ast}\right|^{2}\right]=\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i_{1}}^{\ast\top}\mathrm{Cov}(X_{j^{\prime},i_{2}}^{\ast})X_{j,i_{1}}^{\ast}]

\displaystyle\leq Kn^{2}\left(\max_{i\leq n}\lambda_{\max}(\mathrm{Cov}(X_{j^{\prime},i}^{\ast}))\right)\left(\max_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|X_{j,i}^{\ast}\right\|^{2}\right]\right),

(S422)

where

\max_{j\leq d_{x}}\max_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|X_{j,i}^{\ast}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\max_{j\leq d_{x}}\max_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[x_{j,i,t}^{2}]\leq KT

(S423)

by Assumption
4
(ii). By Assumptions
1
(ii), (iv), and
4
(ii), and Corollary A.2. in
26
,

\max_{i\leq n}\max_{t\leq T}\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{T}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[x_{j,i,t}(x_{j,i,t^{\prime}}-\mathbb{E}[x_{j,i,t^{\prime}}])\right]\right|\leq\max_{i\leq n}\max_{t\leq T}||x_{j,i,t}||_{2+\delta/2}^{2}\sum_{h=0}^{\infty}\left|\alpha_{i}(h)\right|^{\delta/(4+\delta)}\leq K.

Hence by Geršgorin’s disc theorem

\max_{j\leq d_{x}}\max_{i\leq n}\lambda_{\max}(\mathrm{Cov}(X_{j,i}^{\ast}))\leq K,

(S424)

which along with (
S422
) and (
S423
), and Markov’s
inequality shows that for any
j,j^{\prime}=1,\ldots,d_{x}

(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}X_{j,i_{1}}^{\ast\top}X_{j^{\prime},i_{2}}^{\ast}=O_{p}((n^{2}T)^{-1/2}).

(S425)

Combining the results in (
S420
), (
S421
) and (
S425
) yields

T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\bar{x}_{j,t}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{j,t}\right])(\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},t}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},t}\right])=O_{p}(n^{-1}).

(S426)

Next note that

T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{j,t}\right](\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},t}])=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{j,t}\right]x_{j^{\prime},i,t}^{\ast}.

(S427)

By Assumptions
1
(ii, iii, iv) and
4
(ii), (
S417
)
and Corollary A.2. in
26
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{j,t}\right]x_{j^{\prime},i,t}^{\ast}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{j,t}\right]x_{j^{\prime},i,t}^{\ast}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq n\max_{i\leq n}\sum_{t_{1},t_{2}=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{j,t_{1}}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{j,t_{2}}\right]\mathbb{E}[x_{j^{\prime},i,t_{1}}^{\ast}x_{j^{\prime},i,t_{2}}^{\ast}]

\displaystyle\leq n\max_{j\leq d_{x}}\max_{t\leq T}(\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{j,t}\right])^{2}\sum_{t_{1},t_{2}=1}^{T}\left|\mathbb{E}[x_{j^{\prime},i,t_{1}}^{\ast}x_{j^{\prime},i,t_{2}}^{\ast}]\right|

\displaystyle\leq KnT\max_{j\leq d_{x}}\max_{i\leq n,t\leq T}||x_{j,i,t}||_{2+\delta/2}^{2}\sum_{h=0}^{\infty}\left|\alpha_{i}(h)\right|^{\delta/(4+\delta)}\leq KnT,

(S428)

which together with (
S427
) and Markov’s inequality implies that

T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{j,t}\right](\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},t}])=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2}).

(S429)

Since

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\bar{x}_{t}\bar{x}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{t}\right]\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{t}^{\top}])

\displaystyle=T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{t}\right](\bar{x}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{t}^{\top}])+T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\bar{x}_{t}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{t}\right])\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{t}^{\top}]

\displaystyle+T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\bar{x}_{t}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{t}\right])(\bar{x}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{t}^{\top}]),

the claim of the lemma in this part follows from Assumption
1
(i), (
S426
) and (
S429
).

(iv) By the similar arguments as in the proofs of (
S413
) and (
S418
), we obtain

\mathrm{Var}(\bar{x}_{j})=O((nT)^{-1/2})\text{ \ \ and \ \ }\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j}]\left(\bar{x}_{j}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j}]\right)=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2})

(S430)

for any
j=1,\ldots,d_{x}
. Therefore, using (
S430
), Hölder’s inequality and Markov’s inequality,

\displaystyle\bar{x}\bar{x}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}\right]\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}^{\top}]

\displaystyle=(\bar{x}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}\right])(\bar{x}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}])^{\top}+\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}\right](\bar{x}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}])^{\top}

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ \ }+(\bar{x}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}\right])\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}^{\top}]\overset{}{=}O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2}),

which establishes the claim of the lemma in this part.
Q.E.D.

Lemma S34
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
4
(ii), we
have:

(i)
\left\|\theta_{2}^{\ast}\right\|+\max_{i\leq n}|\gamma_{2,i}^{\ast}|+\max_{t\leq T}|\gamma_{2,t}^{\ast}|\leq K
;

(ii)
\max_{i\leq n,t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{8}]\leq K
.

Proof of Lemma
S34
. (i) By the definition of
\theta^{\ast}
and Assumption
4
,

\displaystyle||\theta_{2}^{\ast}||^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[y_{i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\ast\top}]\right)\left(\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\ast}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\ast\top}]\right)^{-2}\left(\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\ast}y_{i,t}]\right)

\displaystyle\leq\lambda_{\max}\left(\left((nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\ast}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\ast\top}]\right)^{-1}\right)(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[y_{i,t}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq K\max_{i\leq n,t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[y_{i,t}^{2}]\leq K.

(S431)

By Assumption
4
(ii) and the triangle inequality, we have

\max_{i\leq n,t\leq T}\left(|\mathbb{E}[\bar{y}_{i}]|^{2}+|\mathbb{E}[\bar{y}_{t}]|^{2}+|\mathbb{E}[\bar{y}]|^{2}\right)\leq 3\max_{i\leq n,t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[y_{i,t}^{2}]\leq K.

(S432)

Combining the results in (
S417
), (
S431
), (
S432
) yields

\max_{i\leq n}\left|\gamma_{2,i}^{\ast}\right|\leq\max_{i\leq n}\left|\mathbb{E}[\bar{y}_{i}]\right|+||\theta_{2}^{\ast}||\max_{i\leq n}\left\|\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i}]\right\|\leq K

(S433)

and

\max_{t\leq T}\left|\gamma_{2,t}^{\ast}\right|\leq\max_{t\leq T}\left|\mathbb{E}[\bar{y}_{t}-\bar{y}]\right|+||\theta_{2}^{\ast}||\max_{i\leq n}\left\|\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{t}-\bar{x}]\right\|\leq K.

(S434)

This completes the proof of part (i) of the lemma.

(ii) By Assumption
4
(ii), (
S431
), (
S433
), (
S434
), the triangle inequality, the
c_{r}
-inequality
and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality

\max_{i\leq n,t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{8}]\leq K\max_{i\leq n,t\leq T}\mathbb{E}\left[|y_{i,t}|^{8}+||x_{i,t}||^{8}||\theta^{\ast}||^{8}+\left|\gamma_{1,i}^{\ast}\right|^{8}+\left|\gamma_{1,t}^{\ast}\right|^{8}\right]\leq K.

(S435)

This completes the proof of part (ii) of the lemma.
Q.E.D.

Lemma S35
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
4
, we have

(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}=(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}-\mathbb{E}[(\bar{x}_{i}+\bar{x}_{t})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}])+o_{p}(1)=O_{p}(1),

(S436)

where
\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\equiv y_{i,t}-x_{i,t}^{\top}\theta_{2}^{\ast}-\gamma_{2,i}^{\ast}-\gamma_{2,t}^{\ast}
.

Proof of Lemma
S35
.
We begin by providing some
axillary results which are useful for the proof. First by the first-order
conditions of
\theta^{\ast}
,
\gamma_{1,i}^{\ast}
and
\gamma_{2,t}^{\ast}
, we obtain

\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}]=0\text{ \ \ \ \ and \ \ \ \ }\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}]=\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}]=0.

(S437)

Moreover in view of Lemma
S34
(i), Assumptions
1
(ii),
(iii), (iv) apply to
\left(x_{i,t}^{\top},\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\right)
.

We next prove (
S436
). By the definition of
\ddot{x}_{i,t}
and (
S437
), we can express:

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}x_{i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}-n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\bar{x}_{i}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}-T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{x}_{t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}+\bar{x}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}.

(S438)

Starting with the first term in the RHS of the equality in (
S438
), we can use similar arguments as in (
S413
) to show that

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(x_{j,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}-\mathbb{E}[x_{j,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}])\right|^{2}\right]\leq K\max_{i\leq n,t\leq T}||x_{j,i,t}||_{{}^{4+\delta}}^{2}||\varepsilon_{2,i,t}||_{{}^{4+\delta}}^{2}\leq K,

(S439)

where the last inequality follows from Assumption
4
(ii) and Lemma
S34
(ii). Therefore, by (
S437
) and Markov’s inequality,

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}x_{i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(x_{i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}-\mathbb{E}[x_{i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}])=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2}).

(S440)

To analyze the second term in the RHS of the equality in (
S438
), we first express:

n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\bar{x}_{i}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}=n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\left((\bar{x}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i}])\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}]\right)+n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i}]\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}+n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}].

(S441)

We next study the three terms in the RHS of the equation in (
S441
). Since
\mathbb{E}[(\bar{x}_{j,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}])\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}]=\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}]
in view of (
S437
), by Assumption
1
(i) we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\left((\bar{x}_{j,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}])\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}]\right)\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\max_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[((\bar{x}_{j,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}])\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i})^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\max_{i\leq n}\left(\mathbb{E}[(\bar{x}_{j,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}])^{4}]\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{4}]\right)^{1/2}.

(S442)

Using similar arguments as in deriving (
S415
), we can show that

\max_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{4}\right]=\max_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}])^{4}\right]\leq KT^{-2},

(S443)

which together with (
S415
), (
S442
) and Markov’s
inequality implies that

n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\left((\bar{x}_{j,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}])\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}]\right)=O_{p}((nT^{2})^{-1/2}).

(S444)

For the second and the third terms in the RHS of equation in (
S441
), we can use Assumption
1
(iii), (
S417
), (
S437
) and (
S443
) to show that

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}]\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}\right|^{2}\right]=n^{-2}\sum_{i\leq n}(\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}])^{2}\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{2}]\leq K(nT)^{-1},

(S445)

and use Hölder’s inequality, (
S415
), (
S437
)
and (
S443
) to obtain

\max_{i\leq n}\left|\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}]\right|=\max_{i\leq n}\left|\mathbb{E}[(\bar{x}_{j,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}])\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}]\right|\leq\max_{i\leq n}\left(\mathbb{E}[(\bar{x}_{j,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}])^{2}]\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{2}]\right)^{1/2}\leq K(nT)^{-1}.

(S446)

Combining the results in (
S441
), (
S444
)-(
S446
), and applying Markov’s inequality yields

n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\bar{x}_{i}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}=n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i}]\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}+n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}]+O_{p}((nT^{2})^{-1/2})=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2}).

(S447)

We next turn to the third term in the RHS of the equality in (
S438
). By (
S437
), and the definitions of
\bar{x}_{t}

and
\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}
, we can express:
