\bar{x}_{t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}=\bar{x}_{t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}])=n^{-2}\sum_{i\leq n}x_{i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}+n^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}(x_{i_{1}t}\varepsilon_{2,i_{2},t}^{\ast}+\varepsilon_{2,i_{1}t}^{\ast}x_{i_{2},t}),

where
\varepsilon_{i,t}^{\ast}\equiv\varepsilon_{i,t}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{i,t}]
. Therefore,

n^{2}\sum_{t\leq T}(\bar{x}_{t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}])=\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(x_{i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}-\mathbb{E}[x_{i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}])+\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(x_{i_{1},t}\varepsilon_{2,i_{2}t}^{\ast}+\varepsilon_{2,i_{1}t}^{\ast}x_{i_{2},t}).

(S448)

By employing analogous arguments as demonstrated in the proof of (
S413
), we can establish that

(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(x_{j,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}-\mathbb{E}[x_{j,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}])=O_{p}((n^{3}T)^{-1/2}).

(S449)

Let
e_{i}\equiv(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast})_{t\leq T}
and
X_{j,i}\equiv(x_{j,i,t})_{t\leq T}
. Then

\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}\sum_{t\leq T}x_{j,i_{1}t}\varepsilon_{2,i_{2},t}^{\ast}=\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}(X_{j,i_{1}}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i_{1}}^{\top}])e_{i_{2}}+\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i_{1}}^{\top}]e_{i_{2}}.

(S450)

By Assumption
1
(iii), (
S424
) and Lemma
S34
(ii),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}(X_{j,i_{1}}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i_{1}}^{\top}])e_{i_{2}}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=(n^{2}T)^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}\mathbb{E}[e_{i_{2}}^{\top}\mathrm{Cov}(X_{j,i_{1}})e_{i_{2}}]

\displaystyle\leq(nT)^{-2}\max_{j\leq d_{x}}\max_{i\leq n}\lambda(\mathrm{Cov}(X_{j,i}))\max_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\left\|e_{i}]\right\|^{2}]\leq K(n^{2}T)^{-1}.

(S451)

Consider the expression for the second term on the RHS of the equation in (
S450
):

\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i_{1}}^{\top}]e_{i_{2}}=\sum_{i=1}^{n-1}\left(\sum_{i^{\prime}=i+1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i^{\prime}}^{\top}]\right)e_{i}.

(S452)

Using arguments similar to those employed in deriving (
S424
) we
deduce

\max_{i\leq n}\lambda(\mathrm{Cov}(e_{i}))\leq K.

(S453)

Therefore by Assumptions
1
(iii) and
4
(ii), (
S452
) and (
S453
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i_{1}}^{\top}]e_{i_{2}}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n-1}\left(\sum_{i^{\prime}=i+1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i^{\prime}}^{\top}]\right)\mathrm{Cov}(e_{i})\left(\sum_{i^{\prime}=i+1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i^{\prime}}]\right)

\displaystyle\leq K\sum_{i=1}^{n-1}\sum_{i_{1}^{\prime},i_{2}^{\prime}=i+1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i_{1}^{\prime}}^{\top}]\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i_{2}^{\prime}}]

\displaystyle\leq K\sum_{i=1}^{n-1}\left(\sum_{i^{\prime}=i+1}^{n}||\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i^{\prime}}]||\right)^{2}\leq Kn^{3}T,

(S454)

where the last inequality is by
||\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i^{\prime}}]||^{2}\leq\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[x_{j,i^{\prime},t}^{2}]\leq KT
. Combining the
results from (
S450
), (
S451
) and (
S454
), and applying Markov’s inequality yields:

\displaystyle(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}\sum_{t\leq T}x_{j,i_{1},t}\varepsilon_{2,i_{2},t}^{\ast}

\displaystyle=(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n-1}\left(\sum_{i^{\prime}=i+1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i^{\prime}}^{\top}]\right)e_{i}

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ \ \ }+O_{p}((n^{2}T)^{-1/2})\overset{}{=}O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2}).

(S455)

Next consider the following expression:

\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i_{1},t}^{\ast}x_{i_{2},t}=\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}e_{i_{1}}^{\top}(X_{j,i_{2}}-\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i_{2}}])+\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}e_{i_{1}}^{\top}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i_{2}}].

(S456)

Using the same arguments as in the proof of (
S451
), we can
establish:

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}e_{i}^{\top}(X_{j,i_{2}}-\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i_{2}}])\right|^{2}\right]\leq K(n^{2}T)^{-1}.

(S457)

By employing arguments similar to the proof of (
S454
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i_{2}}^{\top}]e_{i_{1}}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=(n^{2}T)^{-2}\sum_{i=2}^{n}\left(\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{i-1}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i^{\prime}}^{\top}]\right)\mathrm{Cov}(e_{i})\left(\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{i-1}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i^{\prime}}]\right)

\displaystyle\leq K(n^{2}T)^{-2}\sum_{i=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{1}^{\prime},i_{2}^{\prime}=1}^{i-1}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i_{1}^{\prime}}^{\top}]\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i_{2}^{\prime}}]

\displaystyle\leq K\sum_{i=2}^{n}\left(\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{i-1}||\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i^{\prime}}]||\right)^{2}\leq K(nT)^{-1},

(S458)

which along with (
S456
), (
S457
) and (
S458
), and Markov’s inequality implies that

(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i_{1},t}^{\ast}x_{i_{2},t}=(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i=2}^{n}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{i-1}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i^{\prime}}^{\top}]e_{i}+O_{p}((n^{2}T)^{-1/2})=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2}).

(S459)

By Assumptions
1
(iii) and
4
(ii), and (
S453
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i}^{\top}]e_{i}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=(n^{2}T)^{-2}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i}^{\top}]\mathrm{Cov}(e_{i})\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i}]

\displaystyle\leq(n^{2}T)^{-2}\max_{i\leq n}\lambda(\mathrm{Cov}(e_{i}))\sum_{i\leq n}\left\|\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i}]\right\|^{2}\leq K(n^{3}T)^{-1},

which together with Markov’s inequality implies that

(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i}^{\top}]e_{i}=O_{p}((n^{3}T)^{-1/2}).

(S460)

Combining (
S448
), (
S449
), (
S455
), (
S459
) and (
S460
) yields

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\bar{x}_{j,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}])

\displaystyle=(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n-1}\left(\sum_{i^{\prime}=i+1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i^{\prime}}^{\top}]\right)e_{i}

\displaystyle\text{ \ }+(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i=2}^{n}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{i-1}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i^{\prime}}^{\top}]e_{i}+O_{p}((n^{2}T)^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i^{\prime}}^{\top}]e_{i}+O_{p}((n^{2}T)^{-1/2})=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2}).

(S461)

By Assumptions
1
(i, iii) and
4
(ii), (
S437
) and Hölder’s inequality,

\displaystyle\max_{t\leq T}\left|\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}]\right|

\displaystyle=\max_{t\leq T}\left|\mathbb{E}[(\bar{x}_{j,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,t}])\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}]\right|\leq\max_{i\leq n}\left(\mathbb{E}[(\bar{x}_{j,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,t}])^{2}]\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{2}]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\max_{i\leq n,t\leq T}\left(\mathbb{E}[x_{j,i,t}^{2}]\right)^{1/2}\max_{i\leq n,t\leq T}\left(\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{i,t}^{2}]\right)^{1/2}\leq K(nT)^{-1/2}.

(S462)

Collecting the results in (
S460
), (
S461
) and (
S462
) yields

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{x}_{j,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}

\displaystyle=T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}]+(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i^{\prime}}^{\top}]e_{i}+O_{p}((n^{2}T)^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}]+T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,t}]\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}+O_{p}((n^{2}T)^{-1/2})=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2}).

(S463)

We next turn to the last term in the RHS of the equality in (
S438
). Since
\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}]=0
by (
S437
), we can express

\displaystyle(\bar{x}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}])\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}

\displaystyle=(\bar{x}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}])(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}])=n^{-2}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}=1}^{n}(\bar{x}_{i_{1}}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i_{1}}])(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i_{2}}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i_{2}}])

\displaystyle=n^{-2}\sum_{i\leq n}(\bar{x}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i}])(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}])+n^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}(\bar{x}_{i_{1}}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i_{1}}])(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i_{2}}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i_{2}}])

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ \ }+n^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i_{1}}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i_{1}}])(\bar{x}_{i_{2}}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i_{2}}]).

(S464)

By the triangle inequality and Hölder’s inequality, we can show that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|n^{-2}\sum_{i\leq n}(\bar{x}_{j,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}])(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}])\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq n^{-2}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(\bar{x}_{j,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}])(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}])\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\max_{i\leq n}(\mathbb{E}\left[(\bar{x}_{j,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}])^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}])^{2}\right])^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq K(nT)^{-1},

(S465)

where the last inequality is by (
S415
) and (
S443
). Similarly, we can show that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|n^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}(\bar{x}_{j,i_{1}}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i_{1}}])(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i_{2}}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i_{2}}])\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=n^{-4}\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(\bar{x}_{j,i_{1}}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i_{1}}])(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i_{2}}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i_{2}}])\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq n^{-2}\left(\max_{i\leq n}(\mathbb{E}[\left|\bar{x}_{j,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i}]\right|^{4})^{1/2}\max_{i\leq n}(\mathbb{E}[\left|\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}]\right|^{4}])^{1/2}\right)\leq K(nT)^{-2}

(S466)

and

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|n^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}=2}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{i_{1}-1}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i_{1}}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i_{1}}])(\bar{x}_{j,i_{2}}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,i_{2}}])\right|^{2}\right]\leq K(nT)^{-2}.

(S467)

From (
S464
)-(
S467
) and Markov’s inequality, we
deduce that

(\bar{x}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}])\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}=O_{p}((nT)^{-1}).

(S468)

Since
\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}=n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}])
by (
S437
), using
Assumption
1
(iii) we get

\mathbb{E}[\left|\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}\right|^{2}]=n^{-2}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}]\right|^{2}\right]\leq n^{-1}\max_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}]\right|^{2}\right]\leq K(nT)^{-1},

(S469)

where the second inequality is by (
S443
) and Hölder’s
inequality. Therefore, by (
S417
), (
S469
) and
Markov’s inequality, we get

\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}]\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2}).

This, combined with (
S468
) shows that

\bar{x}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}=n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}]\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}+O_{p}((nT)^{-1})=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2}).

(S470)

The claim of the lemma now follows from Assumption
1
(i), (
S438
), (
S440
), (
S447
), (
S463
) and (
S470
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S36
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
4
, we have:

\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast}=\frac{\Sigma_{\ddot{x}}^{-1}}{nT}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\left(\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}-\mathbb{E}[(\bar{x}_{i}+\bar{x}_{t})\varepsilon_{2,i,t}]\right)+o_{p}((nT)^{-1/2})=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2}),

(S471)

where
\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\equiv y_{i,t}-x_{i,t}^{\top}\theta_{2}^{\ast}-\gamma_{2,i}^{\ast}-\gamma_{2,t}^{\ast}
.

Proof of Lemma
S36
.
By the definitions of
\hat{\theta}_{2}
,
\theta_{2}^{\ast}
and
\varepsilon_{2,i,t}
, we can
express:

\displaystyle\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast}

\displaystyle=\hat{\Sigma}_{\ddot{x}}^{-1}(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{x}_{i,t}y_{i,t}-\theta^{\ast}=\hat{\Sigma}_{\ddot{x}}^{-1}(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}

\displaystyle=\Sigma_{\ddot{x}}^{-1}(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}+(\hat{\Sigma}_{\ddot{x}}^{-1}-\Sigma_{\ddot{x}}^{-1})(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}.

(S472)

By the definition of
\hat{\Sigma}_{\ddot{x}}
and Lemma
S33
,

\displaystyle\hat{\Sigma}_{\ddot{x}}-\Sigma_{\ddot{x}}

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(x_{i,t}x_{i,t}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}[x_{i,t}x_{i,t}^{\top}])-n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\bar{x}_{i}\bar{x}_{i}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{i}\right]\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{i}^{\top}])

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }-T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\bar{x}_{t}\bar{x}_{t}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}_{t}\right]\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{t}^{\top}])+(\bar{x}\bar{x}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{x}\right]\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}^{\top}])\overset{}{=}O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2}).

(S473)

This, combined with Assumption
4
(i), further implies that

\lambda_{\min}(\hat{\Sigma}_{\ddot{x}})\geq K^{-1},\text{ \ wpa1.}

(S474)

Summing up the results in Lemma
S35
, (
S473
) and (
S474
) yields

\left\|\frac{\hat{\Sigma}_{\ddot{x}}^{-1}-\Sigma_{\ddot{x}}^{-1}}{nT}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{||\hat{\Sigma}_{\ddot{x}}-\Sigma_{\ddot{x}}||^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}(\hat{\Sigma}_{\ddot{x}})\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{\ddot{x}})}\left\|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}((nT)^{-2}).

This together with (
S472
) shows that

\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}=\Sigma_{\ddot{x}}^{-1}(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}+O_{p}((nT)^{-1}).

(S475)

The claim of the lemma now follows from Assumption
4
(i), Lemma
S35
and (
S475
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S37
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
4
, we have:

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-(y_{i,t}-x_{i,t}^{\top}\hat{\theta}_{2}-\hat{\gamma}_{2,i,t})^{2}}{2}=\frac{n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast})^{2}+T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast})^{2}}{2}+O_{p}((nT)^{-1})

where
\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\equiv y_{i,t}-x_{i,t}^{\top}\theta_{2}^{\ast}-\gamma_{2,i}^{\ast}-\gamma_{2,t}^{\ast}
,
\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}\equiv T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}
,
\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}\equiv n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}
and
\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{\ast}\equiv\varepsilon_{2,i,t}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}]
.

Proof of Lemma
S37
. First note that
y_{i,t}-x_{i,t}^{\top}\hat{\theta}_{2}-\hat{\gamma}_{2,i,t}=\ddot{y}_{i,t}-\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}\hat{\theta}_{2}=\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}-\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})
, where
\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\equiv\varepsilon_{2,i,t}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}
. Therefore

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(-2)^{-1}\left[(y_{i,t}-x_{i,t}^{\top}\hat{\theta}_{2}-\hat{\gamma}_{2,i,t})^{2}-\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=-(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2})+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }-(2nT)^{-1}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})^{\top}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast}).

(S476)

By Lemma
S35
and Lemma
S36
,

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})=O_{p}((nT)^{-1}).

(S477)

By Assumption
4
(ii) and (
S417
),
\left\|\Sigma_{\ddot{x}}\right\|\leq K
which together with (
S473
)
implies that

\hat{\Sigma}_{\ddot{x}}=O_{p}(1).

(S478)

This along with Lemma
S36
implies that

(2nT)^{-1}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})^{\top}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})=O_{p}((nT)^{-1}).

(S479)

By the definition of
\ddot{\varepsilon}_{i,t}
, we can express

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{2}-T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{2}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{2}.

(S480)

Therefore by (
S469
) and Markov’s inequality

-(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2})=(2n)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{2}+(2T)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{2}+O_{p}(nT)^{-1}.

(S481)

The claim and the lemma now follows from (
S437
), (
S476
), (
S477
), (
S479
) and (
S481
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S38
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
4
,
n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}-s_{2,i}^{2})=O_{p}(n^{-1})
, where
s_{2,i}^{2}\equiv T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}]
.

Proof of Lemma
S38
. Using Lemma
S37
and the definition of
\hat{\sigma}_{\varepsilon_{2},i}^{2}
,
we have

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\hat{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2})

(S482)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast})^{2}+T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast})^{2}+O_{p}((nT)^{-1}).

By Assumption
1
and Lemma
S34
(ii)

\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 4}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast 4}]\leq Kn^{-2}\text{ \ \ and \ \ }\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast 4}]\leq Kn^{-4}.

(S483)

Hence by Assumption
1
(i) and Markov’s inequality,

n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast})^{2}+T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast})^{2}=O_{p}(n^{-1}).

(S484)

From (
S482
) and (
S484
), it follows that

n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}+O_{p}(n^{-1}).

(S485)

Similarly, Assumption
1
, Lemma
S34
(ii), and Markov’s
inequality imply

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}])=O_{p}(n^{-1}).

(S486)

Combining (
S485
), and (
S483
) with the
definition of
s_{\varepsilon_{2},i}^{2}
establishes the claim of the
lemma.
Q.E.D.

Lemma S39
.

Under Assumptions
1
and
4
,
n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}-s_{2,i}^{2})^{2}=O_{p}(n^{-1})
.

Proof of Lemma
S39
. By the definition of
\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}
,

\displaystyle\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\hat{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}=T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}-\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast}))^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-2T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})+(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast})^{\top}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{x}_{i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}\right)(\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast}).

Therefore, by the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}-s_{2,i}^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\hat{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-s_{\varepsilon_{2},i}^{2}\right)^{2}

(S487)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Kn^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-s_{\varepsilon_{2},i}^{2}\right)^{2}+K(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\left\|\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}\right\|^{2}||\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast}||^{2}

\displaystyle+K(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\left\|\ddot{x}_{i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}\right\|^{2}||\hat{\theta}_{2}-\theta_{2}^{\ast}||^{4}.

From Assumptions
1
and
4
(ii), and Lemma
S34
(ii),
it follows that

\mathbb{E}[\left\|\ddot{x}_{i,t}\right\|^{4}]+\mathbb{E}[\left\|\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\right\|^{4}]\leq K.

(S488)

This together with Markov’s inequality implies

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\left\|\ddot{x}_{i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}\right\|^{2}+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\left\|\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}\ddot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}(1).

(S489)

Combining this with Assumption
1
(i), Lemma
S36
, and (
S487
) yields

n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}(\hat{\sigma}_{2,i}^{2}-s_{2,i}^{2})^{2}\leq Kn^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-s_{\varepsilon_{2},i}^{2}\right)^{2}+O_{p}(n^{-2}).

(S490)

Using (
S437
) and the definition of
\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}
, we may write

\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}=\varepsilon_{2,i,t}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast}.

Hence

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-s_{\varepsilon_{2},i}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}])+T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}

\displaystyle-2T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast}).

Therefore,

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\ddot{\varepsilon}_{2,i,t}^{2}-s_{\varepsilon_{2},i}^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Kn^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}])\right)^{2}

(S491)

\displaystyle+Kn^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+Kn^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})\right)^{2}.

From (
S483
) and Markov’s inequality,

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 4}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast 4})=O_{p}(n^{-2})\text{ \ \ and \ \ }\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast 4}=O_{p}(n^{-4}).

(S492)

Together with Assumption
1
(i) and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality,
this implies

n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}\right)^{2}\leq K(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast 4}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast 4}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast 4})=O_{p}(n^{-2}).

(S493)

Similarly,

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})\right)^{2}

(S494)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{4}}\sqrt{(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,i}^{\ast}+\bar{\varepsilon}_{2,t}^{\ast}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{2}^{\ast})^{4}}=O_{p}(n^{-1}).

Finally, by Assumption
1
and Lemma
S34
(ii),

\mathbb{E}\left[n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}])\right)^{2}\right]\leq K(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\max_{t\leq T}\left\|\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}\right\|_{2+\delta}^{2}\leq KT^{-1}.

Thus, by Assumption
1
(i) and Markov’s inequality

n^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{2,i,t}^{2}])\right)^{2}=O_{p}(n^{-1}).

(S495)

The claim of the lemma follows from Assumption
1
(i), (
S490
), (
S491
), (
S493
)-(
S495
).
Q.E.D.

E.3
Properties of Estimators from the Heterogeneous Time Effects
Model

Lemma S40
.

Suppose that Assumptions
1
,
4
(ii) and
5
(i) hold. Then we have

(i)
(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}(\bar{x}_{g,t}x_{g,t}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}]\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}^{\top}])=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2});

(ii)
\left\|\theta_{1}^{\ast}\right\|+\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1},t\leq T}|\gamma_{1,g,t}^{\ast}|\leq K
;

(iii)
\max_{i\leq n,t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{8}]\leq K
.

Proof of Lemma
S40
. (i) By Assumptions
1
(iii) and
4
(ii), we can establish

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1},t\leq T}\left(||\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}]||+|\mathbb{E}[\bar{y}_{g,t}]|\right)\leq K,

(S496)

and

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1},t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[n_{g}(\bar{x}_{j,g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,g,t}])^{2}]=\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1},t\leq T}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\mathbb{E}[x_{j,i,t}^{2}]\leq K.

(S497)

Consider the expression:

\displaystyle\bar{x}_{g,t}x_{g,t}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}]\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}^{\top}]

\displaystyle=(\bar{x}_{g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}])(\bar{x}_{g,t}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}^{\top}])

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}](\bar{x}_{g,t}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}^{\top}])+(\bar{x}_{g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}])\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}^{\top}].

(S498)

By the triangle inequality, Holder’s inequality and (
S497
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}(\bar{x}_{j,g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,g,t}])(\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},g,t}])\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(\bar{x}_{j,g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,g,t}])(\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},g,t}])\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\max_{j\leq d_{x}}\mathbb{E}[n_{g}(\bar{x}_{j,g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,g,t}])^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq G_{1}n^{-1}\max_{j\leq d_{x}}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1},t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[n_{g}(\bar{x}_{j,g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,g,t}])^{2}]\leq Kn^{-1}.

(S499)

By the definition of
\bar{x}_{g,t}
, we can write

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}](\bar{x}_{g,t}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}^{\top}])=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}](x_{i,t}^{\top}-\mathbb{E}[x_{i,t}^{\top}]).

Hence by Assumption
1
(iii) and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,g,t}](\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},g,t}])\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,g,t}](x_{j^{\prime},i,t}-\mathbb{E}[x_{j^{\prime},i,t}])\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(n^{2}T)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i^{\prime}\in I_{g}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[x_{j,i^{\prime}t}](x_{j^{\prime},i,t}-\mathbb{E}[x_{j^{\prime},i,t}])\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(nT)^{-1}\max_{i,i^{\prime}\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[x_{j,i^{\prime}t}](x_{j^{\prime},i,t}-\mathbb{E}[x_{j^{\prime},i,t}])\right|^{2}\right].

(S500)

By Assumptions
1
(ii, iv) and
4
(ii), and Corollary A.2. in
26
, we obtain

\max_{i,i^{\prime}\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|T^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[x_{j,i^{\prime}t}](x_{j^{\prime},i,t}-\mathbb{E}[x_{j^{\prime},i,t}])\right|^{2}\right]\leq K

which along with (
S500
) shows that

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,g,t}](\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j^{\prime},g,t}])\right|^{2}\right]\leq K(nT)^{-1}

(S501)

for any
j,j^{\prime}=1,\ldots,d_{x}
. The claim of the lemma in this part
now follows from (
S498
), (
S499
), (
S501
),
Assumption
1
(i) and Markov’s inequality.

(ii) By Assumptions
4
(ii) and
5
(i), we can use similar
arguments in (
S431
) to show that
\left\|\theta_{1}^{\ast}\right\|\leq K
. This together with (
S496
) further implies
that
\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1},t\leq T}|\gamma_{1,g,t}^{\ast}|\leq K
.

(iii) This part can be proved using similar arguments in the proof of Lemma
S34
, and its proof is hence omitted.
Q.E.D.

Lemma S41
.

Suppose that Assumptions
1
,
4
(ii) and
5
(i) hold. Then we have

(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\dot{x}_{i,t}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}=(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\left(\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}]\right)+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}),

(S502)

where

(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\left(\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}]\right)=O_{p}(1).

(S503)

Proof of Lemma
S41
. By the first-order
conditions of
\theta_{1}^{\ast}\
and
\gamma_{1,g,t}^{\ast}
, we have

\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}]=0\text{ \ \ \ \ and \ \ \ \ }\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}]=0

(S504)

where
\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}\equiv n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}
. Moreover in view of Lemma
S40
(ii), Assumptions
1
(ii, iii, iv) apply to
\left(x_{i,t}^{\top},\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\right)
.

Let
x_{i,t}^{\ast}\equiv x_{i,t}-\mathbb{E}[x_{i,t}]
,
X_{i}^{\ast}\equiv(x_{i,t}^{\ast})_{t\leq T}
and
e_{1,i}\equiv(\varepsilon_{1,i,t})_{t\leq T}
. Then

(\bar{x}_{g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}])\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}=n_{g}^{-2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}x_{i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}+n_{g}^{-2}\sum_{i=N_{g}+2}^{N_{g}+n_{g}}\sum_{i^{\prime}=N_{g}+1}^{i}(x_{i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{1,i^{\prime}t}+x_{i^{\prime}t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}).

Since
\mathbb{E}[(\bar{x}_{g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}])\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}]=\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}]
by (
S504
), and
\mathbb{E}[x_{i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{1,i^{\prime}t}]=0
and
\mathbb{E}[x_{i^{\prime}t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}]=0
for
any
i\neq i^{\prime}
, we can use the above expression to obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}\left[(\bar{x}_{j,g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,g,t}])\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}]\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\frac{X_{j,i}^{\ast\top}e_{1,i}-\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i}^{\ast\top}e_{1,i}]}{n_{g}}+\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i=N_{g}+2}^{N_{g}+n_{g}}\sum_{i^{\prime}=N_{g}+1}^{i}\frac{X_{j,i}^{\ast\top}e_{1,i^{\prime}}+X_{j,i^{\prime}}^{\ast\top}e_{1,i}}{n_{g}}.

(S505)

By Assumptions
1
(ii, iii, iv) and
4
(ii), and Lemma
S40
(iii), we can establish

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\frac{X_{j,i}^{\ast\top}e_{1,i}-\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i}^{\ast\top}e_{1,i}]}{n_{g}}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\left|X_{j,i}^{\ast\top}e_{1,i}-\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i}^{\ast\top}e_{1,i}]\right|^{2}\right]}{n_{g}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq G_{1}(nT)^{-2}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\max_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|X_{j,i}^{\ast\top}e_{1,i}-\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i}^{\ast\top}e_{1,i}]\right|^{2}\right]\leq K(n^{2}T)^{-1}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}

(S506)

where the last inequality is by

\max_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[T^{-1}\left|X_{j,i}^{\ast\top}e_{1,i}-\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i}^{\ast\top}e_{1,i}]\right|^{2}\right]\leq K

(S507)

which can be verified using Assumptions
1
(ii, iv) and
4
(ii),
and Lemma
S40
(iii) and similar arguments in (
S413
).
Therefore by (
S506
) and Markov’s inequality, the first term in
the second line of (
S505
) satisfies

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\frac{X_{j,i}^{\ast\top}e_{1,i}-\mathbb{E}[X_{j,i}^{\ast\top}e_{1,i}]}{n_{g}}=O_{p}((n^{2}T)^{-1/2}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1/2}).

(S508)

We next study the second term in the second line of (
S505
). By
Assumption
1
(iii), Lemma
S40
(iii) and (
S424
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i=N_{g}+2}^{N_{g}+n_{g}}\sum_{i^{\prime}=N_{g}+1}^{i}\frac{X_{j,i}^{\ast\top}e_{1,i^{\prime}}}{n_{g}}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i=N_{g}+2}^{N_{g}+n_{g}}\sum_{i^{\prime}=N_{g}+1}^{i}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[e_{1,i^{\prime}}^{\top}X_{j,i}^{\ast}X_{j,i}^{\ast\top}e_{1,i^{\prime}}\right]}{n_{g}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{j\leq d_{x}}\max_{i\leq n}\lambda_{\max}(\mathrm{Cov}(X_{j,i}^{\ast})(nT)^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i=N_{g}+2}^{N_{g}+n_{g}}\sum_{i^{\prime}=N_{g}+1}^{i}\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[||e_{1,i^{\prime}}||^{2}\right]}{n_{g}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq KG_{1}(nT)^{-2}\max_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[||e_{1,i}||^{2}\right]\leq K(n^{2}T)^{-1}.

(S509)

Therefore by Markov inequality, we have

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i=N_{g}+2}^{N_{g}+n_{g}}\sum_{i^{\prime}=N_{g}+1}^{i}\frac{X_{j,i}^{\ast\top}e_{1,i^{\prime}}}{n_{g}}=O_{p}((n^{2}T)^{-1/2}).

(S510)

Combining (
S505
), (
S508
) and (
S510
)
yields

(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}\left[(\bar{x}_{j,g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,g,t}])\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}]\right]=O_{p}(n^{-1/2}).

(S511)

Since

\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\dot{x}_{i,t}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}=\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}-\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}(\bar{x}_{g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}])\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t},

the claim in (
S502
) follows from (
S511
).

To show (
S503
), we note that the combination of (
S507
) and Markov’s inequality leads to

(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}=O_{p}(1).

(S512)

Next, by Assumptions
1
(iii),
4
(ii) and Lemma
S40
(iii),

\displaystyle\max_{t\leq T}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\left|n_{g}\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,g,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}]\right|

\displaystyle=\max_{t\leq T}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\left|n_{g}\mathbb{E}[(\bar{x}_{j,g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,g,t}])\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{t\leq T}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\left(n_{g}^{2}\mathbb{E}[(\bar{x}_{j,g,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{j,g,t}])^{2}]\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{2}]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{t\leq T}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\left(\mathbb{E}[x_{j,i,t}^{2}]\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}]\right)^{1/2}\leq K,

which together with Assumption
1
(i) implies that

\left|(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}]\right|\leq KG_{1}.

(S513)

Since
G_{1}
is fixed, the claim in (
S503
) follows from (
S512
) and (
S513
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S42
.

Suppose that Assumptions
1
,
4
(ii)
and
5
(i) hold. Then we have

(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})=\frac{\Sigma_{\dot{x}}^{-1}}{nT}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\left(\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\ast}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}-\mathbb{E}[\bar{x}_{g,t}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}]\right)+o_{p}((nT)^{-1/2})=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2}).

Proof of Lemma
S42
. By the definition of
\dot{x}_{i,t}
, we obtain

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\dot{x}_{i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}x_{i,t}x_{i,t}^{\top}-(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}\bar{x}_{g,t}\bar{x}_{i,t}^{\top}.

Hence by Assumption
5
(i), Lemma
S33
(i) and Lemma
S40
(i),

\hat{\Sigma}_{\dot{x}}-\Sigma_{\dot{x}}=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2})=o_{p}(1)

(S514)

which together with Assumption
5
(i) implies that

\lambda_{\min}(\hat{\Sigma}_{\dot{x}})\geq K^{-1},\text{ \ wpa1.}

(S515)

The rest of the proof follows similar arguments in the proof of Lemma
S36
and hence is omitted.
Q.E.D.

Lemma S43
.

Suppose that Assumptions
1
,
4
(ii)
and
5
(i) hold. Then we have

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}-(y_{i,t}-x_{i,t}^{\top}\hat{\theta}_{1}-\hat{\gamma}_{1,i,t})^{2}}{2}=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\frac{n_{g}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 2}}{2}+O_{p}((nT)^{-1})

where
\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast}\equiv n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{\ast}
.

Proof of Lemma
S43
. First, observe that
y_{i,t}-x_{i,t}^{\top}\hat{\theta}_{1}-\hat{\gamma}_{1,i,t}=\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}-\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})
,
where
\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\equiv\varepsilon_{1,i,t}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(-2)^{-1}\left[(y_{i,t}-x_{i,t}^{\top}\hat{\theta}_{1}-\hat{\gamma}_{1,i,t})^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=-(2nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }-(2nT)^{-1}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})^{\top}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\dot{x}_{i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}).

(S516)

Using Lemma
S41
and Lemma
S42
, and similar arguments as
those used to derive (
S477
) and (
S479
), we can
show that

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})=O_{p}((nT)^{-1})

(S517)

and

(2nT)^{-1}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})^{\top}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}\dot{x}_{i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})=O_{p}((nT)^{-1}).

(S518)

Combining (
S516
)-(
S518
) yields

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(-2)^{-1}\left[(y_{i,t}-x_{i,t}^{\top}\hat{\theta}_{1}-\hat{\gamma}_{1,i,t})^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{i\leq n}\sum_{t\leq T}(-2)^{-1}(\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})+O_{p}((nT)^{-1})

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\frac{n_{g}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{2}}{2}+O_{p}((nT)^{-1}),

which together with (
S504
) shows the claim of the lemma.
Q.E.D.

Lemma S44
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
4
(ii) and
5
(i),
\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}-s_{1,i}^{2})=O_{p}(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1})
,
where
s_{1,i}^{2}\equiv T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}]
.

Proof of Lemma
S44
. Using the definition of
\hat{\sigma}_{\varepsilon_{1},i}^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}-T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\hat{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})

(S519)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}(\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})-2T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})

\displaystyle+(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})^{\top}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\dot{x}_{i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}\right)(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}).

From this expression, we may write

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\left(\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}-T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\hat{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})

(S520)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})

\displaystyle-2T^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})

\displaystyle+T^{-1}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})^{\top}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\dot{x}_{i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}).

By Assumptions
1
and
4
(ii) and Lemma
S40
(iii)

\mathbb{E}[||\dot{x}_{i,t}||^{4}]\leq K\text{ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ and \ \ \ \ \ \ \ }\mathbb{E}[||\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}||^{4}]\leq K.

(S521)

Together with Lemma
S42
, the triangle inequality, the
Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and Markov’s inequality, this implies that the
quadratic term in (
S520
) satisfies

\displaystyle\left|(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})^{\top}\left(T^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\dot{x}_{i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}\right)(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})\right|

(S522)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle||\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}||^{2}\times T^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}||\dot{x}_{i,t}||^{2}=O_{p}(G_{1}(nT)^{-1}).

Similarly,

\left|T^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast})\right|\leq||\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}||\times T^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}||\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}||=O_{p}(G_{1}n^{-1}).

(S523)

Next, since
\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}=\varepsilon_{1,i,t}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}
, we can decompose

T^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2}=T^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}-T^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{2}.

(S524)

By Assumptions
1
, Lemma
S40
(iii), and (
S504
),

\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{4}]=\mathbb{E}[\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{\ast 4}]\leq Kn_{g}^{-2}.

(S525)

Hence by (
S524
) and Markov’s inequality,

T^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})=T^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{2}=O_{p}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\right).

(S526)

Combining (
S520
), (
S522
), (
S523
) and (
S526
), we obtain

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\left(\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}-T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}\right)=O_{p}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}+G_{1}n^{-1}\right).

(S527)

Finally, by Assumption
1
, Lemma
S40
(iii), and Markov’s
inequality,

T^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}])=O_{p}\left(n^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\right)^{1/2}\right).

Together with (
S527
), this implies

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\left(\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}-T^{-1}\sum_{t\leq T}\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}]\right)=O_{p}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}+G_{1}n^{-1}+n^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\right)^{1/2}\right).

(S528)

Since
\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\geq G_{1}n^{-1}\geq n^{-1}
,
the claim of the lemma follows from (
S528
) and the definition
of
s_{\varepsilon_{1},i}^{2}
.
Q.E.D.

Lemma S45
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
4
(ii) and
5
(i),
\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}-s_{1,i}^{2}))^{2}=O_{p}(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1})
.

Proof of Lemma
S45
. Using
1
and the
Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}\left(n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(\hat{\sigma}_{1,i}^{2}-s_{1,i}^{2})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}(n_{g}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}

(S529)

\displaystyle+K||\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}||^{2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}(n_{g}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\left\|\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+K||\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}||^{4}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}(n_{g}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}||\dot{x}_{i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}||^{2}.

By the similar arguments for showing (
S522
) and (
S523
), we have

||\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}||^{4}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}(n_{g}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}||\dot{x}_{i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}||^{2}=O_{p}(G_{1}n^{-4}),

(S530)

and

||\hat{\theta}_{1}-\theta_{1}^{\ast}||^{2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}(n_{g}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\left\|\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}\dot{x}_{i,t}^{\top}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}(G_{1}n^{-2}).

(S531)

Next, since
\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}=\varepsilon_{1,i,t}-\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}(n_{g}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}(\dot{\varepsilon}_{1,i,t}^{2}-\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}(n_{g}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\varepsilon_{1,i,t}^{2}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{2}

(S532)

\displaystyle+2\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}(n_{g}T)^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\leq T}\bar{\varepsilon}_{1,g,t}^{4}\overset{}{=}O_{p}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{1}}n_{g}^{-1}\right),

where the last equality holds by Lemma
S40
(iii), (
S525
) and Markov’s inequality. The claim of the lemma now follows
from (
S529
)-(
S532
).
Q.E.D.

Appendix F
Proofs for Appendix
C

Proof of Theorem
A1
. By (
S624
) in
Lemma
S57
, Assumptions
8
and
10
(i), and Lemma
S58
,

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left|(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\left.\partial^{\left|\nu\right|}\psi(z_{i,t},\tilde{\phi}_{g,m})\right/(\partial\phi_{1}^{\nu_{1}}\cdots\partial\phi_{k}^{\nu_{k}})\right|=O_{p}(1)

(S533)

for
\left|\nu\right|\leq 3
. Applying the Taylor expansion to
the second equation (
S634
) obtains

\displaystyle 0_{d_{\theta}\times 1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left[\hat{\Psi}_{\theta,g,m}+\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\theta,g,m}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})+\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})\right]

\displaystyle\text{ \ }+2^{-1}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\hat{\Psi}_{\theta_{j}\phi\phi,g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})\right)_{j=1,\ldots,d_{\theta}}

\displaystyle\text{ \ }+O_{p}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{3}\right),

(S534)

where the last term is by (
S533
) and the Cauchy-Schwarz
inequality. By Lemma
S62
and (
S549
) in Lemma
S1

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{3}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left((GM)^{1/p}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\right)O_{p}\left(GM+(nT)||\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}||^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left((GM)^{1+1/p}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\right),

(S535)

which together with Lemma
S62
implies that

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{3}

\displaystyle\leq K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{3}+K(nT)||\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}||^{3}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left((GM)^{1+1/p}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\right).

(S536)

By (
S633
) in Lemma
S60
and Lemma
S62
,

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\theta,g,m}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\Psi_{\theta\theta,g,m}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})+o_{p}\left((nT)^{-1/2}\right).

(S537)

Combining the results in Lemma
S63
, Lemma
S64
, (
S534
), (
S536
) and (
S537
), and
applying Assumption
10
(ii) we get

\displaystyle 0_{d_{\theta}\times 1}

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m})

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(\Psi_{\theta\theta,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\Psi_{\gamma\theta,g,m})(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }-(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(n_{g}T_{m})\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{U}_{\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right]

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }-\frac{(nT)^{-1}}{2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\Psi_{\gamma_{j}\gamma\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right]\right)_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+\frac{(nT)^{-1}}{2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\Psi_{\theta_{j}\gamma\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right]\right)_{j\leq d_{\theta}}+o_{p}\left((nT)^{-1/2}\right).

The assertion of the lemma follows from Assumption
9
and the above
equation.
Q.E.D.

Proof of Theorem
A2
. First note that by Lemma
S67
and Lemma
S68

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\hat{\Psi}_{\phi\phi,g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}+o_{p}((GM)^{1/2}(nT)^{-1/2}).

which together with Lemma
S66
implies that

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left[\hat{\Psi}_{g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m})-\hat{\Psi}_{g,m}(\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})\right]

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\phi,g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})

\displaystyle+\frac{(nT)^{-1/2}}{2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}+o_{p}((GM)^{1/2}(nT)^{-1/2}).

(S538)

By Lemma
S62
and (
S565
) in Lemma
S47
,

\left|(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\theta,g,m}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})\right|\leq\left\|\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}\right\|\left\|(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\theta,g,m}\right\|=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2})

which together with (
S538
) implies that

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left[\hat{\Psi}_{g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m})-\hat{\Psi}_{g,m}(\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})\right]

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})

\displaystyle+\frac{(nT)^{-1/2}}{2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}+o_{p}((GM)^{1/2}(nT)^{-1/2}).

(S539)

The assertion of the lemma follows from (
S720
) in the proof of
Lemma
S67
and (
S539
).
Q.E.D.

Proof of Theorem
A4
. By the martingale CLT (e.g., Corollary 3.1 of
26
), to verify Assumption
11
(ii) it is sufficient to show that (
105
) and (
106
)
hold.

To show (
105
), we first notice that

\mathbb{E}[\xi_{nT,i}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{nT,i-1}]=\frac{\mathbb{E}[(\tilde{\Psi}_{i}^{\ast}+\tilde{V}_{i}^{\ast})^{2}]}{n\omega_{n,T}^{2}}+\frac{\mathbb{E}[\tilde{U}_{i}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{nT,i-1}]}{n\omega_{n,T}^{2}}+\frac{2\mathbb{E}[(\tilde{\Psi}_{i}^{\ast}+\tilde{V}_{i}^{\ast})\tilde{U}_{i}|\mathcal{F}_{nT,i-1}]}{n\omega_{n,T}^{2}}.

(S540)

By Assumptions
11
(i) and
12
(iii), and Lemma
S70
,

\frac{\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{U}_{i}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{nT,i-1}]}{n\omega_{n,T}^{2}}=\frac{\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{U}_{i}^{2}]}{n\omega_{n,T}^{2}}+o_{p}\left(1\right).

(S541)

By the independence of
\left\{z_{i,t}\right\}_{t\leq T}
across
i
,
\tilde{\Psi}_{i}^{\ast}+\tilde{V}_{i}^{\ast}
and
\tilde{U}_{i}
are
uncorrelated which implies that

n\omega_{n,T}^{2}=\sum_{i\leq n}\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i}+\tilde{V}_{i}+\tilde{U}_{i})=\sum_{i\leq n}(\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{\Psi}_{i}+\tilde{V}_{i})+\mathrm{Var}(\tilde{U}_{i}))=\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[(\tilde{\Psi}_{i}^{\ast}+\tilde{V}_{i}^{\ast})^{2}+\tilde{U}_{i}^{2}]

(S542)

where the third equality is by
\mathbb{E}[\tilde{U}_{i}]=0
for all
i
. By
(
S540
), (
S541
) and (
S542
) we deduce
that

\displaystyle\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{nT,i}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{nT,i-1}]

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[(\tilde{\Psi}_{i}^{\ast}+\tilde{V}_{i}^{\ast})^{2}+\tilde{U}_{i}^{2}]}{n\omega_{n,T}^{2}}

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ \ \ }+\frac{2\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[(\tilde{\Psi}_{i}^{\ast}+\tilde{V}_{i}^{\ast})\tilde{U}_{i}|\mathcal{F}_{nT,i-1}]}{n\omega_{n,T}^{2}}+o_{p}\left(1\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[(\tilde{\Psi}_{i}^{\ast}+\tilde{V}_{i}^{\ast})^{2}+\tilde{U}_{i}^{2}]}{n\omega_{n,T}^{2}}+o_{p}\left(1\right)=1+o_{p}(1),

(S543)

where the second equality follows by Lemma
S69
and Assumption
12
(iii). The first sufficient condition of the martingale CLT, i.e., (
105
) now follows from (
S543
).

To verify (
106
), we notice that for any
\varepsilon>0
,

\displaystyle\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{nT,i}^{2}I\{|\xi_{nT,i}|\overset{}{>}\varepsilon\}]

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{-2}\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[|\xi_{nT,i}|^{4}]

\displaystyle\leq K\varepsilon^{-2}\frac{\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[(\tilde{\Psi}_{i}^{\ast})^{4}]+\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[(\tilde{V}_{i}^{\ast})^{4}]+\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{U}_{i}^{4}]}{(n\omega_{n,T}^{2})^{2}}.

(S544)

By (
S732
) in the proof of Lemma
S69
,

\frac{\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[(\tilde{\Psi}_{i}^{\ast})^{4}]}{(n\omega_{n,T}^{2})^{2}}=\frac{\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[|\tilde{\Psi}_{1,i}^{\ast}-\tilde{\Psi}_{2,i}^{\ast}|^{4}]}{(n\omega_{n,T}^{2})^{2}}\leq n^{-1}\max_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{\tilde{\Psi}_{1,i}^{\ast}-\tilde{\Psi}_{2,i}^{\ast}}{\omega_{n,T}}\right)^{4}\right]\leq Kn^{-1}.

(S545)

By Assumption
11
(i) and (
S733
) in the proof of Lemma
S69

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[(\tilde{V}_{j,i}^{\ast})^{4}]}{(n\omega_{n,T}^{2})^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\mathbb{E}[(\tilde{V}_{j,i}^{\ast})^{4}]}{(n\omega_{n,T}^{2})^{2}}\leq K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(M_{j}^{2}n_{g}^{-4}T^{-2})

\displaystyle\leq KM_{j}^{2}T^{-2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{g}^{-3}\leq KM_{j}^{2}G_{j}T^{-2}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{g}^{-3}\leq KG_{j}^{-1}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{g}^{-3}

(S546)

where the last inequality is by

M_{j}^{2}G_{j}T^{-2}=(M_{j}G_{j}(nT)^{-1/2})^{2}(nT^{-1})G_{j}^{-1}\leq KG_{j}^{-1},

(S547)

where the inequality follows since
nT^{-1}\leq K
by Assumption
1
(i)
and
M_{j}G_{j}(nT)^{-1/2}\leq K
by Assumption
10
(ii). By Assumption
11
(i) and Lemma
S71
,

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[|\tilde{U}_{j,i}|^{4}]}{(n\omega_{n,T}^{2})^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\mathbb{E}[|\tilde{U}_{j,i}|^{4}]}{(n\omega_{n,T}^{2})^{2}}\leq K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}M_{j}^{2}T^{-2}n_{g}^{-2}

\displaystyle\leq KM_{j}^{2}G_{j}T^{-2}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{g}^{-1}\leq KG_{j}^{-1}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{g}^{-1},

(S548)

where the last equality is by (
S547
). Collecting the results
in (
S544
)-(
S548
), we deduce that

\sum_{i\leq n}\mathbb{E}[\xi_{nT,i}^{2}I\{|\xi_{nT,i}|>\varepsilon\}]\leq K\varepsilon^{-2}\left(n^{-1}+G_{j}^{-1}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}_{j}}n_{g}^{-1}\right)\rightarrow 0\text{, as }n,T\rightarrow\infty

which shows (
106
).
Q.E.D.

Theorem S1
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
7
,
8
,
9
and
10
, we have

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left\|\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}\right\|}{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}}=O_{p}(1),

(S549)

and

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left\|\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}+\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|}{(GM)^{2/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}}=O_{p}(1),

where
\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\equiv(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\psi_{\gamma}(z_{i,t})
.

Proof of Theorem
S1
. By the triangle
inequality,

\displaystyle\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left\|\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}+\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|}{(GM)^{2/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}+||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(\lambda_{\max}((\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})^{2}))^{1/2}\frac{\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})+\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|}{(GM)^{2/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}+||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(\lambda_{\max}((\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})^{2}))^{1/2}\frac{\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})+\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\theta,g,m}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})+\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|}{(GM)^{2/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}+||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}}

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(\lambda_{\max}((\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})^{2}))^{1/2}\frac{\left\|(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|}{(GM)^{2/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}}

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(\lambda_{\max}((\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})^{2}))^{1/2}(nT)^{1/2}\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\theta,g,m}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})\right\|.

(S550)

The first term on the far RHS of (
S550
) can be analyzed
using (
S638
) of Lemma
S62
and Lemma
S60
(ii) to yield

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{(\lambda_{\max}((\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})^{2}))^{1/2}\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})+\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\theta,g,m}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})+\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|}{(GM)^{2/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}+||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}}=O_{p}(1).

(S551)

In order to analyze the second term on the far RHS of (
S550
), we note

\displaystyle\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(\lambda_{\max}((\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})^{2}))^{1/2}\frac{\left\|(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|}{(GM)^{2/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(\lambda_{\max}((\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})^{2})\lambda_{\max}((\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})^{2}))^{1/2}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}\right\|\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|}{(GM)^{2/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}},

(S552)

where
\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(\lambda_{\max}((\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})^{2})\lambda_{\max}((\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})^{2}))^{1/2}=O_{p}(1)
by Lemma
S60
(ii) and Assumption
9
;

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}\right\|}{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}}=O_{p}(1)

(S553)

by Lemma
S59
; and

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|}{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}}=O_{p}(1)

(S554)

by (
S575
) in Lemma
S49
. By (
S552
), (
S553
) and (
S554
), we have

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(\lambda_{\max}((\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})^{2}))^{1/2}\frac{\left\|(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|}{(GM)^{2/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}}=O_{p}(1).

(S555)

In order to analyze the third term on the far RHS of (
S550
),
we use the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, Lemma
S62
, Lemma
S60
(ii), and (
S632
) of Lemma
S60
, and conclude

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(\lambda_{\max}((\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})^{2}))^{1/2}(nT)^{1/2}\left\|\Psi_{\gamma\theta,g,m}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})\right\|=O_{p}(1).

(S556)

From (
S550
), (
S551
), (
S555
)
and (
S556
), we have

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left\|\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}+\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|}{(GM)^{2/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}+||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}}=O_{p}(1).

(S557)

By the triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left\|\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}\right\|}{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left\|\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}+\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|}{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}}

\displaystyle\text{ \ }+\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left\|\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|}{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}},

(S558)

where

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left\|\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|}{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}}=O_{p}(1)

(S559)

by Assumption
9
and (
S554
). Since
||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}=||\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}||^{2}+||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}
and
n_{g}T_{m}\leq nT
,

\displaystyle\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{(GM)^{2/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}+||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}}{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}}

\displaystyle\leq(GM)^{1/p}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}+\frac{(nT)^{1/2}||\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}||^{2}}{(GM)^{1/p}}+\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}}{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}}

\displaystyle=\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||\times\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||}{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1)\times\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||}{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}}+o_{p}(1)

(S560)

where the first equality is by Assumption
10
(i) and Lemma
S62
, the second equality is by Lemma
S58
. By (
S557
) and (
S560
)

\displaystyle\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left\|\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}+\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|}{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}}

\displaystyle=\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left\|\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}+\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|}{(GM)^{2/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}+||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}}\frac{(GM)^{2/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}+||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}}{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}}

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1)\times\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||}{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}}+o_{p}(1).

(S561)

Collecting the results in (
S558
), (
S559
)
and (
S561
), we get

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left\|\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}\right\|}{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}}\leq o_{p}(1)\times\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||}{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}}+O_{p}(1),

which implies that (
S549
) holds. Therefore, by Lemma
S62
and (
S549
),

\displaystyle\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{(GM)^{2/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}+||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}}{(GM)^{2/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}}

\displaystyle=\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left(1+\frac{(nT)^{-1}}{(GM)^{2/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}}(nT)||\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}||^{2}+\frac{\left\|\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}\right\|^{2}}{(GM)^{2/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}}\right)=O_{p}(1).

(S562)

The assertion of the lemma now follows from (
S557
) and (
S562
).
Q.E.D.

F.1
Auxiliary Lemmas

Lemma S46
.

Suppose
X_{1}
and
X_{2}
are zero mean random
vectors with
\max_{j=1,2}\lambda_{\max}(\mathbb{E}[X_{j}X_{j}^{\top}])<\infty
. Then

\lambda_{\max}(\mathbb{E}[X_{1}X_{2}^{\top}]\mathbb{E}[X_{2}X_{1}^{\top}])\leq\lambda_{\max}(\mathbb{E}[X_{1}X_{1}^{\top}])\lambda_{\max}(\mathbb{E}[X_{2}X_{2}^{\top}]).

(S563)

Proof of Lemma
S46
. Let
\Sigma_{X_{j}}\equiv\mathbb{E}[X_{j}X_{j}^{\top}]
and
k_{j}
denote the dimension of
X_{j}
(
j=1,2
). For any
k_{1}\times 1
real vectors
a_{1}
, we shall show

a_{1}^{\top}(\mathbb{E}[X_{1}X_{2}^{\top}]\mathbb{E}[X_{2}X_{1}^{\top}])a_{1}\leq\lambda_{\max}(\Sigma_{X_{1}})\lambda_{\max}(\Sigma_{X_{2}})a_{1}^{\top}a_{1},

(S564)

which directly shows (
S563
). Since (
S564
) holds trivially if

a_{1}^{\top}\Sigma_{X_{1}}a_{1}=0
, we only need to consider the case that
a_{1}^{\top}\Sigma_{X_{1}}a_{1}>0
. Let
(\Sigma_{X_{2}}^{1/2})^{+}
denote
Moore-Penrose inverse of
\Sigma_{X_{2}}^{1/2}
and
\Sigma_{X_{2}}^{1/2}

is the unique symmetric matrix square root of
\Sigma_{X_{2}}
. Then by
Lemma E.1.(b, c) in
30
,

\displaystyle a_{1}^{\top}(\mathbb{E}[X_{1}X_{2}^{\top}]\mathbb{E}[X_{2}X_{1}^{\top}])a_{1}

\displaystyle=(\mathrm{Cov}(a_{1}^{\top}X_{1},X_{2}))^{\top}(\mathrm{Cov}(a_{1}^{\top}X_{1},X_{2}))

\displaystyle=\left((\Sigma_{X_{2}}^{1/2})^{+}\mathrm{Cov}(a_{1}^{\top}X_{1},X_{2})\right)^{\top}\Sigma_{X_{2}}\left((\Sigma_{X_{2}}^{1/2})^{+}\mathrm{Cov}(a_{1}^{\top}X_{1},X_{2})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\lambda_{\max}(\Sigma_{X_{2}})\mathrm{Var}(a_{1}^{\top}X_{1})\leq\lambda_{\max}(\Sigma_{X_{1}})\lambda_{\max}(\Sigma_{X_{2}})a_{1}^{\top}a_{1},

which proves (
S564
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S47
.

Suppose that: (i)
\varphi(z_{i,t};\phi)
is a function
indexed by the parameter
\phi\in\Phi
where
\Phi
is a compact, convex
subset of
\mathbb{R}^{k}
and
k\equiv\dim\left(\phi\right)
; (ii) there
exists a function
D\left(z_{i,t}\right)
such that
\left|\varphi\left(z_{i,t},\phi_{1}\right)-\varphi\left(z_{i,t},\phi_{2}\right)\right|\leq D\left(z_{i,t}\right)\left\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\right\|
for all
\phi_{1},\phi_{2}\in\Phi
; (iii) the
function
D(z_{i,t})
satisfies
\sup_{i,t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|D(z_{i,t})\right|^{p+\delta}\right]<K
for some integer
p\geq\max\{2,k+\delta\}
and for some
\delta>0
; (iv)
\sup_{i,t}\sup_{\phi\in\Phi}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\varphi\left(z_{i,t},\phi\right)\right|^{p+\delta}\right]<K
. Then under Assumption
1
, we have

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right|^{p}\right]\leq K

(S565)

for any
\phi\in\Phi
, where
\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\equiv\varphi\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right]
. Moreover for any
\phi_{1},\phi_{2}\in\Phi

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\left(\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi_{1}\right)-\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi_{2}\right)\right)\right|^{p}\right]\leq K\left\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\right\|^{p}.

(S566)

Proof of Lemma
S47
. Since
\left\{z_{i,t},t=1,2,\ldots\right\}
are independent across
i
, we can use
Rosenthal’s inequality of independent random variables (see, e.g., Theorem
2.12 in
26
) to obtain

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right|^{p}\right]\leq K\left(\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\left\|\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{p/2}+\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\left\|\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right\|_{p}^{p}\right).

(S567)

For any
\tilde{p}\geq 2
, applying Rosenthal’s inequality of strong mixing
processes (see, e.g., Theorem 2 in Section 1.4 of
17
),
we get a moment bound on
\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)
:

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right|^{\tilde{p}}\right]\leq K\max\left\{\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\left\|\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right\|_{2+\delta}^{2}\right)^{\tilde{p}/2},\text{ \ }\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\left\|\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right\|_{\tilde{p}+\delta}^{\tilde{p}}\right\}

(S568)

as long as
\mathbb{E}[\left|\varphi\left(z_{i,t},\phi\right)\right|^{\tilde{p}+\delta}]<\infty
. In the rest of the proof, we shall
consider
\tilde{p}=2\
or
p
. Since
\left\|\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right\|_{\tilde{p}+\delta}\leq K
by condition (iv)
of the lemma, (
S568
) further implies that

\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right|^{\tilde{p}}\right]\leq K(n_{g}T_{m}^{\tilde{p}/2}).

(S569)

Combining the results in (
S567
) and (
S569
), we obtain

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right|^{p}\right]\leq K\left(1+n_{g}^{1-p/2}\right)\leq K

(S570)

where the second inequality is due to
p\geq 2
. The assertion in (
S565
) now follows from (
S570
).

For ease of notation, let
u\left(z_{i,t};\phi_{1},\phi_{2}\right)\equiv\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi_{1}\right)-\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi_{2}\right)
. By the triangle inequality and condition (ii) of
the lemma,

\left|u\left(z_{i,t};\phi_{1},\phi_{2}\right)\right|\leq(D\left(z_{i,t}\right)+\mathbb{E}\left[D\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right])\left\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\right\|,

which together with condition (iii) of the lemma implies that for
\tilde{p}=2\
or
p
,

\sup_{i,t}\left\|u\left(z_{i,t};\phi_{1},\phi_{2}\right)\right\|_{\tilde{p}+\delta}\leq\left\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\right\|.

(S571)

By the same arguments for deriving (
S567
) and (
S568
), we can
show that

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}u\left(z_{i,t};\phi_{1},\phi_{2}\right)\right|^{p}\right]\leq K\left(\left(\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\left\|\sum_{t\in I_{m}}u\left(z_{i,t};\phi_{1},\phi_{2}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{p/2}+\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\left\|\sum_{t\in I_{m}}u\left(z_{i,t};\phi_{1},\phi_{2}\right)\right\|_{p}^{p}\right)

and

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{t\in I_{m}}u\left(z_{i,t};\phi_{1},\phi_{2}\right)\right|^{\tilde{p}}\right]\leq K\max\left\{\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\left\|u\left(z_{i,t};\phi_{1},\phi_{2}\right)\right\|_{2+\delta}^{2}\right)^{\tilde{p}/2},\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\left\|u\left(z_{i,t};\phi_{1},\phi_{2}\right)\right\|_{\tilde{p}+\delta}^{\tilde{p}}\right\},

respectively, which combined with (
S571
) yields

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}u\left(z_{i,t};\phi_{1},\phi_{2}\right)\right|^{p}\right]\leq K\left(1+n_{g}^{1-p/2}\right)(n_{g}T_{m})^{p/2}\left\|\phi_{1}-\phi_{2}\right\|^{p}.

(S572)

The claim in (
S566
) follows directly from (
S572
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S48
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
8
,
9
and
10
, we have

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})\right\|^{4}\right]\leq K

(S573)

and

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq K,

(S574)

Proof of Lemma
S48

. Follows from Assumption
8
(ii) and (
S565
) in Lemma
S47
.
Q.E.D.

Lemma S49
.

Under the conditions of Lemma
S47
, we have

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\sup_{\phi\in\Phi}\left|(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right|=O_{p}((GM)^{1/p}),

(S575)

and for any
\phi^{\prime}\in\mathop{\mathrm{i}nt}\Phi

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\sup_{\phi\in\Phi}\frac{\left|(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}(\varphi\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi\left(z_{i,t};\phi^{\ast}\right)\right])\right|}{(GM)^{1/p}+\left\|\phi-\phi^{\prime}\right\|}=O_{p}(1).

(S576)

Proof of Lemma
S49
. To prove the claim in (
S575
), we first show that

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\sup_{\phi\in\Phi}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right\|_{p}\leq K.

(S577)

Note that by a maximal inequality under the
L_{p}
-norm (see, e.g., Lemma
2.2.2 of
36
) and (
S577
),

\displaystyle\left\|\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\sup_{\phi\in\Phi}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq K(GM)^{1/p}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\sup_{\phi\in\Phi}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right\|_{p}\leq K(GM)^{1/p}

(S578)

which together with Markov’s inequality implies that

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\sup_{\phi\in\Phi}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)=O_{p}((GM)^{1/p}).

(S579)

The result in (
S579
) is then used to show (
S575
).

For ease of notation, we set for each
g\in\mathcal{G}\
and
m\in\mathcal{M}
,

\pi_{g,m}(\phi)\equiv(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right),\quad\phi\in\Phi.

Construct nested sets
\Phi_{0}\subset\Phi_{1}\cdots\subset\Phi
such
that
\Phi_{0}=\{\phi_{0}\}
for any given
\phi_{0}

\in\Phi
, and
for each
j\geq 1
,
\Phi_{j}
is a maximal set of points such that each
pair of distinct elements in
\Phi
has distance greater than
2^{-j}
.
Since
\Phi
is compact, the number of points in
\Phi_{j}
is less than
K(2^{j})^{k}
. Link every point
\phi_{j+1}\in\Phi_{j+1}
to a unique
\phi_{j}\in\Phi_{j}
such that
\left\|\phi_{j+1}-\phi_{j}\right\|\leq 2^{-j}
. Then for any
J\geq 0
and
\phi_{J+1}\in\phi_{J+1}
, we can construct a chain
\phi_{J+1},\ldots,\phi_{0}
, and
hence, by the triangle inequality

\left|\pi_{g,m}(\phi_{J+1})-\pi_{g,m}(\phi_{0})\right|=\left|\sum_{j=0}^{J}\left[\pi_{g,m}(\phi_{j+1})-\pi_{g,m}(\phi_{j})\right]\right|\leq\sum_{j=0}^{J}\max\left|\pi_{g,m}(\phi_{j+1})-\pi_{g,m}(\phi_{j})\right|

(S580)

where, for each
j
, the maximum is taken over all links
(\phi_{j+1},\phi_{j})
from
\Phi_{j+1}
to
\Phi_{j}
(with the total number less than
K(2^{j+1})^{k}
). We then observe

\displaystyle\left\|\max_{\phi\in\Phi_{J+1}}\left|\pi_{g,m}(\phi)\right|\right\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=0}^{J}\left\|\max\left|\pi_{g,m}(\phi_{j+1})-\pi_{g,m}(\phi_{j})\right|\right\|_{p}+\left\|\pi_{g,m}(\phi_{0})\right\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq K\sum_{j=0}^{J}(2^{j+1})^{k/p}\max\left\|\pi_{g,m}(\phi_{j+1})-\pi_{g,m}(\phi_{j})\right\|_{p}+K

\displaystyle\leq K\sum_{j=0}^{J}(2^{j})^{k/p}2^{-j}+\left\|\pi_{g,m}(\phi_{0})\right\|_{p}\leq K,

where the first inequality is by (
S580
); the second inequality is by
the maximal inequality under the
L_{p}
-norm and (
S565
); the third
inequality follows from (
S566
) in Lemma
S47
; and the last
inequality holds because
\sum_{j}(2^{j})^{k/p}2^{-j}<\infty
as implied by

p>k
. Since the stochastic process
\pi_{g,m}(
\phi
)
indexed by
\phi
is separable, by letting
J\rightarrow\infty
, we further have

\left\|\sup_{{\phi}\in\Phi}\left|\pi_{g,m}({\phi})\right|\right\|_{p}\leq K

for any
g\in\mathcal{G}\
and
m\in\mathcal{M}
, which shows (
S577
). Using the same arguments, we can show that

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\sup_{\phi\in\Phi}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}-\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)=O_{p}((GM)^{1/p}).

(S581)

Since

\left|\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right|\leq\max\left\{\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right),\text{ }\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}-\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right\},

(S582)

the claim in (
S575
) follows from (
S579
), (
S581
) and (
S582
).

To show (
S576
), we note that

\displaystyle\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\sup_{\phi\in\Phi}\frac{\left|(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}(\varphi\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi\left(z_{i,t};\phi^{\prime}\right)\right])\right|}{(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}(GM)^{1/p}+\left\|\phi-\phi^{\prime}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\sup_{\phi\in\Phi}\frac{\left|(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right|}{(GM)^{1/p}}

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\sup_{\phi\in\Phi}\frac{\left|(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)-\varphi\left(z_{i,t};\phi^{\prime}\right)\right]\right|}{(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}(GM)^{1/p}+\left\|\phi-\phi^{\prime}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\sup_{\phi\in\Phi}\frac{\sup_{i,t}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left|D(z_{i,t}\right|)\right]\right|\left\|\phi-\phi^{\prime}\right\|}{(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}(GM)^{1/p}+\left\|\phi-\phi^{\prime}\right\|}+O_{p}(1)\overset{}{=}O_{p}(1),

where the first inequality is by the triangle inequality, the second
inequality is by condition (ii) of Lemma
S47
and (
S575
), the
equality is by condition (iii) of Lemma
S47
.
Q.E.D.

For the sake of simplicity in notation, we assume that the observations
across
i
are arranged in a manner where the group assignment function
g(\cdot)
is non-decreasing. This arrangement is inconsequential because of
the independence assumption stated in Assumption
1
.(iii).

Lemma S50
.

Let
\varphi_{j}(\cdot)
:
\mathcal{Z}\longrightarrow\mathbb{R}
be functions on the support
\mathcal{Z}
of
z_{i,t}
for
j=1,2
. Suppose that for
j=1,2
: (i)
\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{j}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right]=0
; (ii)
\sup_{i,t}\mathbb{E}[\left|\varphi_{j}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right|^{4+\delta}]<K
for some
\delta>0
. Then under Assumption
1
, we have

\mathrm{Var}\left(M^{-1/2}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}T_{m}^{-1}\sum_{t_{1}\in I_{m}}\sum_{t_{2}\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i,t_{1}})\varphi_{2}(z_{i,t_{2}})\right)\leq K,

(S583)

and

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i_{1}=i_{g}^{\min}+1}^{i_{g}^{\max}}\sum_{i_{2}=i_{g}^{\min}}^{i_{1}-1}\left(\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}T_{m}^{-1}\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i_{1},t})\right)\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{2}(z_{i_{2},t})\right)\right)\right\|^{2}\right]\leq K(GM),

(S584)

where
i_{g}^{\max}\equiv\max(I_{g})
and
i_{g}^{\min}\equiv\min(I_{g})

for any
g\in\mathcal{G}
.

Proof of Lemma
S50
. For ease of notation, let
Y_{m}\equiv T_{m}^{-1}\sum_{t_{1}\in I_{m}}\sum_{t_{2}\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i,t_{1}})\varphi_{2}(z_{i,t_{2}})
for any
m\in\mathcal{M}
and
\delta^{\prime}\equiv\delta/3
. For any
i
, let
\mathcal{F}_{-\infty}^{i,t}
denote the sigma field generated by
\left\{z_{is}\right\}_{s=-\infty}^{t}
and
\mathcal{F}_{t}^{i,+\infty}
denote
the sigma field generated by
\left\{z_{is}\right\}_{s=t}^{+\infty}
.
Consider any
m_{1},m_{2}\in\mathcal{M}
with
m_{2}>m_{1}
. It is clear
that
Y_{m_{1}}
is
\mathcal{F}_{-\infty}^{i,t_{m_{1}}^{\max}}
measurable
and
Y_{m_{2}}
is
\mathcal{F}_{t_{m_{2}}^{\min}}^{i,+\infty}
measurable
where
t_{m}^{\max}\equiv\max(I_{m})
and
t_{m}^{\min}\equiv\min(I_{m})

for any
m\in\mathcal{M}
. By Corollary A.2. in
26
,

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{Cov}\left(Y_{m_{1}},Y_{m_{2}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq K\left(\alpha_{i}(t_{m_{2}}^{\min}-t_{m_{1}}^{\max})\right)^{\delta^{\prime}/(2+\delta^{\prime})}\left\|Y_{m_{1}}\right\|_{2+\delta^{\prime}}\left\|Y_{m_{2}}\right\|_{2+\delta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq Ka^{(t_{m_{2}}^{\min}-t_{m_{1}}^{\max})\delta^{\prime}/(2+\delta^{\prime})}\max_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|Y_{m}\right\|_{2+\delta^{\prime}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq Ka^{(m_{2}-m_{1})\delta^{\prime}/(2+\delta^{\prime})}\max_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|Y_{m}\right\|_{2+\delta^{\prime}}^{2}

where the second inequality is by Assumption
1
(iv), and the last
inequality is by
t_{m_{2}}^{\min}-t_{m_{1}}^{\max}\geq m_{2}-m_{1}
and
a\in(0,1)
. Therefore
