\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left(M^{-1/2}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}Y_{m}\right)

\displaystyle=M^{-1}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\mathrm{Var}\left(Y_{m}\right)+2M^{-1}\sum_{l=1}^{M-1}\sum_{j=1}^{M-l}\mathrm{Cov}\left(Y_{j},Y_{j+l}\right)

\displaystyle\leq K\left(1+2\sum_{l=1}^{M-1}(1-lM^{-1})a^{l\delta^{\prime}/(2+\delta^{\prime})}\right)\max_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|Y_{m}\right\|_{2+\delta^{\prime}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq K\left(1+2\sum_{l=1}^{M-1}a^{l\delta^{\prime}/(2+\delta^{\prime})}\right)\max_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|Y_{m}\right\|_{2+\delta^{\prime}}^{2}\leq K\max_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|Y_{m}\right\|_{2+\delta^{\prime}}^{2}.

(S585)

By Hölder’s inequality,

\displaystyle\left\|Y_{m}\right\|_{2+\delta^{\prime}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\left(T_{m}^{-1/2}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i,t})\right)\left(T_{m}^{-1/2}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{2}(z_{i,t})\right)\right|^{2+\delta^{\prime}}\right]\right)^{\frac{2}{2+\delta^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left|T_{m}^{-1/2}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i,t})\right|^{4+2\delta^{\prime}}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\left|T_{m}^{-1/2}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{2}(z_{i,t})\right|^{4+2\delta^{\prime}}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2+\delta^{\prime}}}.

(S586)

By the same arguments for deriving (
S568
), we can show that

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{j}(z_{i,t})\right|^{4+2\delta^{\prime}}\right]\leq\max\left\{\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\left\|\varphi_{j}(z_{i,t})\right\|_{4+3\delta^{\prime}}^{4+2\delta^{\prime}},\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\left\|\varphi_{j}(z_{i,t})\right\|_{2+\delta^{\prime}}^{2}\right)^{2+\delta^{\prime}}\right\}\leq KT_{m}^{2+\delta^{\prime}}

(S587)

where the second inequality is by condition (ii) of the lemma. Combining the
results in (
S586
) and (
S587
), we have
\max_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|Y_{m}\right\|_{2+\delta^{\prime}}^{2}\leq K
, which together
with (
S585
) shows (
S583
).

To show (
S584
), we first use Assumption
1
(iii) and conditions
(i, ii) of the lemma to observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i_{1}=i_{g}^{\min}+1}^{i_{g}^{\max}}\sum_{i_{2}=i_{g}^{\min}}^{i_{1}-1}\left(\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}T_{m}^{-1}\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i_{1},t})\right)\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{2}(z_{i_{2},t})\right)\right)\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}n_{g}^{-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{i_{1}=i_{g}^{\min}+1}^{i_{g}^{\max}}\sum_{i_{2}=i_{g}^{\min}}^{i_{1}-1}\left(\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}T_{m}^{-1}\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i_{1},t})\right)\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{2}(z_{i_{2},t})\right)\right)\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}n_{g}^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}=i_{g}^{\min}+1}^{i_{g}^{\max}}\sum_{i_{2}=i_{g}^{\min}}^{i_{1}-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}T_{m}^{-1}\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i_{1},t})\right)\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{2}(z_{i_{2},t})\right)\right|^{2}\right].

(S588)

For any
m_{1},m_{2}\in\mathcal{M}
,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[(T_{m_{1}}T_{m_{2}})^{-1}\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m_{1}}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i_{1},t})\right)\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m_{1}}}\varphi_{2}(z_{i_{2},t})\right)\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m_{2}}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i_{1},t})\right)\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m_{2}}}\varphi_{2}(z_{i_{2},t})\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\left|T_{m_{1}}^{-1}\mathrm{Cov}\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m_{1}}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i_{1},t}),\sum_{t\in I_{m_{2}}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i_{1},t})\right)\right|\left|T_{m_{2}}^{-1}\mathrm{Cov}\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m_{1}}}\varphi_{2}(z_{i_{2},t}),\sum_{t\in I_{m_{2}}}\varphi_{2}(z_{i_{2},t})\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq Ka^{2|m_{2}-m_{1}|\delta^{\prime}/(2+\delta^{\prime})}\max_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|T_{m}^{-1/2}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i_{1},t})\right\|_{2+\delta^{\prime}}^{2}\max_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|T_{m}^{-1/2}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{2}(z_{i_{2},t})\right\|_{2+\delta^{\prime}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq Ka^{2|m_{2}-m_{1}|\delta^{\prime}/(2+\delta^{\prime})}

(S589)

where the first inequality is by Corollary A.2. in
26
and
Assumption
1
(iv), the second inequality is by (
S587
) and Hölder’s inequality. Using (
S589
), we can show that

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}T_{m}^{-1}\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i_{1},t})\right)\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{2}(z_{i_{2},t})\right)\right|^{2}\right]\leq KM.

(S590)

The assertion in (
S584
) follows from (
S588
) and (
S590
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S51
.

Let
\hat{\varphi}_{j,g,m}\equiv(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{j}\left(z_{i,t}\right)
, where
\varphi_{j}(\cdot)
:
\mathcal{Z}\longrightarrow\mathbb{R}
are functions on the support
\mathcal{Z}
of
z_{i,t}
for
j=1,2
. Then under the conditions of Lemma
S50
, we have

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}\right]+O_{p}((GM)^{1/2})=O_{p}(GM).

(S591)

Proof of Lemma
S51
. First note that by Assumption
1
(iii),

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}\right]=n_{g}^{-1}\mathbb{E}\left[T_{m}^{-1}\sum_{t_{1},t_{2}\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i,t_{1}})\varphi_{2}(z_{i,t_{2}})\right]

where
\sum_{t_{1},t_{2}\in I_{m}}
denotes the double summation over
t_{1},t_{2}\in I_{m}
. Therefore, for any
g\in\mathcal{G}
and
m\in\mathcal{M}
, we can express the equations

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}-\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}T_{m}^{-1}\sum_{t_{1},t_{2}\in I_{m}}\left(\varphi_{1}(z_{i,t_{1}})\varphi_{2}(z_{i,t_{2}})\right)^{\ast}

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i_{1}=i_{g}^{\min}+1}^{i_{g}^{\max}}\sum_{i_{2}=i_{g}^{\min}}^{i_{1}-1}\left(\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}T_{m}^{-1}\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i_{1},t})\right)\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{2}(z_{i_{2},t})\right)\right)

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i_{1}=i_{g}^{\min}+1}^{i_{g}^{\max}}\sum_{i_{2}=i_{g}^{\min}}^{i_{1}-1}\left(\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}T_{m}^{-1}\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{2}(z_{i_{1},t})\right)\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i_{2},t})\right)\right)

(S592)

where
\left(\varphi_{1}(z_{i,t_{1}})\varphi_{2}(z_{i,t_{2}})\right)^{\ast}\equiv\varphi_{1}(z_{i,t_{1}})\varphi_{2}(z_{i,t_{2}})-\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{1}(z_{i,t_{1}})\varphi_{2}(z_{i,t_{2}})\right]
. For any
m\in\mathcal{M}
, we can use the triangle inequality, Hölder’s
inequality, Corollary A.2. in
26
and Assumption
1
(iv) to deduce that

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[T_{m}^{-1}\sum_{t_{1},t_{2}\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i,t_{1}})\varphi_{2}(z_{i,t_{2}})\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq T_{m}^{-1}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{1}(z_{i,t})\varphi_{2}(z_{i,t})\right]\right|+T_{m}^{-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T_{m}-1}\sum_{t=t_{m}^{\min}}^{t_{m}^{\max}-l}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{1}(z_{i,t})\varphi_{2}(z_{i,t+l})\right]\right|

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+T_{m}^{-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T_{m}-1}\sum_{t=t_{m}^{\min}}^{t_{m}^{\max}-l}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{2}(z_{i,t})\varphi_{1}(z_{i,t+l})\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq K\left(1+\sum_{l=1}^{T_{m}-1}(1-lT_{m}^{-1})a^{l\delta/(2+\delta)}\right)\max_{j=1,2}\sup_{i,t}\left\|\varphi_{j}(z_{i,t})\right\|_{2+\delta}^{2}\leq K,

(S593)

where
t_{m}^{\max}\equiv\max(I_{m})
and
t_{m}^{\min}\equiv\min(I_{m})
, and the last inequality is by condition (ii) of Lemma
S50
.
This implies that

\left|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}\right]\right|\leq K(GM).

(S594)

By Assumption
1
(iii) and (
S583
) in Lemma
S50
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}T_{m}^{-1}\sum_{t_{1},t_{2}\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i,t_{1}})\varphi_{2}(z_{i,t_{2}})\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}n_{g}^{-2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}T_{m}^{-1}\sum_{t_{1},t_{2}\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i,t_{1}})\varphi_{2}(z_{i,t_{2}})\right)\leq K(GM)\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}}n_{g}^{-1}

which together with Markov’s inequality and (
S593
) implies that

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}T_{m}^{-1}\sum_{t_{1},t_{2}\in I_{m}}\left(\varphi_{1}(z_{i,t_{1}})\varphi_{2}(z_{i,t_{2}})\right)^{\ast}=O_{p}((GM)^{1/2}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}}n_{g}^{-1/2}).

(S595)

By Markov’s inequality and (
S584
) in Lemma
S50
, we have

(GM)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i_{1}=i_{g}^{\min}+1}^{i_{g}^{\max}}\sum_{i_{2}=i_{g}^{\min}}^{i_{1}-1}\left(\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}T_{m}^{-1}\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i_{1},t})\right)\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{2}(z_{i_{2},t})\right)\right)=O_{p}(1)

(S596)

and

(GM)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}n_{g}^{-1}\sum_{i_{1}=i_{g}^{\min}+1}^{i_{g}^{\max}}\sum_{i_{2}=i_{g}^{\min}}^{i_{1}-1}\left(\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}T_{m}^{-1}\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{2}(z_{i_{1},t})\right)\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}(z_{i_{2},t})\right)\right)=O_{p}(1),

(S597)

which together with (
S592
) and (
S595
) shows the first
equality in (
S591
). The second equality in (
S591
) holds by (
S594
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S52
.

Let
\varphi_{j}(\cdot)
:
\mathcal{Z}\longrightarrow\mathbb{R}
be functions on the support
\mathcal{Z}
of
z_{i,t}
for
j=1,2,3
. Suppose that for
j=1,2,3
: (i)
\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{j}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right]=0
; (ii)
\sup_{i,t}\mathbb{E}[\left|\varphi_{j}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right|^{6+\delta}]<K
for some
\delta>0
. Then under Assumption
1
, we have

(GM)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{3,g,m}=O_{p}\left(\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left((n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}+(GM)^{1/2}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\right)\right)

where
\hat{\varphi}_{j,g,m}\equiv(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{j}\left(z_{i,t}\right)
for
j=1,2,3
.

Proof of Lemma
S52
. To prove the lemma, it is
sufficient to show that

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left|(n_{g}T_{m})\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{3,g,m}\right]\right|\leq K\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}

(S598)

and

\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{3,g,m}\right)\leq K(GM)\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}.

(S599)

Because by the triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\left|(GM)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{3,g,m}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|(GM)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left(\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{3,g,m}-\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{3,g,m}\right]\right)\right|

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+(GM)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\left|(n_{g}T_{m})\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{3,g,m}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left((n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}+(GM)^{1/2}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\right)\right),

(S600)

where equality follows by (
S598
), (
S599
) and Markov’s
inequality. The assertion of the lemma follows directly from (
S600
).

We first show (
S598
). By the independence assumption in Assumption
1
and condition (i) in the lemma, we have

\mathbb{E}\left[(n_{g}T_{m})^{2}\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{3,g,m}\right]=(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{t_{1},t_{2},t_{3}\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}\left(z_{i,t_{1}}\right)\varphi_{2}\left(z_{i,t_{2}}\right)\varphi_{2}\left(z_{i,t_{3}}\right)\right],

(S601)

where
\sum_{t_{1},t_{2},t_{3}\in I_{m}}
denotes the triple summation
\sum_{t_{1}\in I_{m}}\sum_{t_{2}\in I_{m}}\sum_{t_{3}\in I_{m}}
. Next, we
write

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{1},t_{2},t_{3}\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}\left(z_{i,t_{1}}\right)\varphi_{2}\left(z_{i,t_{2}}\right)\varphi_{2}\left(z_{i,t_{3}}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\varphi_{2}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\varphi_{3}\left(z_{i,t}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{(a,b,c)\in S_{1,m}}\sum_{t_{1}<t_{2}<t_{3}}\varphi_{a}\left(z_{i,t_{1}}\right)\varphi_{b}\left(z_{i,t_{2}}\right)\varphi_{c}\left(z_{i,t_{3}}\right)

\displaystyle+2^{-1}\sum_{(a,b,c)\in S_{1,m}}\sum_{t_{1}\neq t_{2}}\varphi_{a}\left(z_{i,t_{1}}\right)\varphi_{b}\left(z_{i,t_{1}}\right)\varphi_{c}\left(z_{i,t_{2}}\right),

(S602)

where
S_{1,m}\equiv\left\{(a,b,c):a,b,c\in\{1,2,3\}\text{ and }a\neq b\neq c\right\}
,
\sum_{t_{1}<t_{2}<t_{3}}
denotes the summation over
t_{1},t_{2},t_{3}\in I_{m}
with
t_{1}<t_{2}<t_{3}
, and similar definition
applies to
\sum_{t_{1}\neq t_{2}}
. By condition (ii) of the lemma, the
triangle inequality and Hölder’s inequality

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\varphi_{2}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\varphi_{3}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right]\right|\leq\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{1}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\varphi_{2}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\varphi_{3}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right]\right|\leq KT_{m}.

(S603)

Consider any
(a,b,c)\in S_{1,m}
, we next study the moment of
\varphi_{a}\left(z_{i,t_{1}}\right)\varphi_{b}\left(z_{i,t_{2}}\right)\varphi_{c}\left(z_{i,t_{3}}\right)
for any
t_{1}<t_{2}<t_{3}
. For this
purpose, let
l\equiv\max\{t_{2}-t_{1},t_{3}-t_{2}\}
. By Corollary A.2.
in
26
and Assumption
1
(iv), we have

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{a}\left(z_{i,t_{1}}\right)\varphi_{b}\left(z_{i,t_{2}}\right)\varphi_{c}\left(z_{i,t_{3}}\right)\right]\right|\leq Ka^{l\delta/(4+\delta)}\left\|\varphi_{a}\left(z_{i,t_{1}}\right)\right\|_{2+\delta/2}\left\|\varphi_{b}\left(z_{i,t_{2}}\right)\varphi_{c}\left(z_{i,t_{3}}\right)\right\|_{2+\delta/2}\leq Ka^{l\delta/(4+\delta)}

when
l=t_{2}-t_{1}
. The same upper bound
Ka^{l\delta/(4+\delta)}

applies to
\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{a}\left(z_{i,t_{1}}\right)\varphi_{b}\left(z_{i,t_{2}}\right)\varphi_{c}\left(z_{i,t_{3}}\right)\right]\right|
when
l=t_{3}-t_{2}
. Therefore for
any
t_{1}<t_{2}<t_{3}
with
l\equiv\max\{t_{2}-t_{1},t_{3}-t_{2}\}
, we
have

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{a}\left(z_{i,t_{1}}\right)\varphi_{b}\left(z_{i,t_{2}}\right)\varphi_{c}\left(z_{i,t_{3}}\right)\right]\right|\leq Ka^{l\delta/(4+\delta)}.

(S604)

Since
t_{1}<t_{2}<t_{3}
, it is clear that
1\leq l\leq T_{m}-1
. Let

S_{2,m,l}\equiv\left\{(t_{1},t_{2},t_{3}):\text{ }t_{1},t_{2},t_{3}\in I_{m}\text{, }t_{1}<t_{2}<t_{3}\text{ and }l=\max\{t_{2}-t_{1},t_{3}-t_{2}\}\right\}.

Then
\#S_{2,m,l}\leq 2l(T_{m}-l)
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{t_{1}<t_{2}<t_{3}}\varphi_{a}\left(z_{i,t_{1}}\right)\varphi_{b}\left(z_{i,t_{2}}\right)\varphi_{c}\left(z_{i,t_{3}}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t_{1}<t_{2}<t_{3}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\varphi_{a}\left(z_{i,t_{1}}\right)\varphi_{b}\left(z_{i,t_{2}}\right)\varphi_{c}\left(z_{i,t_{3}}\right)\right|\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{T_{m}-1}\sum_{(t_{1},t_{2},t_{3})\in S_{2,m,l}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\varphi_{a}\left(z_{i,t_{1}}\right)\varphi_{b}\left(z_{i,t_{2}}\right)\varphi_{c}\left(z_{i,t_{3}}\right)\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq K\sum_{l=1}^{T_{m}-1}l(T_{m}-l)a^{l\delta/(4+\delta)}\leq KT_{m}

(S605)

where the second inequality is by (
S604
), and the last inequality
is by
a\in(0,1)
. By the same arguments for deriving (
S604
),

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\varphi_{a}\left(z_{i,t_{1}}\right)\varphi_{b}\left(z_{i,t_{1}}\right)\varphi_{c}\left(z_{i,t_{2}}\right)\right]\right|\leq Ka^{|t_{1}-t_{2}|\delta/(4+\delta)}

which implies that

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{t_{1}\neq t_{2}}\varphi_{a}\left(z_{i,t_{1}}\right)\varphi_{b}\left(z_{i,t_{1}}\right)\varphi_{c}\left(z_{i,t_{2}}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t_{1}\neq t_{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\varphi_{a}\left(z_{i,t_{1}}\right)\varphi_{b}\left(z_{i,t_{1}}\right)\varphi_{c}\left(z_{i,t_{2}}\right)\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq K\sum_{t_{1}\neq t_{2}}a^{|t_{1}-t_{2}|\delta/(4+\delta)}

\displaystyle\leq K\sum_{l=1}^{T_{m}-1}(T_{m}-l)a^{l\delta/(4+\delta)}\leq KT_{m}.

(S606)

Since the size of
\#S_{1,m}=6
, combining the results in (
S602
), (
S603
), (
S605
) and (
S606
) we get

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[T_{m}^{-1}\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{1}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right)\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{2}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right)\left(\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\varphi_{3}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right)\right]\right|\leq K,

which together with (
S601
) and the triangle inequality shows that

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left|(n_{g}T_{m})^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{3,g,m}\right]\right|\leq K.

(S607)

The desired result in (
S598
) follows by (
S607
).

We next prove (
S599
). By the same arguments for showing (
S585
),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left(M^{-1/2}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{3,g,m}\right)

\displaystyle\leq K\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{3,g,m}\right\|_{2+\delta^{\prime}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq K\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|(n_{g}T_{m})^{3/2}\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{3,g,m}\right\|_{2+\delta^{\prime}}^{2}

(S608)

where
\delta^{\prime}=\delta/3
. Using similar arguments for deriving (
S570
), we can show that

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}\hat{\varphi}_{j,g,m}\right|^{6+\delta}\right]\leq K

which together with (
S608
) and Hölder’s inequality implies that

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\mathrm{Var}\left(M^{-1/2}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{3,g,m}\right)\leq K\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}.

(S609)

By Assumption
1
(iii) and (
S609
)

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{3,g,m}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\varphi}_{1,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{2,g,m}\hat{\varphi}_{3,g,m}\right)

\displaystyle\leq K(GM)\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1},

which shows (
S599
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S53
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
8
,
9
and
10
, we have

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ \ \ \ }-(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\mathbb{E}\left[(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right]=O_{p}((GM)^{1/2}(nT)^{-1}),

(S610)

where
\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\equiv(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\psi_{\gamma}(z_{i,t})
for any
g\in\mathcal{G}

and
m\in\mathcal{M}
.

The proof of (
S610
) of Lemma
S53
is omitted since it directly
follows from (
S591
) of Lemma
S51
.

Lemma S54
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
8
,
9
and
10
, we have

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ \ }+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\mathbb{E}\left[(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right]

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left((1+(GM)^{1/2}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2})(GM)^{1/2}(nT)^{-1}\right).

(S611)

Proof of Lemma
S54
. Since

\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}=-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}-(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1},

we can derive the following equations:

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ \ }-\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}

(S612)

where

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }-\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\mathbb{E}\left[(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right]\overset{}{=}O_{p}((GM)^{1/2})

(S613)

by Lemma
S51
. Next, we will examine the second term on the right-hand
side (RHS) of equation (
S612
). By the triangle inequality and the
Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\psi_{\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right\|

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\psi_{\gamma}(z_{i,t})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(\lambda_{\max}(\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\theta,g,m})\lambda_{\max}((\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})^{2}))^{1/2}\lambda_{\max}((\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})^{2})

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ \ }\times\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}\right\|^{2}\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|,

(S614)

where
\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(\lambda_{\max}(\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\theta,g,m}))^{1/2}\lambda_{\max}((\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})^{2})\leq K
because Assumption
9
,
(
S632
) of Lemma
S60
, and
\lambda_{\max}((\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})^{2})=O_{p}(1)
implied by Lemma
S60
(ii).

Equations (
S573
) and (
S574
) in Lemma
S48

together with Markov’s inequality and Hölder’s inequality imply that

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}\right\|^{2}\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|=O_{p}((GM)\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}).

(S615)

Collecting the results in (
S614
) and (
S615
) obtains

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}

\displaystyle=O_{p}((GM)\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}).

(S616)

The assertion of the lemma now follows from (
S612
), (
S613
) and (
S616
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S55
.

Under Assumptions

,

,

and

, we have

(S617)

and

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\mathbb{E}\left[(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right]=O(GM).

) and (

) in the proof of

.

Lemma S56
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
8
,
9
and
10
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(n_{g}T_{m})\mathbb{E}\left[(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}))\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right]

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ \ }+O_{p}\left((1+(GM)^{1/2}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2})(GM)^{1/2}\right)\overset{}{=}O_{p}\left(GM\right).

Proof of Lemma
S56
. By the triangle inequality,
the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, and (
S632
) of Lemma
S60
, we
have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m})(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m})\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq K\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(\lambda_{\max}((\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})^{2}))^{1/2}

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ \ }\times\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\right\|\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}\right\|\left\|(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|.

(S619)

By Assumption
8
(ii), and (
S565
) in Lemma
S47
, we have

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m})\right\|^{4}\right]\leq K

(S620)

which together with Lemma
S48
, Markov’s inequality and Hölder’s inequality implies that

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\right\|\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}\right\|\left\|(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|

(S621)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}((GM)\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}).

Therefore by Lemma
S60
(ii), (
S619
) and (
S621
)

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m})(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}

(S622)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}((GM)\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}).

The claim of the lemma follows from Lemma
S53
, Lemma
S54
, Lemma
S55
and (
S622
).
Q.E.D.

F.2
Intermediate Results

Lemma S57
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
8
and
10
, we
have

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\sup_{\phi\in\Phi}\left|(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\left(D^{\nu}\psi\left(z_{i,t},\phi\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[D^{\nu}\psi\left(z_{i,t},\phi\right)\right]\right)\right|=O_{p}((GM)^{1/p}),

(S623)

and

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\sup_{\phi\in\Phi}\left|\frac{(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\left(D^{\nu}\psi\left(z_{i,t},\phi\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[D^{\nu}\psi\left(z_{i,t},\phi^{\ast}\right)\right]\right)}{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}+\left\|\phi-\phi^{\ast}\right\|}\right|=O_{p}(1)

(S624)

for any
\nu\equiv\left(\nu_{1},...,\nu_{k}\right)^{\top}
with
\left|\nu\right|=\sum_{j=1}^{k}\nu_{j}\leq 3
.

The proofs of (
S623
) and (
S624
) of Lemma
S57

are omitted since they directly follow from Lemma
S49
.

Lemma S58
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
7
,
8
and
10
(i),
||\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}||+\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}\right\|=o_{p}(1)
.

Proof of Lemma
S58
. Let
\psi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\theta,\gamma\right)\equiv\psi\left(z_{i,t};\theta,\gamma\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[\psi\left(z_{i,t};\theta,\gamma\right)\right]
. Without loss of generality, let
\Phi=\Phi_{\theta}\times\Phi_{\gamma}
where
\Phi_{\theta}
and
\Phi_{\gamma}
include
\theta^{\ast}
and
\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}
, respectively. By the triangle
inequality,

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta,\{\gamma_{g,m}\}_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}}\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\psi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\theta,\gamma_{g,m}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}\sup_{\phi\in\Phi}\left|(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\psi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\sup_{\phi\in\Phi}\left|(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\psi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2},

(S625)

where
\sup_{\theta,\{\gamma_{g,m}\}_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}}

is taken over
\theta\in\Phi_{\theta}
and
\gamma_{g,m}\in\Phi_{\gamma}
for
g\in\mathcal{G}
and
m\in\mathcal{M}
. Under Assumptions
1
and
8
, we can apply (
S623
) of Lemma
S57
to obtain

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\sup_{\phi\in\Phi}\left|(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\psi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\phi\right)\right|=O_{p}((GM)^{1/p}).

(S626)

Since
\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}n_{g}T_{m}=nT
, we have

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}\leq\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2},

which together with (
S625
) and (
S626
), and Assumption
10
(i) implies that

\sup_{\theta,\{\gamma_{g,m}\}_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}}\left|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\psi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\theta,\gamma_{g,m}\right)\right|=o_{p}(1).

(S627)

Recall that
\Psi_{g,m}(\theta,\gamma)\equiv(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\mathbb{E}\left[\psi\left(z_{i,t};\theta,\gamma\right)\right]
. For any
\eta>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(||\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}||\geq\eta\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\psi\left(z_{i,t};\theta^{\ast},\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}\right)\leq\sup_{\left\|\theta-\theta^{\ast}\right\|\geq\eta,\{\gamma_{g,m}\}_{g,m}}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\psi\left(z_{i,t};\theta,\gamma_{g,m}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(o_{p}(1)\leq\sup_{\left\|\theta-\theta^{\ast}\right\|\geq\eta,\{\gamma_{g,m}\}_{g,m}}(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left[\Psi_{g,m}(\theta,\gamma_{g,m})-\Psi_{g,m}(\theta^{\ast},\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(o_{p}(1)\leq(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left[\sup_{\{\phi\in\Phi:\text{ }||\phi-\phi^{\ast}||>\eta\}}\Psi_{g,m}(\phi)-\Psi_{g,m}(\phi^{\ast})\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(o_{p}(1)\leq\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left[\sup_{\{\phi\in\Phi:\text{ }||\phi-\phi^{\ast}||>\eta\}}\Psi_{g,m}(\phi)-\Psi_{g,m}(\phi^{\ast})\right]\right)\rightarrow 0,\ \ \text{as }n,T\rightarrow\infty,

where
\sup_{\left\|\theta-\theta^{\ast}\right\|\geq\eta,\{\gamma_{g,m}\}_{g,m}}
is taken over
\theta\in\Phi_{\theta}
with
\left\|\theta-\theta^{\ast}\right\|\geq\eta
and over
\gamma_{g,m}\in\Phi_{\gamma}
for any
g\in\mathcal{G}\
and any
m\in\mathcal{M}
, the second inequality is by (
S627
), and the
convergence is by Assumption
7
. This shows the consistency of
\hat{\theta}
.

To show the consistency of
\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}
, we first note that by the
definition of
\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}

\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}=\arg\max_{\gamma_{g,m}\in\Phi_{\gamma}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\psi(z_{i,t};\hat{\theta},\gamma_{g,m})

for any
g\in\mathcal{G}\
and any
m\in\mathcal{M}
. Therefore for any
\eta>0
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}\right\|\geq\eta\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\sup_{\{\gamma_{g,m}\in\Phi_{\gamma}:\text{ }\left\|\gamma_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}\right\|\geq\eta\}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\left[\psi(z_{i,t};\hat{\theta},\gamma_{g,m})-\psi(z_{i,t};\hat{\theta},\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})\right]\geq 0\right)

(S628)

By Assumption
8
(i) and the triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\sup_{\gamma_{g,m}\in\Phi_{\gamma}}\left|(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\left[\psi(z_{i,t};\hat{\theta},\gamma_{g,m})-\psi(z_{i,t};\theta^{\ast},\gamma_{g,m})\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq||\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}||\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}M\left(z_{i,t}\right)

\displaystyle\leq||\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}||\left(\sup_{i,t}\mathbb{E}\left[M(z_{i,t})\right]+\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left|(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}M^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right|\right).

(S629)

where
M^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\equiv M\left(z_{i,t}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[M(z_{i,t})\right]
. Applying (
S623
) of Lemma
S57

yields

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left|(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}M^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t}\right)\right|=O_{p}\left((GM)^{1/p}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\right)=o_{p}(1)

(S630)

where the second equality is by Assumption
10
(i). Since
\sup_{i,t}\mathbb{E}\left[M(z_{i,t})\right]\leq K
by Assumption
8
(ii), we
can deduce from (
S629
), (
S630
) and the consistency of
\hat{\theta}
that

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\sup_{\gamma_{g,m}\in\Phi_{\gamma}}\left|(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\left[\psi(z_{i,t};\hat{\theta},\gamma_{g,m})-\psi(z_{i,t};\theta^{\ast},\gamma_{g,m})\right]\right|=o_{p}(1).

(S631)

Invoking (
S623
) of Lemma
S57
and Assumption
10
(i) obtains

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\sup_{\gamma_{g,m}\in\Phi_{\gamma}}\left|(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\psi^{\ast}\left(z_{i,t};\theta^{\ast},\gamma_{g,m}\right)\right|=o_{p}(1)

which together with (
S628
), (
S631
) and Assumption
7
implies that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}\right\|\geq\eta\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\sup_{\{\gamma_{g,m}\in\Phi_{\gamma}:\text{ }\left\|\gamma_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}\right\|\geq\eta\}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\left[\psi(z_{i,t};\theta^{\ast},\gamma_{g,m})-\psi(z_{i,t};\theta^{\ast},\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})\right]\geq o_{p}(1)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\sup_{\{\gamma_{g,m}\in\Phi_{\gamma}:\text{ }\left\|\gamma_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}\right\|\geq\eta\}}\left[\Psi_{g,m}(\theta^{\ast},\gamma)-\Psi_{g,m}(\theta^{\ast},\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})\right]\geq o_{p}(1)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\min_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left[\Psi_{g,m}(\phi^{\ast})-\sup_{\{\phi\in\Phi:\text{ }||\phi-\phi^{\ast}||>\eta\}}\Psi_{g,m}(\phi)\right]\leq o_{p}(1)\right)\rightarrow 0\text{, as }n,T\rightarrow\infty,

which shows
\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}\right\|=o_{p}(1)
.
Q.E.D.

Lemma S59
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
8
,
9
and
10
, we have

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\theta,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\theta,g,m}\right\|+\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\right\|+\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}\right\|}{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}}=O_{p}\left(1\right).

Proof of Lemma
S59
. Follows from (
S623
)
in Lemma
S57
.
Q.E.D.

Lemma S60
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
8
,
9
and
10
, we have

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left(\left\|\Psi_{\theta\theta,g,m}\right\|+\left\|\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\right\|+\left\|\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}\right\|\right)\leq K.

(S632)

We also have with probability approaching 1 (wpa1),

(i)
\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left(||\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\theta,g,m}||+||\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}||+||\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}||\right)\leq K
;

(ii)
\min_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\lambda_{\min}(-\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})>K^{-1}
;

(iii)
\min_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\lambda_{\min}(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\theta,g,m}-\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\theta,g,m})>K^{-1}
.

Proof of Lemma
S60
. (i) Equation (
S632
)
follows from Assumption
8
(iii). Lemma
S59
and Assumption
10
(i) imply that

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\theta,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\theta,g,m}\right\|+\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\right\|+\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}\right\|=o_{p}(1).

(S633)

The assertion in part (i) of the lemma now follows from (
S633
) and
(
S632
).

(ii) Since
\min_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\lambda_{\min}(-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})>K^{-1}
by Assumption
9
, the assertion in this
part of the lemma follows from (
S633
).

(iii) By the assertions in parts (i) and (ii) of the lemma, (
S633
) and (
S632
), we can conclude that

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\theta,g,m}-\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\theta,g,m})-(\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\Psi_{\gamma\theta,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\theta,g,m})\right\|=o_{p}(1).

This, combined with Assumption
9
, shows the assertion in part (iii)
of the lemma.
Q.E.D.

Lemma S61
.

Let
\psi_{\theta}(z_{i,t};\phi)\equiv\frac{\partial\psi(z_{i,t};\phi)}{\partial\theta}
and
\psi_{\gamma}(z_{i,t};\phi)\equiv\frac{\partial\psi(z_{i,t};\phi)}{\partial\gamma}
. The M-estimator
(\theta^{\top},\gamma_{\mathcal{G},1}^{\top},\ldots,\gamma_{\mathcal{G},M}^{\top})^{\top}
satisfies the
first-order condition

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\psi_{\theta}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{g,m})=0_{d_{\theta}\times 1},

(S634)

and hence,

0_{d_{\gamma}\times 1}=\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}+\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\phi,g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})+2^{-1}\left((\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\tilde{\Psi}_{\gamma_{j}\phi\phi,g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})\right)_{j\leq d_{\gamma}},

(S635)

where
\tilde{\Psi}_{\gamma_{j}\phi\phi,g,m}\equiv(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial\phi\partial\phi^{\top}}\left(\frac{\partial\psi(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{g,m})}{\partial\gamma_{j}}\right)
for
j=1,\ldots,d_{\gamma}
and
\tilde{\phi}_{g,m}
lies between
\hat{\phi}_{g,m}
and
\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}
. Under Assumptions
1
,
7
,
8
,
9
and
10
, we also have

\max_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\frac{\tilde{\Psi}_{\gamma_{j}\phi\phi,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma_{j}\phi\phi,g,m}}{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}+||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||}\right\|=O_{p}(1).

(S636)

Proof of Lemma
S62
. The M-estimator also
satisfies

\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\psi_{\gamma}(z_{i,t};\hat{\phi}_{g,m})=0_{d_{\gamma}\times 1}

(S637)

for any
g\in\mathcal{G}
and
\ m\in\mathcal{M}
. Applying the Taylor
expansion to (
S637
), we obtain (
S635
). By (
S624
) in Lemma
S57
, we obtain (
S636
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S62
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
7
,
8
,
9
and
10
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}+\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\theta,g,m}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})+\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})\right\|}{(nT)^{-1}+||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}}=O_{p}(1),

(S638)

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}=O_{p}\left((GM)(nT)^{-1}+||\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}||^{2}\right),

(S639)

and

\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2}).

Proof of Lemma
S62
. By (
S636
) of Lemma
S61
, Assumptions
8
(iii) and
10
(i), and Lemma
S58
,

\max_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\tilde{\Psi}_{\gamma_{j}\phi\phi,g,m}\right\|=O_{p}(1)

(S640)

which together with (
S635
) of Lemma
S61
and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality implies that (
S638
) holds.

Applying the Taylor expansion to (
S634
) obtains

\displaystyle 0_{d_{\theta}\times 1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left[\hat{\Psi}_{\theta,g,m}+\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\theta,g,m}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})+\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})\right]

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+2^{-1}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\tilde{\Psi}_{\theta_{j}\phi\phi,g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})\right)_{j=1,\ldots,d_{\theta}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left[\hat{\Psi}_{\theta,g,m}+\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\theta,g,m}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})+\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})\right]

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+O_{p}\left(\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}\right),

(S641)

where
\tilde{\psi}_{\theta_{j}\phi\phi}(z_{i,t})\equiv\psi_{\theta_{j}\phi\phi}(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{g,m})
,
\tilde{\psi}_{\theta_{j}\phi\phi}(z_{i,t};\phi)\equiv\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial\phi\partial\phi^{\top}}\left(\frac{\partial\psi(z_{i,t};\phi)}{\partial\theta_{j}}\right)
for
j\leq d_{\theta}
and
\tilde{\phi}_{g,m}
lies between
\hat{\phi}_{g,m}
and
\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}
, the second equality is by the
Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and

\max_{j\leq d_{\theta}}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\tilde{\Psi}_{\theta_{j}\phi\phi,g,m}\right\|=O_{p}(1),

(S642)

which can be established using the same arguments for showing (
S640
).

By (
S638
) of Lemma
S62
and Lemma
S60
(i,
ii),

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})

\displaystyle=-\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}+\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\theta,g,m}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}))

\displaystyle\text{ \ }+O_{p}\left(1+\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}\right).

(S643)

Applying the above representation in (
S641
), we obtain

\displaystyle 0_{d_{\theta}\times 1}

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta,g,m}-\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m})

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ \ \ }+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\theta,g,m}-\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\Psi_{\gamma\theta,g,m})(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ \ \ }+O_{p}\left((nT)^{-1}+||\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}||^{2}+(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}\right),

(S644)

where by Lemma
S56
, Assumptions
1
and
8
(iii),

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta,g,m}-\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m})=O_{p}\left((nT)^{-1/2}+(GM)(nT)^{-1}\right).

(S645)

By (
S638
) of Lemma
S62
, Lemma
S60
(ii)
and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}

\displaystyle\leq K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+K||\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}||^{2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\theta,g,m}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+O_{p}(1)\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left((nT)^{-2}+||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{4}\right).

(S646)

Note that

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|^{2}\leq\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\lambda_{\max}((\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})^{2})\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|^{2}.

(S647)

By (
S574
) in Lemma
S48
,

\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq K(GM)

which together with Markov’s inequality implies that

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}(GM).

(S648)

Hence by Lemma
S60
(ii), (
S647
) and (
S648
),

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}(GM).

(S649)

By Lemma
S60
(i, ii),

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\theta,g,m}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}(nT).

(S650)

Combining the results in Lemma
S58
, (
S646
), (
S649
) and (
S650
), we get (
S639
).

Lemma
S60
(iii) implies that wpa1,

\lambda_{\min}\left(-(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\theta,g,m}-\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\Psi_{\gamma\theta,g,m})\right)>K^{-1},

(S651)

which together with (
S644
), (
S645
) and (

) shows that

||\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}||=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2}+(GM)(nT)^{-1}).

(S652)

The claim of the lemma follows from (

) and Assumption

(ii).

Lemma S64
.

Under Assumptions

,

,

,

Proof of Lemma
S64
. By the definition of
\Psi_{\gamma_{j}\phi\phi,g,m}
and the triangle inequality, we obtain:

\displaystyle\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left|\frac{(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma_{j}\phi\phi,g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})-(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma_{j}\gamma\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})}{(nT)^{-1}+(nT)^{-1/2}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left|(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma_{j}\theta\theta,g,m}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})\right|+2\left|(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma_{j}\theta\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})\right|}{(nT)^{-1}+(nT)^{-1/2}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||}=O_{p}(1)

(S667)

(iii), the Cauchy-Schwarz
inequality and Lemma
S62
. By (
S635
) and (
S636
) of Lemma

, we have

(S668)

Applying the triangle inequality, Assumption
10
(i), Lemma
S1
obtains

\displaystyle\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}||(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})||

\displaystyle\leq\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|

\displaystyle+\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}+\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m})\right\|

By the maximum inequality under

-norm, Markov’s inequality,

) in Lemma

(S670)

which together with (

) implies that

(S671)

and (

),

\displaystyle\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}||(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})||

\displaystyle+\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}||(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})||

\displaystyle=O_{p}((GM)^{1/p})

which implies that

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{(nT)^{-1}+(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}+||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{3}}{(nT)^{-1}+(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}+(nT)^{-1/2}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||}=O_{p}(1).

(S672)

Collecting the results in (
S667
), (
S668
)
and (

), we deduce that

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}+\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\phi,g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})+\frac{\left((\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma_{j}\gamma\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})\right)_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}}{2}\right\|}{(nT)^{-1}+(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}+(nT)^{-1/2}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||}=O_{p}(1).

(S673)

Next note that by Lemma

(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}=O_{p}\left((GM)(nT)^{-1/2}\right)

which together with Assumption
10
and (

) implies
that

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left((nT)^{-1}+(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}+(nT)^{-1/2}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||\right)

\displaystyle\leq(nT)^{-1/2}+\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||

\displaystyle+(GM)^{1/p}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left((nT)^{-1/2}+(1+(GM)(nT)^{-1/2})(GM)^{1/p}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\right)=o_{p}(1).

(S674)

Therefore, by (
S673
) and (
S674
), we have

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})

\displaystyle=-(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }-(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\Psi_{\gamma\theta,g,m}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }-\frac{(nT)^{-1/2}}{2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}((\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma_{j}\gamma\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}))_{j}+o_{p}(1).

(S675)

Since

\displaystyle\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}

\displaystyle=(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}+\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m})(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}),

and

\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}=-\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\left(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}\right)\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1},

by the triangle inequality and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\left\|(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq K\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(\lambda_{\max}((\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})^{2}))^{1/2}(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\right\|\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}\right\|

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+K(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\right\|

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+K\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(\lambda_{\max}((\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})^{2})^{1/2}(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}\right\|.

(S676)

By (
S565
) in Lemma
S47
,

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m})\right\|^{2}\right]\leq K\text{ and }\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m})\right\|^{2}\right]\leq K,

(S677)

which together with Markov’s inequality, Assumption
10
(i), Lemma
S60
(ii) and (
S676
) implies that

(nT)^{-1}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})=o_{p}\left(1\right).

(S678)

Therefore by Lemma
S62
and (
S678
), we have

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\Psi_{\gamma\theta,g,m}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\Psi_{\gamma\theta,g,m}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})+o_{p}(1).

(S679)

By Lemma
S56
,

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}(n_{g}T_{m})\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{U}_{\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right]+o_{p}\left(1\right).

(S680)

By Lemma
S60
(i, ii), Lemma
S62
, Lemma
S59
and
(
S639
) of Lemma
S62
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1})((\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma_{j}\gamma\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}))_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq K\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left(\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\right\|\max_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma_{j}\gamma\gamma,g,m}\right\|\right)

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }\times\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}((GM)^{1+2/p}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1})=o_{p}((nT)^{1/2}).

(S681)

Similarly, we can show that

(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}((\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma_{j}\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{\ast}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}))_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}=o_{p}(1)

(S682)

where
\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma_{j}\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{\ast}\equiv\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma_{j}\gamma\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma_{j}\gamma\gamma,g,m}
. By
the similar arguments for deriving (
S666
), we can show
that

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}((\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma_{j}\gamma\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}))_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}(\mathbb{E}[\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\Psi_{\gamma_{j}\gamma\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}])_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}+o_{p}\left(1\right).

(S683)

The assertion of the lemma follows from (
S675
), (
S679
) and (
S680
)-(
S683
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S65
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
7
,
8
,
9
and
10
, we have

(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}||(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})||^{s}=O_{p}\left((GM)(nT)^{-1/2}\right)

for any
s\leq p
.

Proof of Lemma
S65
. By the triangle inequality
and the
c_{r}
-inequality,

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}||(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})||^{s}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}||(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}+\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m})||^{s}

(S684)

\displaystyle+K\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}||(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}||^{s}.

By Lemma
S1
,

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}||(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}+\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m})||^{s}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left\|\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}+\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|^{s}}{(GM)^{2s/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-s}}(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(GM)^{2s/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-s/2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left((GM)^{1+2s/p}(nT)^{-1/2}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-s/2}\right)=o_{p}\left((GM)(nT)^{-1/2}\right)

(S685)

where the second equality is by Assumption
10
(ii). By Assumption
9
and (
S565
) in Lemma
S47
,

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\mathbb{E}\left[||(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}||^{s}\right]\leq K\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\mathbb{E}\left[||(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}||^{s}\right]\leq K

(S686)

which together with Markov’s inequality implies that

(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}||(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}||^{s}=O_{p}((GM)(nT)^{-1/2}).

(S687)

The assertion of the lemma now follows from (
S685
) and (
S687
).
Q.E.D.

Lemma S66
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
7
,
8
,
9
and
10
, we have

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left[\hat{\Psi}_{g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m})-\hat{\Psi}_{g,m}(\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})\right]

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\phi,g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+\frac{(nT)^{-1/2}}{2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\hat{\Psi}_{\phi\phi,g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+O_{p}\left((GM)^{1/2}(nT)^{-1/2}\left((GM)^{1/2+2/p}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}+\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\right)\right).

Proof of Lemma
S66
. Applying the Taylor
expansion obtains

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left[\hat{\Psi}_{g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m})-\hat{\Psi}_{g,m}(\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})\right]

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\phi,g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+\frac{(nT)^{-1/2}}{2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\hat{\Psi}_{\phi\phi,g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+\frac{(nT)^{-1/2}}{6}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})((\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\tilde{\Psi}_{\phi\phi\phi_{j},g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}))_{j\leq d_{\phi}}^{\top}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}),

(S688)

where

\tilde{\Psi}_{\phi\phi\phi_{j},g,m}\equiv(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\sum_{i\in I_{g}}\sum_{t\in I_{m}}\frac{\partial}{\partial\phi_{j}}\left(\frac{\partial^{2}\psi(z_{i,t};\tilde{\phi}_{g,m})}{\partial\phi\partial\phi^{\top}}\right)

for any
j\leq k
, and
\tilde{\phi}_{g,m}
is between
\hat{\phi}_{g,m}

and
\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}
. By (
S576
) in Lemma
S49
, we can show
that

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\frac{\tilde{\Psi}_{\phi\phi\phi_{j},g,m}-\Psi_{\phi\phi\phi_{j},g,m}}{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}+||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||}\right\|=O_{p}(1),

(S689)

where
\Psi_{\phi\phi\phi_{j},g,m}\equiv\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial\phi_{j}}\left(\frac{\partial^{2}\psi(z_{i,t};\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})}{\partial\phi\partial\phi^{\top}}\right)\right]
. By
Assumptions
8
(iii),
10
(i) and Lemma
S58

\displaystyle\max_{j\leq k}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\tilde{\Psi}_{\phi\phi\phi_{j},g,m}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{j\leq k}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\Psi_{\phi\phi\phi_{j},g,m}\right\|

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+O_{p}\left(((GM)^{1/p}+||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi^{\ast}||)\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1).

(S690)

By the triangle inequality and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\left|(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})((\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\tilde{\Psi}_{\phi\phi\theta_{j},g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}))_{j\leq d_{\theta}}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\max_{j\leq d_{\theta}}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\tilde{\Psi}_{\phi\phi\theta_{j},g,m}\right\|\right)||\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}||(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}.

(S691)

From Lemma
S62
and Lemma
S66
,

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}

\displaystyle=(nT)^{1/2}||\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}||^{2}+(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}((nT)^{-1/2}+(GM)(nT)^{-1/2})=O_{p}((GM)(nT)^{-1/2}),

which together with (
S691
) and Lemma
S62
implies
that

\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})((\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\tilde{\Psi}_{\phi\phi\theta_{j},g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}))_{j\leq d_{\theta}}^{\top}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})=O_{p}((GM)(nT)^{-1/2}).

(S692)

Similarly, we can show that

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})((\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})^{\top}\tilde{\Psi}_{\theta\theta\gamma_{j},g,m}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}))_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}^{\top}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})

(S693)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}\left((GM)(nT)^{-1}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}\right)

and

\displaystyle\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})((\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})^{\top}\tilde{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma\gamma_{j},g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}))_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}^{\top}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})

(S694)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}((GM)(nT)^{-1/2}).

By the triangle inequality, Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, Lemma
S58
and (
S689
),

\displaystyle\left|(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})((\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}(\tilde{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma_{j},g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma\gamma_{j},g,m})(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}))_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}^{\top}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})\right|

\displaystyle\leq(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{3}\left\|\tilde{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma_{j},g,m}-\Psi_{\gamma\gamma\gamma_{j},g,m}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}(1)(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{3}((GM)^{1/p}+||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||+||\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}||)

\displaystyle=O_{p}((GM)^{1/p})(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{1/2}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{3}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left((GM)^{1+1/p}(nT)^{-1/2}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\right),

(S695)

where the last equality is by Lemma
S65
. By Assumption
8
(iii), Lemma
S1
and Lemma
S48

\displaystyle\left|\begin{array}[]{c}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left((\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma\gamma_{j},g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})\right)_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}^{\top}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})\\
-\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma\gamma_{j},g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m})_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\end{array}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\Psi_{\gamma\gamma\gamma_{j},g,m}\right\|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left\|\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|\left\|\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}+\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }+\max_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\left\|\Psi_{\gamma\gamma\gamma_{j},g,m}\right\|\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left\|\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|^{2}\left\|\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}+\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1)(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}\left((GM)^{4/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\left\|\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|+(GM)^{2/p}\left\|\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left((GM)^{1+2/p}(nT)^{-1/2}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\right),

(S698)

where the last equality is by Assumption
10
(i). Applying Lemma
S52
, we get

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma\gamma_{j},g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m})_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left((GM)^{1/2}(nT)^{-1/2}\left(\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}+(GM)^{1/2}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\right)\right).

(S699)

Collecting the results in (
S692
)-(
S699
), we
deduce that

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\left((\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\tilde{\Psi}_{\phi\phi\phi_{j},g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})\right)_{j\leq k}^{\top}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left((GM)^{1/2}(nT)^{-1/2}\left((GM)^{1/2+2/p}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}+\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}\right)\right)

(S700)

which together with (
S688
) shows the assertion of the
lemma.
Q.E.D.

Lemma S67
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
7
,
8
,
9
and
10
, we have

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})

\displaystyle=(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}+o_{p}\left((GM)^{1/2}(nT)^{-1/2}\right).

Proof of Lemma
S67
. By Lemma
S62

and (
S671
),

\displaystyle\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{(nT)^{-1}+(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}+||\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{3}}{(nT)^{-1}+(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{(nT)^{-1}+(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}+||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{3}}{(nT)^{-1}+(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}}

\displaystyle+\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}||\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}||^{2}+||\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}||^{3}}{(nT)^{-1}+(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 1+\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left(\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||\right)||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}}{(nT)^{-1}+(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}}+o_{p}(1)=O_{p}(1).

Therefore, we can use (
S668
) to obtain

\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}\frac{\left\|\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}+\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\phi,g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})+\frac{\left((\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma_{j}\phi\phi,g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})\right)_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}}{2}\right\|}{(nT)^{-1}+(GM)^{1/p}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}||\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}||^{2}}=O_{p}(1).

(S701)

To employ the above expansion, we write

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})

\displaystyle=-(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}(\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}+\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma\theta,g,m}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast}))

\displaystyle\text{ \ \ }-2^{-1}(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}((\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma_{j}\phi\phi,g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}))_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}+R_{1,n,T}

(S702)

We can apply the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, Lemma

, (

) and (

\displaystyle\left|R_{1,n,T}\right|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left((GM)^{1/p}(nT)^{-1/2}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1/2}+(GM)^{1+1/p}(nT)^{-1/2}\max_{g\in\mathcal{G},m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})^{-1}\right).

(S703)

Next, we bound the second term in the RHS of (
S702
). For this

\displaystyle(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}((\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma_{j}\phi\phi,g,m}(\hat{\phi}_{g,m}-\phi_{g,m}^{\ast}))_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle+2(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}((\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma_{j}\theta\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}))_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle+(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}((\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast})^{\top}\Psi_{\gamma_{j}\gamma\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}))_{j\leq d_{\gamma}}.

(S705)

(S718)

Lemma S68
.

(S722)

Proof of Lemma
S68
. By the triangle inequality
and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality

\displaystyle\left|(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})^{\top}\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\theta,g,m}(\hat{\theta}-\theta^{\ast})\right|

S62
and Lemma
S60
(i).
This shows the assertion in (
S721
). To show (
S722
), we
note that by the triangle inequality

\displaystyle\leq\left\|(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m}\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})(\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}-\Psi_{\theta\gamma,g,m})\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}(\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}+\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq(nT)^{-1/2}\sum_{g\in\mathcal{G}}\sum_{m\in\mathcal{M}}(n_{g}T_{m})||\hat{\Psi}_{\theta\gamma,g,m}||\left\|\hat{\gamma}_{g,m}-\gamma_{g,m}^{\ast}+\Psi_{\gamma\gamma,g,m}^{-1}\hat{\Psi}_{\gamma,g,m}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1+(GM)^{1+2/p}(nT)^{-1/2})

where the second inequality is by (
S565
) in Lemma
S47
and Lemma

, the equality is by Lemma
S60
(i) and Lemma
S1
. The assertion in (

) and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality.
